GURUVANIKAL · May 2001 · Volume 3
Malayalam source paragraphs GV3:P1148 to GV3:P2317
English line for line; each paragraph carries its Malayalam source number.

[GV3:P1148] May 2001

[GV3:P1149] Those with experience will see everything. But there is no point in just seeing like that. After seeing, we must put it into practice and create actions that benefit the household. This will only work if the family members accept it. Otherwise, what can an ascetic do after accepting it? You should think about what you can do after understanding this. Let us accept the truth of what each theoretician has said. We should change what scholars have added to it. To make such a change, the presence of a Guru is needed. Otherwise, it is impossible to change it.

[GV3:P1150] If not spoken with the courage of experience, everything will turn bad. We need to bring about a situation where we know what is right and what is wrong in the actions committed by us and our ancestors. Then, how is this mistake broken? A god, goddess, light, or form appears before us. How can it be broken? I am talking about what is being done here every day. It's not just some trade talk. That act of breaking is done by the small children standing next to me. I just keep watching. Why is that? Can't I do it? If people who can do this do not step up from among you, what will you do after my time? Only a person who has imbibed the light of the nine planets encompassing twelve zodiac signs and twenty-seven stars can break this. They can be taught this only while I am present. You must understand this in this manner. Otherwise, there is no point in just arguing. I can only leave after bringing them to a certain position.

[GV3:P1151] We can only proceed cautiously, targeting the fact that Jinn rule the world. A disciple must be there to catch that Jinn moving in the sky. The Guru cannot catch it. My presence must be there. If I am not present, they will not get what is above. These indwellers will confuse them. They are seeing this, it is true. But I need time to familiarize them. They must also be ready for it.

[GV3:P1152] It is not that this is only for ascetics. Householders can also do it, if they are correct. This is the Rishi principle. After the Rishi principle comes the principle of knowledge. Our principle is not the principle of gods or Jinn. These principles will be understood by Brahmins and Kshatriyas. Vaishyas and Shudras will not understand it. A Shudra is not entitled to Brahma Jnana, after all. But I cannot just say what is to come will come, like a Vedantin, and leave. That is not my duty. My duty is to improve any child, or whoever is at hand—be it a Brahmin or a Shudra. According to Yoga philosophy, a Guru will get only one such disciple. According to Karma philosophy, two or three people will come at different levels. But that is not the path of a true Jnani. He will only leave after making the obedient disciple equal to himself. But if one is not ready to obey, the Guru cannot do anything. It takes some time to break the karma of a person and set them right. Twenty-seven women and twenty-seven men for the twenty-seven stars. If the zodiac constellations are not broken using them and placed in the hands of this disciple, he cannot grasp it. That is why I said time is needed. Don't I always tell you—that I have no time, no time? The Vedantic principle that this atmosphere is empty will not work here. We have to bring about this path shown by God. It will not work if we try to understand this by comparing it with the madmen, Siddhas, and Jnanis we have seen.

[GV3:P1153] Scholars are summoned to the royal assembly, the topics they select are written down, and it is published in the king's name. There will be no one to question it. There has been a lot of such propaganda in our midst as religious doctrines. It is still happening. We never think whether there have been any shortcomings on the religious fronts. Even if we think, we cannot point out those shortcomings. Thus, religious theorists try to expand their respective religions. They separate into castes and try to grow. Then they add Advaita and establish meaning. There is also a practice in India of establishing the greatness of many sadhus. Similarly, there is a practice of speaking of the spiritual and the material as two. There is no meaning in speaking like that. Except that it is said for the sake of realization, there is no real substance in it. Can one live without a body? Is it possible to take and know anything other than the knowledge of life? What else do we have other than many kinds of familiarities supporting one another? Where will we reach if we follow the knowledge part stated by all religious theorists?

[GV3:P1154] Question: Is spirituality rising as a stepping stone to the material world?

[GV3:P1155] Is it so?

[GV3:P1156] Guru: The material is needed. There is no meaning in saying that the material is not needed.

[GV3:P1157] We should only consider that spirituality supports the material and the material supports spirituality. What is the use for us if other isolated things are spoken and made to sound convincing? I don't think there is anything else other than catching each other in a duel. Even though we speak of this as one, we are merely acquiring the very practice of holding a dualistic conflict. We have such a flaw. How can this flaw be rectified? Only this should be investigated.

[GV3:P1158] Question: Should I think that I need to reach a certain level?

[GV3:P1159] Guru: That comes later. In fact, that is not what is needed first.

[GV3:P1160] We investigate starting from the mistake. As long as we do not understand it this way, we cannot correct the mistake. There is no point in blaming the material or blaming spirituality. There is no point in blaming religious culture either. Underlying all this is a real mistake.

[GV3:P1161] We should see it like this. There are mistakes among all religious people. But that mistake did not come from their religious doctrine. There was a time when girls were not allowed to associate with movements. Today, it is mostly seen in Islam. So what have Muslim women become in the present situation? So a flaw of not bringing them up with a foundation has caught up with us. This is the truth.

[GV3:P1162] If we look at any social activity, each caste and each religion is entangled in knots. As householders, can we not only improve the women in our homes? Can we improve those wandering in the country? We should bring them forward and instill humility in them. At the same time, we must also see their flaws. If we lack the experience to fix their flaws, we should have others speak to them and fix it. Otherwise, their flaws will never change. We must give them dignity and make them do it outwardly themselves. Otherwise, they will take on their own responsibilities and act out like possessed. That is the only way to make them do it. They will not do it just because you give a lot of advice.

[GV3:P1163] If you don't guide the children in each of your homes on the path of that culture, they will just learn some knowledge, teach, marry someone in that way, and leave. Then you won't be able to do anything. You will only be able to play the role of a father and mother. You should show happiness not only to your children but to any child. Only upon seeing that happiness should they feel a sense of inner fulfillment.

[GV3:P1164] If you peel the shell of an egg, there is a membrane inside. Children have a membrane like that. Your interaction with children should be to slowly peel that membrane. But if peeling the membrane causes everything to spill down, there is no point in peeling it.

[GV3:P1165] All errors have started from the point where one's own Dharma went wrong. What do people say, not just commoners but those with some knowledge? Isn't it Kaliyuga? This is the word of scholars who do not know what the Dharma of Kali is. Ordinary people just repeat it. Among the four ages described, we haven't heard much about Satyayuga. Satyayuga is the same for everyone. When Treta Yuga and Dwapara Yuga come, systems of worship change according to the changing times. The change happens right from the beginning of Treta Yuga. The Gurus suited for that time appear then. At the time when Treta Yuga is completed, the era of the Devas is also completed. It is the Rishis who flawlessly correct that era of the Devas. We can more or less understand the actions of those Rishis. Kings learn how to turn the wheel of administration by going to the Rishis and studying. That is, the Rishis are the ones who give corrections (guidance) for administrative matters.

[GV3:P1166] Even though it is like this, the root cause of the error exists right from the beginning. We have never understood it ourselves till today, nor has anyone explained it to us. Through Ramayana, Bhagavatam, and the eighteen Puranas, this error has kept repeating. That is, everything has been mixed up and thrown into confusion. In any matter, when the order and method go wrong, a state will come where everything is called a lie and nothing is accepted. It is not that these things do not exist. Rama and Dharmaputra are all real. When the Dharma of the time went wrong, everything got messed up and fell into error. There is a primal error as the root cause for all this.

[GV3:P1167] That is the mistake made by a great soul named Satyatranan in the third Chaturyuga of the Vaivasvata Manvantara. Desiring to proceed with the utmost truth, when he looked at past history with his spiritual vision, all truthfulness merged into Brahman. Thus, the four statements like Ayam Atma Brahma, Tat Tvam Asi were joined with the Pancha Mahavakya and completed. Everything was seen as Brahman. When Karma Yoga, Jnana Yoga, and Bhakti Yoga came into different eras creatively, castes were created using the four statements. When the Pancha Mahavakya was vulgarized by the Shudras, it became the scholarly action of those without true knowledge. To see Advaita, efforts were made for the non-dual one. Things that should have been achieved through action became a reality without action or Dharma. And it succeeded. When that reality was intellectually twisted, it became the act of the elite scholars devoid of true knowledge. When this continued for ages, without knowing where or how it went wrong, ignorance confounded human Dharma.

[GV3:P1168] One might ask, couldn't God fix this? Whether scholar or commoner, this question can be asked. There is a calculation that brings the nine planets, twelve zodiac signs, and twenty-seven stars to the exact planetary moment. That is the planetary position (Grahanila). This is an era where one cannot fathom where the constructed planetary position has gone wrong. It cannot be seen and understood, nor is it known how to familiarize it through the four statements. That calculation is also brought up to the star and weekday. What has happened in this planetary dharma? They did not know where to subtract or add the time multiplied by twelve zodiac signs and twenty-seven stars. Therefore, the Gurus who have come so far have been stuck, not knowing where this action has gone.

[GV3:P1169] All those who follow the correct order will merge in Brahman. This is true. In the practices written and followed like Hatha Yoga, Chandrika Yoga, and Hamsa Yoga, Hatha Yoga gained more prominence. Then the Hatha Yogis tried to bring the life force to Brahman. They had no way of knowing if this was a yoga combination according to God's design. Therefore, everyone gave importance to different things based on their own scholarship and practice. But they did not know that Brahman is not something to be won by argument. Brahman is the embodiment of love.

[GV3:P1170] 2001 Birthday Issue (August)

[GV3:P1171] The State of Yuga Dharma and the Error in Worship

[GV3:P1172] When each great soul comes, they do their part and then leave. But it must be said that no one who completed a subject and left has returned within one or two crore years. It has been twenty-four Chaturyugas since the world's path of Dharma went astray. How can this calculation be known so accurately?

[GV3:P1173] What is being said is this: Great men are born in all parts of the world. They will act according to the nature of their birth time and the state of the world. The impact of their actions will last for some time. The longest period among these is up to two thousand, three thousand, four thousand, or five thousand years. The rest is subject to many changes.

[GV3:P1174] How many Gurus came to India! Does anyone stand out as an individual figure today? Why does it fail to persist like that? The time of Jesus is said to be for two thousand years. That became somewhat true. Now it is slowly changing to another level. Everyone talks about the greatness of the God they caught hold of. But based on which God's greatness should we adopt?

[GV3:P1175] Islam split from Christianity. Its time was said to be for one thousand five hundred years. It remains so. Similarly, Sikhism, Jainism, and Buddhism exist. Hindus lost their way long ago. It is because Hindus strayed into error that all this happened. But by bringing in the tribal repetition of Brahmin, Kshatriya, Vaishya, and Shudra, Hindus stubbornly turn this way and that to call upon God.

[GV3:P1176] How can one extract a truly sincere part from this? The fact is simply that such a part cannot be taken from this. This is no one's fault.

[GV3:P1177] Manusmriti is the authoritative text of human history. But someone's personal wishes have been added to Manusmriti. When speaking of Kaliyuga, we have been made to believe that Kali is bad. Truly, for whom is Kali bad? Kali is bad for people starting from the Brahmins who stood firmly in the worship of gods and goddesses. When their lineage became mixed, tribal purity was lost. In North India today, more women have become Christians and Muslims.

[GV3:P1178] It is believed that Bengalis are pure Brahmins, isn't it? They are the ones who believe more in matters of education and prosperity like Lakshmi and Saraswati. This is our knowledge. This is not the call of God at all. Such errors have occurred for many epochs. Today's Manusmriti is a remnant of this error.

[GV3:P1179] Hindus say "Aham Brahmasmi" (I am Brahman). Moreover, everything is Brahman. It is true that a part of Brahman is in all of us. But what is the need for me to say that I am Brahman? This came from a Vedantic aphorism. Similarly, whatever branches have emerged in that religion, each of them stands on its own greatness. From where will man take God, from which one? I am not saying these religions have no power. Each has its own power. God cannot be called from any of this. According to the movement of Karma in each era, each person established different things. Many people have believed in all this in the past. As far as we are concerned, is there a need to investigate all this in such detail? How long is our life? Within a hundred years. We might ask, why do we need all this within this short time? But it's not like that.

[GV3:P1180] According to human tradition, the right and wrong of the human race must be investigated and extracted while humans are alive. There is no point in thinking about it after that. It has fallen so low that it cannot be figured out while living. This has not happened to just one country or one religion. It happened to all countries and all religions.

[GV3:P1181] Some time ago, scientists said they found the place where God resides. Is it really like that? God's abode cannot be seen like that. Just like our solar system that includes this Earth, there are many solar systems. What was seen there was the cover of one solar system. The solar system is constantly rotating. What is being said is what they saw when it reached a certain cover.

[GV3:P1182] Can we, living within a hundred years, see anything that is fifty thousand light-years away? That was science's calculation. Even if there are many solar systems, there will be only one sphere like our Earth. We cannot even fully know about this Earth itself. Do we need to struggle so much? There is such a blunder in our efforts.

[GV3:P1183] How did the worship of God come about? First in the forest, then in the country, then in the temple. Gradually, it was in many places. With the system of worship evolving like this, it became impossible to invoke God truly.

[GV3:P1184] For mankind, there is God. Let's look at history after Moses. He left after laying a curse. Jesus had to say the words, "Have you forsaken me?" The Prophet asked, "What are you showing before me, who believed there is nothing else but you?" We cannot say there is a mistake in their words. So how do we sort out the bad and the good of what they have seen?

[GV3:P1185] Sri Rama is spoken of as if he was born today or yesterday. The time of Sri Rama is seventeen lakh years old. Why did Sri Rama step into the Sarayu river and sacrifice his life? The reason is not what we think. When they progress according to their situation, what they see is not what they intended. Then whatever they had done till then seems wrong. They abandon what they had established so far and leave. Shankaracharya also did the same. Countless great souls are like this. It is not because none of them sacrificed or because they didn't want the good.

[GV3:P1186] This blunder happened to all these lineages of Gurus, Prophets, and gods and goddesses due to the primal error mentioned earlier. The mistake made by the teacher of the Manu lineage twenty-five Chaturyugas ago. That mistake begins with "Aham Brahmasmi". No Hindu can agree with this truth. If they have a little education, they won't agree at all.

[GV3:P1187] This is how we deteriorated. In place of saying Father, Son, and Holy Spirit, why did Father, Son, and Holy Spirit repeatedly come up? Isn't there this question among us? Hindus brought Varna Dharmas and turned them into castes. If a system of worship has emerged not just in India but in all parts of the world, it is from Hinduism.

[GV3:P1188] How did the rights and wrongs that we and our ancestors committed knowingly or unknowingly occur even during the times when our Gurus lived? They are the ones who guided us. What is the reason for them to speak words of sorrow? We never question this one thing. The action we need to perform is one that enables us to question this. This is the correction.

[GV3:P1189] It can be corrected by living as a human being itself. There is no point in correcting it in any other way. For that, we must believe that everything that has come so far is real. One should not believe that it does not exist. If we believe so, where will we go and find what exists? How can we know what is there? It can only be seen from this Earth, or within the atmospheric sphere of the nine planets (solar system). After that, one can go beyond it.

[GV3:P1190] When these planets rotate, can we extract anything subtly from them? Nothing is possible. So it is better not to go that way. Doing nothing is better than wandering on that path and making mistakes.

[GV3:P1191] I am sharing a knowledge I received. Hindus think that God resides in the Trinity system. Every religion has God in its own layer. But is God only this much? The glory of God should not stop with this much. It is in this context that I am sharing my knowledge. It is knowledge from a vision.

[GV3:P1192] We speak of a Manvantara. The current one is the twenty-eighth Chaturyuga of the seventh Manvantara. The current Yuga is the Kaliyuga, the fourth quarter of this Chaturyuga. It is our fortune to be born in this Kaliyuga. Why? All Shudra classes got the freedom to get an education. From the time of Sri Rama to today, we see all governments as great. But today, man lives greater than all of that. Even though man has such a life, he cannot utilize it due to the ruin of Varnasrama. Because of that, some became Hindus. Some became Christians. Some became Muslims. Some became Buddhists and Jains. Even though people split into various ways like this, the word of God did not come straight. Who can we blame for this?

[GV3:P1193] Among the Brahmins, most of the decent boys have gone somewhere, got married, and are living their lives. They send something to their parents and summarize things in a letter—no convenience to come. It has become like this among all classes. Living and learning like this, which truth will we respect? This situation has not happened to just one person, one caste, or one religion. It is for all castes, religions, and classes. In this corrupted action, how can we seek the truth? There are those among you who handle the government. Even if you desire to bring some good, can you? During our lifetime, we must at least realize that living according to what we see is not the truth.

[GV3:P1194] We have made such a mistake. It hasn't happened to just one caste or one religion, but to all sections. We all live our lives without knowing this. We need a solution for this. Here, the ones who hold onto faith most strongly are the Pentecostals. They say they will catch hold of God now. Who are we, going around trying to catch God like this?

[GV3:P1195] While we are alive, in the Karma that has come to us as good and bad, even if we can only do a sesame seed's worth, we must do it well. It doesn't have to be a lot. We cannot throw away what is in our hands and go lift up the good. You can keep everything you have with you now. While keeping it, you can accept this goodness.

[GV3:P1196] There is a calculation that if one gets a vision in the Trinity system, this will happen. There is a mistake in that calculation too. I took seventeen years to resolve that mistake. When I fixed this mistake, it turned out that none of it is the truth. Now we can say that this is the truth. But if I say it suddenly, you will not understand. It can be said slowly.

[GV3:P1197] We have the Venkatachalapathy pooja, right? Where Venkateswara is worshipped, importance is given to the Goddess. Thus, we are familiar with calling upon God in various ways for achieving ends, for knowledge, wealth, and lordship. Crores of years have passed this way. How can we untie these knots?

[GV3:P1198] The knots are formed; when Karma is referred to as Purusha, it is about the life force (Jiva). The life force is Purusha. It has no distinction of male and female. Only material objects have dualities. Knots must be untied from the Purusha. The knot stands as female and male. It must be untied. God, Goddess, Rishi, Ascetic, Jnani, Prophet, and all such knots must be untied. We have worshipped all these.

[GV3:P1199] How will we untie this knot? We do not know about this. Jesus took hold of everything up to his time. If Jesus caught hold of this, there is no doubt that there was a mistake in the Trinity system or Manu system prevalent then.

[GV3:P1200] If you have worshipped placing a Lakshmi or Saraswati, it can be told here without asking you. Even a demon can say that. It can say there is a god, a goddess, and a particular pooja. There is no use in seeing or saying any of this. Jesus grasped up to what the ancestors had worshipped. What have we believed instead of that? That Jesus washed all our sins with his blood. Is the subject studied and taught by those who worship Lakshmi and Saraswati from the land of Lakshmi and Saraswati? We have placed one thing in our familiarity. The education we learn is Western. 'Anything goes' is our convenience and belief. If we worship for the sake of achieving goals like this, will our obligation to God end?

[GV3:P1201] All those who invoke God in this manner are doing so with the expectation of reward. They are not calling out to God. If one has the belief that God exists for everyone, regardless of the language or attire humans come in, is there a need to invoke God in such diverse ways? For all of us, God is one and the same. Then why is there this fragmented invocation?

[GV3:P1202] This is wrong. We must bring about a change from this. How should we change? What should we base the change on? All this can be seen in the vision. The devil, the god, the goddess, the sage, the ascetic, and however many crores of them have come till today, we can see all of them here through the vision. Similarly, we can see all the lineages of Gurus and prophets. All this is being said to reveal a truth.

[GV3:P1203] Hindu, Christian, Jain, and Buddhist are all basically the same subject. It has many divisions. All this must come into this vision. The truth behind bringing all this is the divine light. What do we say in that light? That God stands formless.

[GV3:P1204] Where is this formless sky? This is what we need to know. We mention a color for the rainbow, don't we? Seven colors. Crossing everything sequentially, finally it is white. That is, the seventh sky is white. We do not know how many skies are there in the white. This is its calculation (science). It is in this sky that there is no form. In the language of Hindus, when one crosses the six steps and reaches there, they say they can see Shiva. That means they can see the soul.

[GV3:P1205] How does the soul exist? That is, what is the karmic course of the soul? Life exists only up to this realm. Only in this one solar system. Beyond that, there is no life. Even beyond this, the sky exists at a distance of crores of years. So, we must cross these skies far beyond this solar system, crores of light years away. Science should not bother to penetrate into any of this. It is enough if they understand this solar system.

[GV3:P1206] The karma that we have worshipped must be severed. Children with vision here have proof of that. The knowledge for that has been imparted to them. And it has been of good use to them. This is the science of Santhigiri. The science of vision that determines the calculation of the formless skies.

[GV3:P1207] 2001 Birthday Edition (August)

[GV3:P1208] Exploration

[GV3:P1209] We have an old habit of worship where we offer some insignificant vow to God to get things done. This is not it; shouldn't we worship God for His mercy? We have a duty towards God. We must realize that and act with a sense of duty. Because we have not done so, we have been committing mistakes in our worship of God for a long time.

[GV3:P1210] Quarrel between husband and wife. Quarreling like this, when they reach old age, the children will also continue it. This is how I have seen it to this day. This lack of culture is absent only in a very few households.

[GV3:P1211] Here, in the ashram, you have never entrusted your children with what has been done for you. Till today, you have not entrusted them. I don't know what you will do tomorrow.

[GV3:P1212] God does not need your offerings. Do we give offerings to get a straight path? If you want to give something, you can. Why? Because such a custom has come into society, we can do it to learn some model from it. That can be done in a temple or an ashram.

[GV3:P1213] That is not the subject I am telling you regarding the worship of God. There is an ignorance that our ancestors have been practicing till today. You are holding on to some karmic flaws by saying some words in the same nature of that ignorance and trying to win with those words.

[GV3:P1214] Wherever they go in the hilly areas, they speak big words and act like people with great culture. But they don't have a trace of culture in them. By showing off their culture, they became like Westerners showing off their culture. What is that culture? It is only an external culture. Not inside. They have decided they don't want culture inside. This has entered every house and inside it.

[GV3:P1215] Since the practice of making offerings has been going on for a long time, it grew in the temple system and grew to a large extent. God has given us an understanding at various times to change the trouble caused by it. It was given through many great souls. But we still haven't understood that knowledge.

[GV3:P1216] Christians are the sect that practice saying the greatest sacrifice. But is there sacrifice among Christians now? Sacrifice means practicing to live truthfully. Are Christians living truthfully now? Christians stand like machines looking to the west. Except that the culture of the house remains in some poor people, it is not there in a single house that has enough to eat, drink, and wear. Why? Hindus themselves have moved there today. They have also adopted the method of offerings like that of Hindus. They are ready to commit any violence in the name of God.

[GV3:P1217] Muslims say they are ruling by the law of God's will. Is God's law that any robbery can be carried out? If this is the situation within the Islamic religion which originated a short while ago, Hindus have a history that has changed so much due to its age. Hindus grabbed the oracle god and became uncultured. When power decreased, they showed stupidity and tried to become great. Similarly, caste and caste after caste. The one whose caste was great and the one whose caste was not great started living like Westerners. Coming like this, they have moved away from the true nature of man. With the departure of human nature, a consciousness has spread that men and women need freedom.

[GV3:P1218] The sense of freedom is good. But what is freedom? They have assumed that freedom is for men and women to argue and justify properly. Freedom is not that. What went wrong for us before? The women in the house should know this. What existed in that shortcoming. How did it happen. Teach them this first. Because of that shortcoming, we have got a different life. When the uncultured Westerner started imparting culture, it became even worse.

[GV3:P1219] Except for showing the model of sacrifice, there is no sacrifice in Christians. Who have come as Christians? Hindus who lived as the most downtrodden. If they get money and ability, they will not stand on the ground. They will only behave without manners. Today, Christians and Muslims have become rich in Kerala. But their culture is different. They speak nicely with beautiful words. They won't act on it. This is a path to danger.

[GV3:P1220] God has given you the most suitable opportunity. But you will not take it. I will only tell you and behave truthfully with you. What do you do? You throw it away. What can I do? What level of freedom have you seen? That is the inferiority complex that arose from living in slavery. This is one kind of freedom. Another is the freedom of living with arrogance. Another is the freedom fought with caste and religion. Is this the freedom of man?

[GV3:P1221] Have you felt this idea to be true? If you have, you are the ones who have to act on it. I have done this so that you can act on it. People have assumed that this is how freedom should be sought. This freedom is not freedom. This is the freedom that drags our children into error.

[GV3:P1222] The most ignorance is in the hands of the educated. In uneducated children, the fault is seen to be less. Wherever wealth, ability, and education have risen. Mayyanad culture, Kottayam culture, if viewed a little more broadly, Ernakulam, Kochi, then Kozhikode is another culture. Like this, how many types of culture are there in Kerala itself. If you are imparting such uncultured knowledge, what have you decided to give your children? I am asking those who come and stay here. You Hindus, Christians, Muslims, people from Brahmin to Chandala come and stay here because I have love and obligation towards you and you towards me. I have told you after doing something. But you haven't seen that. You are living here like a resentment which is the biggest layer of an undignified life. You should not bring your old resentment inside here and spend it. If you spend it, your children will be lost. I won't be able to do anything then. Why do you do this mischief here? Catching the flaw that has come in your tradition, I am telling you by changing even the freedom of worship to another path so that the culture of your house can change. But you are not able to think, know, or obey it.

[GV3:P1223] I removed the tradition you have been worshiping. I didn't do it by looking whether it is Hindu, Christian, Muslim, or Buddhist. The core of that old culture has been uprooted from your tradition. Did I look whether you are a Brahmin or a Chandala? I am asking if you have ever thought that at least one child who has lived here till today should grow up in this new culture. The experience I have got from everyone who worked with me, from their family as a whole, is pain. If so, you should say.

[GV3:P1224] Why have I done this for you? If you don't need it, then why did you make me do it? Has anyone among you been ready to live in that state and show it to me? Are you living here to grow this idea given by God in an evil way and ruin yourselves?

[GV3:P1225] Having grabbed all your old meannesses and doing the work of freeing you from it, the first thing told to you is that you must abandon caste, color, and dharma. If you have abandoned it, you must say so. If you have abandoned it, you wouldn't behave like this here, would you? Haven't you made a culture carried from somewhere your asset? Are you being raised with so much effort for this? Have I done it for you by sustaining myself? Tell me, I need to know it from you yourself.

[GV3:P1226] You should not assume that I am talking only about the matter of caste. I state firmly that none of you have been careful in any matter. What is the purpose of you wrapping these beautiful words near me? Why did you come here, believe in this, live here, that is what you should tell me. If any one person has acted lovingly towards this idea, towards me, and towards the institution, tell me that. Except for spreading the friendship of prosperity wherever you see it and living engaged in the action of creating prosperity that is not there, what have you done for this? If you do not want your culture to grow in your children, you must definitely move from here. Otherwise, you will go only after making it a culture that destroys and cuts this.

[GV3:P1227] You have aimed somewhere and done karma. To appease whom, to appease God, are you showing this meanness? If you show this in the future, I will not stand with you. It is enough if those standing in this and those related to this understand that and act. I have definitely told you this. If you have the intention that you can live truthfully, only then believe in this, live here, believe in this, associate with this. You don't have to say anything and make me listen. I won't believe it.

[GV3:P1228] You became the Shudra class. The mistake that happens from you is not intentional. When acting, it will come like that. How many of you came after seeing this before seeing me physically. Only if you handle this understanding that it is the departure of God's will, you can increase your merit. Otherwise, no theory or miracles are being told here. If I show a miracle, what will you get? Someone showed a miracle.

[GV3:P1229] Someone showed a miracle. Someone showed off as a big person. So what peace of mind do we have? You are seeing this in person. You are seeing the merit of the soul in person and taking it. There are so many people who have known me for a long time. Because they lacked family fortune, they went astray. What is family fortune? Brahmin, Kshatriya, Vaishya, Shudra. You have understood that now it is the time when the class standing in the fourth quarter has power. What power? The Shudra class is entitled to the knowledge of Brahman. When saying Shudra class, there is a Brahmin and a Shudra in each of our houses. This is justice. But this is not what we have been made to understand. Instead of that, they celebrated the Varna-Ashrama dharma, hid us under that Varna, and made it a caste. It has grown to such an extent that no one can do anything. Today, if we take India as a whole, there are one hundred and eighty-four castes. Everyone should think where we have reached and stand.

[GV3:P1230] 2001 Birthday Edition (August)

[GV3:P1231] Karma Realization of Yugadharma

[GV3:P1232] Through the Coming Generation

[GV3:P1233] (From an interview with the Guru)

[GV3:P1234] Question: Guru, I recently happened to read Ahamporul Vivekamudritam, which you published as a reply letter to Guru Nitya Chaitanya Yati in the April 1990 issue of Santhigiri spiritual magazine. It is considered as a correction and guidance for the activities prevailing in the intellectual and philosophical fields. If examined closely, it is also a warning for the severe ideological and karmic crises that have arisen in India and many parts of the world. The letter written in March 1984 to Indira Gandhi, who was the Prime Minister then, the notices published at various times after that, and the warnings given to the people, administrators, and intellectuals through other media were about the crises that India and other countries of the world are going to face in the near future and their remedies. Whatever disaster the suggestive moods and knowledge expressions in the excellent guidelines were about, all of it has already happened on the face of the earth today. Are there any more suggestions to be given as a remedy for this bad situation?

[GV3:P1235] Guru: There are instructions for that in all that has been said. I do not know whether you have fully imbibed it or not. Start with a small karma and move to a bigger one - that is right. Starting from the big, studying from a research perspective, and finally taking the subjects spoken by wise visionaries and passing on a side of knowledge to the people through oneself - there is no meaning in that line of thought. Then, doing something is better than doing nothing at all. That is the only relevance it has.

[GV3:P1236] Question: I didn't understand the reference to "something".

[GV3:P1237] Guru: There are four categories of people who set out for asceticism or with the intention of knowing the path of knowledge - the doer, the devotee, the yogi, and the wise. Among the yogis themselves, there are many types like Hamsa Yogi, Hatha Yogi, Jnana Yogi, and Karma Yogi. There are many divisions in each. Also, those who accepted the authority of the Vedas and the non-Vedic people who rejected it. For example, Buddhist-Jain sects. Then the Vedic class who accepted the authority of the Vedas. For example, Sankhyas, Naiyayikas, Mimamsakas, Advaitins, etc. They are the ones who have acquired the authority of Vedanta philosophies.

[GV3:P1238] Like this, there are countless divisions. There are stories of some of them wandering around without even a means of livelihood and destroying even their own families. There are also those who set out giving up everything, aiming only for peace of mind. Not a few set out seeing the ascetics who have acquired name and fame by offering peace to various diseases and showing various siddhis. Thus, harboring many resolves and expectations, these seekers set out motivated by their culture, tradition, and personal likes and dislikes. But I do not see anyone among this group who is able to think and know about their own soul's fortune.

[GV3:P1239] In these types of people, who continue their search about the fortunes and misfortunes of their birth by asking someone according to their knowledge, some spend ages knowing a little and not knowing a little, and finally live as a family person and live in some ashram for eight or ten months and look after themselves. While some others lead an ashram life with the thought that they are the ones who set out for something, let their own life pass like this itself. Some of such people, even if they cannot carry the idea, spend time in that movement itself and decide that this is enough.

[GV3:P1240] Question: What does Guru mean by "even if one cannot carry the idea"?

[GV3:P1241] Guru: An idea conducive to self-purification. Living in a movement without thinking about anything conducive to self-purification - this is one meaning. Some get visions in dreams of gods, goddesses, and great souls whom they love, believe in, and worship. This is enough for them. There are some householders belonging to the category mentioned earlier who have got such visions. It is known that thousands of people in this movement of ours itself have visions in this manner. But none of this can be said as realization. After experiencing some petty visions of some gods, goddesses, and great souls like this, some assume that they have acquired something great. It cannot be said that it is not knowledge. In a way, it is knowledge. But our need is not just that.

[GV3:P1242] Question: If souls curious to search for truth are like this, what will be the situation of those who have grown up in the political and other material fields?

[GV3:P1243] Guru: Some very rich people will behave like knowledgeable people. Some behave like misers and pretend to be very charitable. They will also give small amounts for name and fame. But some are not like that. They live like merchants with what they have, give value to justice, and preserve the merit they have earned without losing it. Some others degenerate like yogabhrashtas after a while. Among yogabhrashtas themselves, there are those who become virtuous souls who take birth again. They are the ones who do virtuous works politically and otherwise. Such works are what become good for the land and its people. Some others become good householders. Such householders give birth to one or two children and end their lives wishing to live as a vanaprastha and become an ascetic. It is seen that they make their own children do good and live like a sacrificing person.

[GV3:P1244] Question: All this is true. All is agreed upon. In today's situation, those in political administration and the doer, devotee, yogi, and wise all suffer by standing as the remainder of a smoldering karma. What is the remedy for this?

[GV3:P1245] Guru: What we are doing in Santhigiri today is the remedy for this. Ancestral purification is the most important in it. When talking about ancestors, there are even ancestors like devas. But in the special state called Kaliyuga, administrators and even other high ancestors are wandering around.

[GV3:P1246] Question: I have read about this information indicated in the letter written to Indira Gandhi then. In addition to that, we have also known through experience that all the ancestral purifications being done here are very effective. Also, we know that you have been exhorting since 1987 that rulers will have various difficulties, and there will be a situation where they will be confused not knowing how to lead their followers and people. Today, conflicts are happening not only in Kerala, but in India and all over the world. Your prophecies that it will remain strong have also become reality. In this background, we are curious to know when a lasting peace can be achieved for this.

[GV3:P1247] Guru: All this has been told in detail in many ways. The present situation of conflict will continue for another ten years. The world situation will suffer various damages within this period. After ten years, the damages will start decreasing. By the year two thousand and five, people will start working with karmic strength in a state where a state of peace will be created. It will definitely happen as Brahma's decision.

[GV3:P1248] Question: The present time has the freedom for any individual in society to reach the administrative level. But after a while, their idea self-destructs or is destroyed. Or their karmic path splits or pieces capable of splitting are moved. We saw that even big countries like Russia are not an exception to this. Guru, why is this?

[GV3:P1249] Guru: That is true. Now there is a scene building a greater danger than that.

[GV3:P1250] Question: What is that danger?

[GV3:P1251] Guru: Middle-class officials are interested in raising and educating their children like the children of the richest people. That desire is also fulfilled to some extent. No matter how much effort is made, most of the children are not able to rise to that level. Even if special tuition is arranged for each subject, they fail in lower classes and SSLC. Other than a desire of the guardian, there is no benefit from spending this money. Instances of a child studying for pre-degree, writing it four or five times, passing, reaching the degree level, and failing no matter how many times they write are not rare. The reason for all this can be contained in a single word: lack of fortune. Even ascetics, those in South Travancore, are seen using the word karmaphala (fruit of action). If one is a true ascetic, they say it after ultimately knowing it. A householder will also say these very words. But not understanding the meaning of that word.

[GV3:P1252] Question: What do you mean by saying without knowing the meaning?

[GV3:P1253] Guru: You have many examples in your own experience for that.

[GV3:P1254] Question: Whenever talking about the nation and politics, whatever you had indicated earlier that Russia, China, and similar communist countries will be subjected to split and power change has happened. Does it mean that this is not applicable to Congress and similar right-wing parties?

[GV3:P1255] Guru: Not just for Congress, it will wonderfully happen to any party or ideology. It will happen even to their houses and their followers' houses. This happens because big actions are orchestrated by sitting at the helm of the government in such a way as to protect selfish interests. This karma accumulates in individuals and happens at home. This is a specialty of the time.

[GV3:P1256] Question: Are you saying that the specialty of the time is applicable regarding any karma? Could you explain what the specialty of the time is?

[GV3:P1257] Guru: The specialty of Kali has been spreading since the birth of Sri Krishna. The temple entry in Travancore is also an indication of a strong specialty of the time. But people who didn't know what a temple is didn't get the benefit of temple entry.

[GV3:P1258] Question: You often say that communists who do not believe in the temple are better than temple believers. That is not clear.

[GV3:P1259] Guru: The mind of the communists is not as we say. Even if their mind is different, they are not bad at all in saying no. It is much better to say no than to say yes and destroy the land and home through various actions. At least they have that much honesty. It is appropriate not to believe and worship the temple system which does not have karma suitable for the times.

[GV3:P1260] Question: If so, we are worshiping and making others worship in Santhigiri, aren't we!

[GV3:P1261] Guru: The worship happening here is different. The worship happening here shows what the result of the merit established as the foundation stone of dharma is by seeking karma imagining the god and goddess, doing karma for ages, and making others do it.

[GV3:P1262] Question: Is it to be understood that the consecration concept of Santhigiri is a symbol of the fulfillment of karmic merit acquired over crores of births through ages and the merit which is the birthplace of dharma? If so, what is intended by this worship?

[GV3:P1263] Guru: The principle established here is Sankalpa Karma Manasa. Guru is a special state that functions by understanding the karmas of different times and the traditions of karma of each time. This special state of Guru is the medium of worship in Santhigiri. The karmic course established with four varnas is being made into one through this medium. Through this, not only is the principle of the Trimurti system made clear to the world, but it is also intended to create a path for spiritual thinkers to find out if there is a spiritual merit above the Trimurti system. By discovering that there is such a spiritual merit, one realizes how great a visionary truth this stands as, to turn that religion-transcending consciousness into action beneficial to the people. What is established in Santhigiri is a symbol of a karmic concept to make not only spiritual thinkers but also seekers and even common people embodiments of dharma by bringing them to the path of knowledge.

[GV3:P1264] Question: You yourself say that there are karmas of different times and traditions for karmas. Then how can one deny the gods and goddesses who are the instruments of karmic efficiency of Treta and Dwapara Yugas?

[GV3:P1265] Guru: Those who have observed my life can understand that the karma and instruments of karma of Treta and Dwapara Yugas have been greatly respected. In the early stages of my life, those karmas have been taken up by accepting gods and goddesses. In that way, we have taken up crores of karmas with all their rights and wrongs. Thus, it has become a guiding liberation karma not only of gods and goddesses but also of ancestors and even lineages of sages. That is, our ideology is the liberation ideology of the world. This is an ideology that has been given with authority only to us.

[GV3:P1266] Question: Isn't it the intellectuals and rulers who should put into practice this karmic concept authorized to the Guru?

[GV3:P1267] Guru: But are they able to control themselves or control their homes or followers? They can only mold generations like them. None of them will be able to act on this concept in a way that saves the world.

[GV3:P1268] Question: If contemporaries could not be made a mouthpiece, then through whom do you intend to grow this karma?

[GV3:P1269] Guru: They will bring it about as much as the true nature of this path is understood through the growing generation. But there is a goodness hidden in this idea that will completely change their children. This goodness will fulfill them through karmic course and make them owners of knowledge wealth necessary to sustain them through dharmic course. That same wealth of knowledge will also entitle them to liberation. Thus, through the actions of these children who elevate themselves in the high states of qualifications, goodness to the home, karmic eligibility to the land, and good governance to the nation will rise. This karma is the foundation stone that contains all the greatness in all the traditional karmic courses that are ages old as God.

[GV3:P1270] Question: If we examine the history from the Vedic period and before, we can see that there is a strong social background that can defeat even the modern philosophical systems built by the Acharyas. Will this idea be able to overcome the unassailable nature of this social structure which has become complex with rules and subtle customs and practices through Dharmashastras?

[GV3:P1271] Guru: No one can destroy this. To destroy this idea that came into being by God's decision, God's permission is also needed. All good and bad have been shown to us. This is a basic concept that has completely discarded what needs to be discarded and completely maintained and strengthened what needs to be maintained, obeying what is told by a disembodied voice. There are no additions in this. This is a basic model that has the qualification for the future generation to take and handle with strength and faith at all times. The time when children who are the breeding ground of karmic eligibility to imbibe this idea will grow and develop is not far.

[GV3:P1272] Question: Does your statement imply that even the births of such children will be God's mandate?

[GV3:P1273] Guru: Yes. Such births will happen in the near future not only in India but in all continents of the world. You yourself will be able to see it. This is a complete goal of Santhigiri.

[GV3:P1274] Question: I understood that the goal of Santhigiri is immense and broad. I also understood that the idea of Santhigiri is Brahma's decision. The karmic success of any idea depends on geniuses and rulers, doesn't it? But you say that contemporary rulers who do not have the ability to control themselves or control their own homes and followers will not be able to do any fruitful action for the realization of this idea. Who is then intended for this?

[GV3:P1275] Guru: It has been told in detail in the birthday message of the sixty-fourth Guru Jayanti edition in nineteen ninety, hasn't it? Didn't you see that? People like the great souls of the past will be born among the people of this generation. Only they can do the work suitable for the time.

[GV3:P1276] Question: I saw the birthday message. While thinking about the discrimination of such great souls, a news item that came under the title 'He comes, in India this time' in a Malayalam newspaper during the same period the birthday message was published seems relevant. It is the computer calculation of R. Subramaniam, an astronomer at the Calcutta Birla Planetarium. He calculated and observed the definite possibility of spheres shining like the star of Bethlehem seen by the Magi in the past (Jupiter and Venus) coming together and transforming into a conjunction of light. Just as it happened as a prelude to the birth of Christ, he considers this as a clear indication of the birth of some other great soul. Can't this conjunction of light be considered as an indication of the birth of some great soul who is to be born as Brahma's mandate for the karmic realization of your idea according to Brahma's decision?

[GV3:P1277] Guru: The scientific conclusions of scientists are correct. Because science is calculation. Their instruments will also be able to find what is there. But when thinking about its causal state, the course of events does not yield to their conclusions. The controller of this universe stands adorning this entire nature. Many great souls born in the purity of intention of that power stand as shining spheres in the sky. Along with this, there are also children born in the concept of those respective ideas. I will give an example. The origin of Kali is with the ascension of Sri Krishna to heaven, isn't it? When he was about to give up his body, Sri Krishna told Arjuna to take the women and children to the mainland. Then Krishna goes for penance. On the way to the mainland, in Gujarat, the king of the forest dwellers attacks them. Despite being such a great archer and expert in penance and weaponry, and shooting all the arrows in the quiver and hitting with the bow, Arjuna was defeated. Then Uddhava said, in the presence of Krishna, the Lord of Yoga, everything would have been possible for you. Kali has already started. Your weapons, empowered by the mantra siddhi of gods and goddesses, can no longer invoke power. With the Samadhi of Krishna, that power was lost. Now it is better for you to hand over the reign of the kingdom to Parikshit and go for penance. This is history.

[GV3:P1278] It is not difficult for those who examine history closely to understand that great calamities happened to India and other countries adjacent to India with the beginning of Kali. After Sri Krishna, Buddha, Vardhamana Mahavira, Jesus, Nabi, the Gurus of Parayipetta Panthirukulam, the eighteen Siddhars, Sree Narayana Guru, Chattampi Swami - thus many great souls were born in various parts of the world. Scholars do not think about this or understand it properly. If those who pride themselves on their scholarship had thought and known about this with value, they could have evaluated and known the existing situation with power of discrimination and subtlety and changed the perspective of the people and made them children who desire a good life.

[GV3:P1279] Question: Are you saying that scholars and geniuses are people traveling on the wrong path?

[GV3:P1280] Guru: Why do you think so? Honoring the ideas of great souls, they did not strengthen through action and impart knowledge appropriate for the time to generations. This is the meaning of what I said.

[GV3:P1281] Many Mahatmas stand as numerous spheres. What scientists see might not necessarily be that true radiance. Another thing is, at the time of the birth of Mahatmas, millions of other children will also be born in that karmic potential and in that zodiac sign. Let me tell you an example of a vision we have received. Since nineteen seventy-three, many such visions have been received. Their real nature cannot be revealed here. The facts can only be stated briefly.

[GV3:P1282] From nineteen seventy-three onwards, the intelligence of children born every twelve years will be extraordinary. There will be some thinkers and sages scattered among them. From nineteen eighty-five to ninety-seven, many events will occur inside and outside India. The intelligence of children born until then will be very high. From nineteen ninety-seven to two thousand, the situation will be balanced. By the year two thousand and five, new governments will come around the world through the thinking habits of children born in nineteen seventy-three. Those governments will be the ones sowing the seeds of world peace. Thus, the karmic realization of the Yuga Dharma will be achieved through the coming generations.

[GV3:P1283] 2001 Birthday Edition (August)

[GV3:P1284] Worship of God is overflowing with mischief

[GV3:P1285] Our society lacks knowledge. The reason is that it has become a Shudra class. I have told you this many times. Father, mother, wife, husband, children, wealth, education, lordship—what is this society doing other than fighting keeping these in mind? They have not understood that this is not their good time.

[GV3:P1286] We were lucky with the birth of Sri Krishna. But we couldn't take advantage of that luck. After Sri Krishna, thousands of acharyas came and spoke in the Hindu logic. But not even one person understood that worshiping God is not about magic tricks and stubborn arguments.

[GV3:P1287] I know a Balakrishnan who is called Anchal Swami. This man contracted a disease. To cure it, a Kunjuraman near Varkala railway station and another person brought him to me. That was the time I was staying in Varkala Santhigiri. They came to me and said, this person has a disease, he was a good person, but he has lost his way, Swami must do something. Then I said, you should bring some of their relatives too. Otherwise, I can't do anything. They went and brought all the relatives. This man stayed in the ashram for four or five months. This disease was cured. Then I said, you shouldn't touch anything. All these things are Siddhi practices. I convinced him of all this and he took it as truth and went away. But he again got immersed in the magic tricks involving Siddhis.

[GV3:P1288] I was taken there again. When I went there, two or three statues had been brought from somewhere and kept there. He was practicing this Siddhi on them. I took all that and threw it into a dry well there. Then I gave him a picture of Sree Narayana Guru to keep. I also told him not to do anything anymore. The disease was cured. The disease is nothing else, he got a vision and there was no one to correct it. Pretending to be a great devotee, he would go to Palani two or three times. After going, he would be brought back tied up from there.

[GV3:P1289] What God has given should be seen as given by God. I am only an instrument. Do not understand vision like Siddhi logic. If God has given you some knowledge, you should take it exactly like that. The knowledge we have about God is in the form of Siddhi logics. In that logic, we cannot see God. Magic tricks are never the way to see God.

[GV3:P1290] People see Sri Shankara, Sri Narayana Guru, and Chattampi Swami as great Siddhas. Siddhi is not wisdom. Doing stupid things in magic is not wisdom. Sri Shankara, Sri Narayana Guru, and Chattampi Swami cannot be understood in this way. It is written that during Sri Shankara's last days, he left his body and entered another. Sankaracharya used to go to the forest for penance with his disciples. One day a king died there. The story is written that he asked his disciples to keep his body there, entered the king's body, and went to the palace. If Sri Shankara can be destroyed like this by a poet's imagination, what can anyone not do?

[GV3:P1291] What is said about what motivated Sri Shankara to enter this king's body? That is an even stranger story. It is said that Saraswati appeared and asked about sexual pleasure. See the nonsense they have said? Who are these people who are ready to say such things about Sri Shankara? For what purpose are they saying all this. If anyone has shown magic tricks like this, if any stories have been written about it, taking it and babbling to show greatness is your ignorance.

[GV3:P1292] What I have to tell you is, you should not come to me with miracles. I do not have that practice. Many people who could have reached a good position are getting ready to line up behind Siddhi magic. Some people who come to me also do so. It cannot be said that they have no devotion. But they do not know what true devotion is. What can I say to such people?

[GV3:P1293] Things come and go in the hands of those who show miracles. What all do they do to make people believe that everything in the world is happening through them! God will help for all their struggles. No one can deny that. Therefore, one must keep the knowledge that this was received through God's grace.

[GV3:P1294] There are some who have stood with me in Aluva, Aruvippuram, and Sivagiri. They are those who stood with me and understood God's glory well. But they couldn't preserve it. Today they go after magic tricks and show foolishness. They haven't harmed anyone. I am well convinced of that too. But they have a character of not being able to know that good path of God. But I haven't spoken about that subject till today. It is not appropriate for me to speak about it. Among them, one person has studied every sentence of Narayana Guru Swami and goes around as his spokesperson. That's a good thing. I am also happy. But why is he showing these foolish acts?

[GV3:P1295] Another example—there is another family in Anchal. I don't remember his name. It's Idamula. One of his children is a doctor, another girl was studying to become a doctor then. Another one is studying engineering. At that time, I called all these kids and talked to them. But they didn't understand it. That was a time when I was sitting idle, unable to do anything according to the intimation that I shouldn't do anything for two and a half years. What have they done? Their job is to show results using this Seva Murthi.

[GV3:P1296] I am not into this miracle-showing business. It's a matter in my experience. Can't I also do something to make a name? When I told this family, I felt that they didn't have the luck to obey the word. After that, I haven't gone there. Later, that mother came and told me about the child. I didn't get involved in anything after that. Especially, it was a time when I shouldn't do anything. Doing nothing, I just kept looking at what was there in spirituality alone. Then there was an intimation that I could do something after ten years. When I started doing that, again there were many difficulties. What is that? All the demons belonging to the Trimurti system and under it started opposing me. I fell into a difficult phase where I couldn't do anything.

[GV3:P1297] After that, the same thing happened to a Bhaskara Pillai who was living in Trivandrum at that time. He held onto this Seva system. I told him not to do any of this. But he went according to the wishes of a boy in that house. Today he is standing on a different path. Will he get any salvation? I told him then, you only have this much luck. You should only see that much. If there is a child with any such character, shouldn't you make them understand and do what is necessary for them? He didn't obey when I told him. With that, he alienated those kids from me. These children are now useless. Someone said that his brother-in-law's child would die at the age of thirteen and brought him to me. After he turned fifteen, he hasn't come to me again.

[GV3:P1298] Why am I saying this? Our ancestors served or worshiped something like this, didn't they? We cannot avoid doing that. Aren't you worshiping even Karimkuttichathan in your families? This is the work done instead of worshiping God. Other than an acquaintance of having worshiped many such Yogiswaras and others, what knowledge do we have about worshiping God?

[GV3:P1299] It is said that Chattampi Swami wrote the Balasubramanya Moola Mantra for Narayana Guru Swami. They are just playing around saying that. Are they saying all this because they know anything about it?

[GV3:P1300] This is what you should understand. Historically, a tragedy of not being able to tell the truth happened to the Brahmin, Kshatriya, Vaishya, and Shudra here. Why? The Manvantaras were written incorrectly. For the sake of Kali's dharma, Mahatmas were made to be born in every Kali. If that Mahatma goes astray, it will again go and stand in the Trimurti system. It won't move forward from there. This is the history of Atri Maharshi and Gautama Maharshi. Even Sri Rama was amazed like this and even Sri Rama couldn't escape from it.
