A story is told regarding this Brahmarakshas. Summoning gods like Agni, Vayu, and Varuna, a Yaksha threw a blade of grass. But these gods couldn't move or shake it. Agni couldn't burn it, or Vayu couldn't blow it away. What can we understand about this blade of grass? We are evaluating this miracle while standing inside a karmic potential that has been kept as a time feature in the Puranas. What is the meaning in saying I don't respect this? There is a matter of it having been respected, right. Is it that we don't respect it because it doesn't exist, because we have too much knowledge, or because we lack knowledge?

[GV3:P1360] So, we must get a matter according to our knowledge. Otherwise, you will face great destruction. Let this be understood by you first.

[GV3:P1361] What is your knowledge? Yesterday, I went to certain places. Today too, I went. Now, I am coming back. To put it differently, I went, got tired, lay down, and slept well. How does he know that he slept? Sleeping is a transformation of life. Only he knows about sleeping well. But what is the reason? We need the action-oriented capability to recover what the reason is.

[GV3:P1362] If what is seen in the waking state appears in the dream state, and what is seen in the dream state appears in deep sleep (Sushupti), there is a way to give liberation to deep sleep. Sushupti means the state of sleeping without knowing anything. But it is not that. There is a part where one sees without eyes in Sushupti. Then which eye is this that sees? Whatever is seen in the core of the mind, that is it. That is the course of action we practice. How will that dharma reach us?

[GV3:P1363] In the nature of this mind's eye, the course of action of billions of years is being dragged before us. I never did that. My ancestors did not do it. Then who did this? What we see here is an action that was hiding behind some mistakenly committed action. If you do not understand this completely, it will be difficult for you to grasp it.

[GV3:P1364] What you have assumed is that if you call upon God, you should become rich tomorrow. Or become the greatest ruler. Or roam around freely without any disease and attain happiness by acting as you please. Which of these is good? All are good indeed. But you must understand one thing. We have some familiar awareness that our ancestors believed in, knew, and made known. We need to know where this will lead us. How does this blossom before us in the waking, dream, and deep sleep states? We have been made an instrument for this.

[GV3:P1365] We are coming to know and see what blossoms in the waking, dream, and deep sleep states one by one. What are this waking, dream, and deep sleep? In the waking state, the eyes may be closed, or they may be open. Whether the eyes are closed or open, there is a nature of seeing. Even when sleeping, that special quality does not leave us. Not only that, one sits and knows silently and motionlessly, in a way that the nature of space and time cannot be known. This is another side of deep sleep. It is difficult to know how this comes to us. If we start counting the seeds of this, another problem arises. Did the seed or the mango come first? What answer will be given to this? Who can we become by reaching a sense of dharma that is effective by asking such things and dismissing them with logic? No one.

[GV3:P1366] If you accept a condition like Guru, Deva, Rishi, or Sannyasi, you must understand one thing. What did we get in the actions of about twenty-five prophets who have passed away? Similarly, what will you get if you hear, know, and read about the Rishis who came and went over the ages, and a Guru who came as the controller of these miraculous actions, showing many supernatural powers and miraculous deeds? Not that; what you need is a center of faith that grasps these past matters. What will you see in this action that stands firm, grasping and attaining the actions of the ages? This is what should be seen in the waking, dream, and deep sleep states. Thus, when it comes in the waking, dream, and deep sleep states, and is experienced, one should be able to know that it is such and such. There are some here who can know like that. Then what should we do? We should acquire the capability of action to grasp the cause, which became the reason for getting it when we caught hold of it. That cause, which guides to recover the good and the bad, can only tell the capability to acquire this path. That should be told according to each one's knowledge. Otherwise, thinking that one's own action is completely perfect, one will be confused, love will come as a reflection of ego, and nothing will happen.

[GV3:P1367] This is not God's love. It is a course of action standing after traveling in thousands of paths that we cross, walk, know, and act upon. It cannot be taken by hearing, knowing, and talking about it. They will only guide you by telling you what you see and hear according to your knowledge. Before the Guru who tells like this, all these - all the Jnanis, Yogis, Gurus, and Prophets who have come in all the traditions that have come till today - must come. Only such a person should you accept as a Guru. Otherwise, you will make a mistake, and fall to the ground like a person grasping a palm frond.

[GV3:P1368] At least having understood this much and imbibed life, how should you perform your duties? You will not incur any harm by not thinking of God. But you will not get its benefits.

[GV3:P1369] What is meant by belief in God? The ancestral dosha (flaw) of many predecessors, who built many mandapas and large temples with offerings and gifts, waiting for its prosperity and wealth to come, remains in us. How will this dosha be removed?

[GV3:P1370] You don't need to wander around for this. No matter where you wander, it won't work. What you need is a Guru who has the ability to liberate everything that you and your ancestors previously worshipped. Whether it is a prophet, or one who has stood in the path of prophets and shown smartness by telling much historical wealth, or one who has gone far in the work of Siddhis and attained the Siddhi of flying in the sky, all of them must come before this Guru for the sake of life. Unless you surrender and pray to such a Guru, you will not get any special benefit.

[GV3:P1371] 2001 Birthday Edition (August)

[GV3:P1372] Certain truths that must be known at the respective times

[GV3:P1373] What all can we do, and what can we not do in an action that stands beyond thought, beyond intellect, and similar to the sky? If learned people and great thinkers, unable to evaluate and study their own lives, impart love by specifying and making others speak with unique imagination, how can that eternal special love, covered by Maya, be imparted and sustained?

[GV3:P1374] Intellect, ability, and capability are indeed needed to sustain that richness of love. But the scholarly nature is not like that. The scholarly nature is to try to endear by learning the meaning of words and writing commentaries on the letters. That should not be considered a mistake of that life. They are trying to maintain the dignity of action, and in that process, if they lose their object of pride, all their thoughts will be flawed. Not only that, if one tries to gain merit by showing the world all the knowledge learned, spoken, sung, and written, destroying all the goodness of life, one will fall into the biggest blunder.

[GV3:P1375] Whenever such a soul is born, it will come hiding behind scholarship. Most people will not be able to respect the dharma of the time or acquire earned merit. The soul is frustrated as it cannot endear and merge the ability of the learned intellect. They can only waste time engaging in logic by finding faults in the Vedas, epics, and Puranas. Even if God gives them severe diseases of some kind, their scholarly pride will not leave them. When this disease torments them in various ways, leaving them helpless and not a subject for an inquiring mind, keeping fortune completely away, and when they can do nothing but call God and find their mind lightened in the final days, it might come as the merit of some birth. Even in that birth, they might become the owners of a mind that thinks in the worldly path they learn and teach, and dismisses others as ignorant. Other than that, they cannot impart love. They will reach a stage where they cannot do anything in the matter of sharing love, even with their own wife or children. If one is a Brahmachari or a Sannyasi, after learning much, they will end up in an action that curses this learning. Other than sharing their knowledge, wealth, and all subsequent life comforts, they can never think for the goodness of their own soul. Or even if they think, this soul, exhausted by reading, knowing, learning, and teaching, will only end up like the chaff of the actions of a man's life caught in a dilemma. Finally, this is the only way to see salvation.

[GV3:P1376] In the case of Hindus, they would gather some chaff and waste, turn it into ash, smear it on their bodies, and to get some decoration for it, they would adorn it with sandalwood paste and vermilion, and become those who explain the meaning of the name.

[GV3:P1377] Question: What does the Guru mean by the meaning of the name?

[GV3:P1378] Guru: There is a group of people who show off by telling the story of how Shiva, when he became the guardian of the cremation ground, smeared ash to keep away ghosts and stood with a spear and trident, guarding the cemetery. Another group, after seeing the adornment of Shiva and Vishnu, when a vermilion dot is added, have preserved this decoration as the meaning of the name, becoming Shaivite, Vaishnavite, and Shakta. Hindu scholars are still assigning various meanings to this. When there was similarity in the father's seed and the mother's blood, some people gave the Vaishnava concept a coolness and pointed to it as the seat of the mind. Thus, when exhausted following all this, some people find another religious doctrine and take refuge in it.

[GV3:P1379] Do not assume that all this is being rejected. This is said to understand that ours is a familiarity that has been practiced and maintained, whatever has changed and turned in any religious doctrine from ancient times.

[GV3:P1380] Then what will be of use to us? The difficulty of seeing experienced people is also great. Rationalists and liberationists who believe that there is or isn't a course of action acquired over many births will drag the experienced person, who comes with good thoughts, into a suffocating action.

[GV3:P1381] Question: Guru mentioned earlier that it is very difficult to recognize experienced people. What kind of people would the experienced people Guru refers to here be?

[GV3:P1382] Guru: Those who have made Yakshis, demons, Madan, and Mantramurthis experienced with great devotion and faith are also experienced people. They can also show some miraculous feats. Then there are some people who have made deities and prophets experienced. We may also get experiences in the way they learned and the way they experienced. We understand this as a truth and attach our lives behind it. There are not a few people who narrate and make others listen to the histories of great men and engage in discussions about good people. Because they have the familiarity of experiencing all this. But these are the lights of misfortune that cannot be utilized for liberation. This is also an action where our mind itself is lost.

[GV3:P1383] Question: It is seen that every path has its own shortcomings. What is the way to understand only what is right and move forward?

[GV3:P1384] Guru: There is truly only one path to mention. Do not prepare to learn and know. Even if it is small knowledge, the familiarities sought in the light of experience should be seen for a long time.

[GV3:P1385] Everything seen in this world is the fulfillment of good and bad courses of action. It will change and turn depending on the increase or decrease of the habit of thought at each level. By God's grace, there is no other instrument but to highlight that personality like Aurobindo, who knew the dharmas of the era and became a vessel for realization. When such people search and get exhausted, in return for their calls to God for a long time, five Gurus of different periods will come, giving corrections to the dharmas of time without mixing up the disembodied voice and the visible. They will help us. If these Gurus of different periods do not help us, we will never be capable of seeking the path of that personality mentioned earlier.

[GV3:P1386] Find the personality that helps one become a vessel for realization. We should not be ignorant of the dharma of the era. We must be capable of imparting dharma by looking at the dharma of direct inferences. Unable to do that, we will fall into the theories of scholars and ignoramuses that help us fall into a much harsher pit, like water stagnating in a pit of meaning interpretation, with termites and insects falling and decaying in it, acting like people without roots. Believe firmly that scholars and ignoramuses who attach all this behind nihilism, collect the meaning of the non-existent word Maya, and explain its meaning, cannot help us.

[GV3:P1387] If life merges into the sweetness of the voice that condemns everything with the thought that anyone can be destroyed, combining the negative thought of rejecting everything and the emotionality mixed with it, liberation will not be attained. Understand that not only a human body that has turned into sheaths with the help of the five elements and the five senses, but whatever living beings exist, they are all translations of these sheaths. Understand completely that the word 'meaning of the knowledge section' can only be attained by the Guru of the era who is the core of the dharma of the era, where the five elements, five senses, and five sheaths seek the section of action through knowledge sheaths and find the meaning. The Guru of the era will not deny anything, but will give importance to everything, attribute dignity, and be the one who finds the long-term familiarity seen in the meaning. This follower of the dharma of the era will be our guide.

[GV3:P1388] Question: How can we recognize this guide?

[GV3:P1389] Guru: It is not recognizing, it comes to us by our luck. It is the merit of infinite length of time. By what measure can it be recognized? Evolving from an insect, whatever life becomes, acting together with the five elements, five senses, and five sheaths, that great soul will be the owner of the four-yuga dharma determined as Satya Yuga, Treta Yuga, Dwapara Yuga, and Kali Yuga. Only that great soul can be such a guide.

[GV3:P1390] Many years ago, there were distinguished scholars who wrapped letters and gave meanings to them. Similarly, there are men of the era who speak of devotion, action, yoga, and knowledge. If we get a birth at the time when those Gurus are born, we can see a very large number of living souls who have attained family dharma through their lives, with our physical eyes and with the purity inherent within. We can also be told what they attained in the five senses, five sheaths, and five elements. If that experience comes to millions of people, they will have the luck to get birth when the appropriate visionary with the right signs, time, and context comes.

[GV3:P1391] Because they did not know the dharma of time, our predecessors, tyagis and religious scholars, became guides to the time cycles determined in the deity tradition, and thus the dharma of liberation is not useful. The author of the Gita himself says that the person who explains this is eligible for the title of Guru. There are many courses of action that have been known by believing this. Sri Krishna stood as a Guru and made Arjuna fight and destroy many experienced people and scholars like Drona and Bhishma. Even Sri Krishna, the Guru of the era, could not explain the path of the Guru of the era, which the Brahmin, Kshatriya, Vaishya, and Shudra had to work together to bring about, due to the ignorance of Arjuna, the great archer who completely won the eighteenth war, and the Shudra class here, and the scholarly dharma of the distinguished scholars. In this situation, where should we hold, whom should we hold - this should be our thought.

[GV3:P1392] Aurobindo said that Sri Krishna is not the supramental radiance. To say this, what should we see about the situation that existed here? The meaning of the yuga dharma divided into four periods can be understood only if we get a chance to be born, grow, and bring up in the period when a dharma Guru who shows what the dharma of the era is born. Without a timely search for such a great soul, we will never be able to find the meaning of that yuga dharma.

[GV3:P1393] The arrival of great souls itself can be taken as proof for this. For example, who acquired the capability to explain the sky dharma reached by Buddha, many other Gurus who went in search, and the prophets and apostles attached to that tradition?

[GV3:P1394] Question: Is sky dharma referring to the subtle?

[GV3:P1395] Guru: Yes. When we say subtle, it means seeing the many Gurus who came and went according to the calculation of time.

[GV3:P1396] Question: Does the Guru speak in two ways at the same time?

[GV3:P1397] Guru: Not two ways, it is the same way. We need a personality to explain what the time and dharma of time of the deities are, what the prophets and Gurus belonging to it are, and how they have sacrificed and martyred themselves for us. The density of love that ripens in that known knowledge is this sky nature.

[GV3:P1398] Question: Guru, please have the compassion to explain these things to us by placing us in some position in accordance with the yuga dharma.

[GV3:P1399] Guru: First, evaluate the sacrifices of the great souls who came before and after Sri Krishna. What sky nature they attained through their sacrifices - the Guru is the one who shows the courses of these souls. It is shown that it is far even for Sri Krishna. Also look at what the action is. If we take the case of a few who came after Sri Krishna, we can think about that action to some extent. Moreover, pay attention to the words of Vyasa, who took Arjuna for penance after the end of Sri Krishna. I don't think there are many who do not accept the teaching of the author of the Gita, who shows that Sri Krishna is everything and the same Sri Krishna points out everything and explains to Arjuna. Nor can it be said that anyone here has created scholarly hymns greater than those of Sri Krishna. Moses is one of the prophets who came after that. At the same time, both scholars and the ignorant agree that Lord Buddha is the one who came closest to the state of Nirvana. What did the followers of that Buddha turn into? I do not believe anyone would say that Jesus, who came after Buddha, was not a tyagi. In the final stage, Jesus asks his disciples to pray. We cannot be without compassion for that richness of sacrifice, which was announced three times before the cock crowed.

[GV3:P1400] What is the reason to ask to pray before the cock crows? The time from six o'clock to six o'clock is divided into four watches. The night and day are divided into watches without difference. Can anyone say that the watch when asked to pray before three o'clock in the morning has no special significance? It is when we know what this action is, which affects even Sri Krishna, that we can understand the subtle state of this world. We can talk about that later.

[GV3:P1401] 2001 Birthday Edition (August)

[GV3:P1402] The Knowledge Known Only to Great Men

[GV3:P1403] After reciting Vedanta verses, they call it Darshana (vision). Their side is that Darshana is wrong. If Darshana is wrong, how did the name Darshana come about? Darshana is not the course of action that sees things as existing and non-existing. Darshana is seeing only what exists. Who says it does not exist? This is the theory of consciousness. It is painful to speak nonsense in the exercise of intellect, citing knowledge of letters. This is the limitation of Hindus and Muslims. There is a limitation to everything called Darshana that has come so far. When one reaches the side of this limitation, helplessness occurs. No one will understand Darshana. This is my experience.

[GV3:P1404] An event of completion took place here. It was told to do such and such things, and it was done. Such and such people are coming. That great man is such and such a person. This experience is Darshana. Darshana is not the Vedanta they talk about. There is no need to conduct a Mimamsa here that destroys action by relying on the Vedanta of literal knowledge. It was the result of the vision seen inside the Prophet that led him to ask, what have you shown me? If religious theorists who know this do not understand it, what can we do? Whether anyone asks or not, I will say this. Because if there is no action of imparting and receiving love in the action of speaking face to face, to whom can we say this?

[GV3:P1405] The theorists seem to be standing to praise and glorify a mistake. That is why I say do not take up theoretical religion. I am expressing the sorrow that has come into my experience. We have only one religion. The religion of liberation. There is no liberation through any other religion. It will not happen. I have nothing to say to those who say it will happen.

[GV3:P1406] We have insulted the Gurus who showed what love is. God gave each great soul the position of that time. We know about that position here. Two thousand years, five thousand years, ten thousand years - I have not said anything more than the knowledge given to us.

[GV3:P1407] We need to know what Yuga Dharma is. There is no point in taking all these four together without following what needs to be followed in each Yuga. But our tradition is one that has taken all these four together. We were born into that very tradition. Where else can we go now to set things right?

[GV3:P1408] When we look at the long-term vision of time, we do not even have the time of an atom. What can we do in this short time? What is the knowledge of the theoretical religion that is filled in the atom and the infinite like? Our beginning is from the position it has reached. We cannot impart love by learning philosophy.

[GV3:P1409] Does anyone of us know the word of sorrow asked by the Prophet? All the great souls who came after and before it have come violating this Yuga Dharma. If one says that one can learn much and use Mimamsa, it will not work.

[GV3:P1410] Question: Guru's is experiential knowledge. We only have bookish knowledge. Practical knowledge is the greatest in knowledge, isn't it?

[GV3:P1411] Guru: We are all people who have stepped out of the verandah of destructive actions that cause great sorrow. Our goal will not be achieved without love coming to the fore. We need respect for that love. We need the knowledge to take it with humility. Without this, it will not become Dharma. It will become Adharma.

[GV3:P1412] Within these past five thousand years, many known great souls have come. They do not belong to the demonic action mentioned by these theorists. The demonic level mentioned by them is the remainder of the evil action standing at the practical level. Each of these great souls has come not in my knowledge or familiarity. I am giving an account for this. I have received this account many times. We must see all these great souls without exception. We need to find the side where a mistake occurred at their practical level. Whatever it is, we cannot reject them and place someone else. There are millions of people who obey and believe them. Even counting in millions is not enough. If we do not take on their failures, they have no other way. By accepting them, we are taking on their sins. We must experience that sin. Can we discard it saying we don't want it? We must see it in the practical action that is mixed as blood, marrow, and flesh. If we accept the action of a great soul, we must see what happened to that great soul and detail it in the action, not formalize it.

[GV3:P1413] God resides where science reaches a dead end. If I tell you this, will you be convinced?

[GV3:P1414] Question: Does it mean Guru is not a spiritualist?

[GV3:P1415] Guru: I have come to clear a path for you because you have no path to go.

[GV3:P1416] Question: What is the most difficult thing in this world?

[GV3:P1417] Guru: There is an action that our ancestors worshipped and made others worship. We cannot destroy it. We also do not have the knowledge to sustain it. Seeing this in a subtle way, the Prophet asks, what is this I am seeing! Why are you dragging me among these demons? This is the sorrow. If there is no solution to this sorrow, we will not have a solution to anything.

[GV3:P1418] The highest truth of the mind is called love. If that love has no respect, it is not love. We need someone to clear the path of love for us. We should be able to show this path. Once this is understood, there is no need to worry. If you understand this and act sincerely, God will give the result. I am not the one to give it. You just need to see me as a medium. We are performing actions within this circle that is joined corner to corner. If everyone cannot respect and take it equally, what can be done?

[GV3:P1419] All religions teach Vedas and scriptures. Aren't those who teach learned? Then why do you need to learn specially? Is it not because you cannot practice what you learned? That is why I said, do not take anything else - it will not work. You should grasp the ultimate history of the field where a great man came and worked. And then elevate that great man. Otherwise, we cannot move forward.

[GV3:P1420] We are taking upon ourselves the mistake that has come in the prophetic religion. Thus, we are taking on the 2444 Gurus as well. After taking them, we will make them work. What did you understand?

[GV3:P1421] The time of all these Gurus, our predecessors, has passed. However, the Prophet still has some more time. The time he mentioned is 1500 years. There are still a few more years left for that. The word of that great man cannot go wrong. Only after that much time has passed can it be taken up.

[GV3:P1422] When we pray, importance is given to each in a different way. Each has a different kind of vision. Getting this vision, one must come to a Guru authorized to carry that vision. There needs to be a personality to go down to the people and perform actions. For that, we must take up the action sitting from the apostles to the Ustad in the lineage of Gurus who have come so far. Sri Krishna and these mentioned prophets did not get time to take this up. This is the reason for them being stuck. This is a historical truth. No one can deny it. But we must respect whoever came wherever. It is our duty and necessity to clear the path for them who are stuck. We are acting like that because of God's grace.

[GV3:P1423] On the side of vision, if we get a Guru or a visionary, we must reach there and only by looking from there can we see what is beyond. Like climbing a mountain. Climbed and saw what was there. Then got down. Only by climbing up and down seven folds like that can we see what needs to be seen: there is a Guru on the mountain........

[GV3:P1424] It is not enough to mention the theory of Dharma. Importance lies in the theory of Karma (action). When the experience gained is utilized, it will be useful forever. When achieved, one can only say it is achieved. When it is utilized among the people, it reaches the path of knowledge.

[GV3:P1425] 2001 Birthday Edition (August)

[GV3:P1426] The Role of the Disciple

[GV3:P1427] Due to the flaw of the Trinity system and the Vedic rule that a Shudra cannot be touched, the Shudra was brought very low. The ignorance of that lineage also increased. When we say Shudra, there are Shudras in the houses of all sections, in the Brahmin's house, the Kshatriya's house, the Vaishya's, and the Shudra's houses. To maintain class consciousness, people of each caste have specified their class. There is a situation where one is not ready to accept someone as a Guru or assume the attitude of a Guru unless they belong to one's own caste. For example, this is a disaster that has been happening not just in Kerala, but in India itself since Shankaracharya. We have people in our land who enjoy talking at length mentioning the names of each great soul. If we think about how many people are there who absorb and act upon a great soul in any way, the situation will be different again. But remember the fact that the special power that accomplished the auspicious action of India through numerous Gurus is always carefully studying us.

[GV3:P1428] God has nothing to learn. Nevertheless, God is watching how our actions will be, and how those actions will reach the karma of life. It is easy to carefully understand a society that acts and suffers misery by being involved in their respective times, and the events that pass by earning some fortune behind the display of wealth, lordship, and ability, holding on to some extent without the misery being complete. God has granted us that easy way.

[GV3:P1429] There are four types of Gurus. Brahmavit, Brahmavaryan, Brahmavarishtan, Brahmavarishtan. Among them, Brahmavit is in the position of the Trinity, and Brahmavaryan is in the position of a Rishi. Only these two types of Gurus act among the people. The rest are not those who act among the people. You will not find them in front of you either. Because they are not the ones who come down much and work. They just keep telling what they see. It is difficult for you even to go and see them. You don't have the intellect to grasp what they say. Even if they lack capability, their knowledge is very high. They will continuously bring out that knowledge showing humility out of love. You will not be able to absorb them and act. That is not because they have no love for you or you have no love for them. There is no side where you can accept these two Gurus. They might have given teachings or guidance to a few people. Do not expect anything else. You will get something only from the Gurus Brahmavit and Brahmavaryan.

[GV3:P1430] Beyond these four types of Gurus, there are some other Gurus. They are born according to the times. God gives them a special rule and makes them born. Such Gurus will not have Siddhi (miraculous powers).

[GV3:P1431] Ramananda Swami is the Guru of Kabir Das. The very life of Kabir Das, as understood from Bhakta Vijayam and some other Puranas, is the life of Shuka Brahmarshi. Kabir Das is not born from a womb. The birth is of a special nature. That cannot be explained and convinced here. There is a story that the Trinity requested Kabir Das to be born in a certain way. Only if born like that could he be made to act according to their intention. Then Kabir Das says that he cannot be born from a womb. The Trinity promises Kabir Das that they will manage it otherwise. He agreed to take birth only after that. There is the history of only this one great man in this way. What that great man says about the world and the Guru is in very grand dimensions.

[GV3:P1432] Only through Kabir Das can we understand the glory of a Guru in such a great way. Gurus like those glorified by Kabir Das are rare in South India. Even if such an incident happens, we lack the tendency to sustain its glory, apart from tying it under a deity. The reason for that is that the rule of the Shudra happened to come here. Shudra means class Shudra. Not caste Shudra. Among humans, excluding Brahmin, Kshatriya, and Vaishya, all the rest are Shudras.

[GV3:P1433] This class Shudra was made to rule. Actually, it was not ruling. Through that rule, various kinds of subjects came under one king. Meaning, this king had no sense. He became king by force. Not as a king in the Kshatriya position. There are proofs for that in history. The history of Travancore itself is an example. The Ettuveetil Pillamar, Marthanda Varma, Arayogam, all are members of the Shudra class. They were adopting women from Kshatriya houses. The Mavelikkara royal family is an example of this. We did not get a chance to think about any of these historical events.

[GV3:P1434] History does not say that there were castes here a thousand years ago. All the castes seen today have come within the last thousand years. Before that, there were only the four varna castes of the Varnashrama here.

[GV3:P1435] The fundamental blunder we made was not understanding the glory of the Guru. In South Indian history, the matter of the Guru itself was forgotten. Apart from a few praises, we did not get an administrative familiarity of respecting and obeying the Guru. We did not get that in our family familiarity either. That is why the position of the Guru deteriorated. We suffered a lot of troubles due to the absence of the Guru tradition.

[GV3:P1436] The times when they were considered as untouchable castes are not small. This is where we made the first blunder. We brought a king here and made him rule. Subjects who grew up in such a country were not allowed to learn education. Not allowed to handle wealth. The administration did not assign education and wealth to those without them. Thus, the Shudra was trampled down and made to do very hard labor. The rulers who ate and enjoyed what they got and ruled with arrogance, along with some officials who took bribes and ate, created a path here for their use. They made them do all kinds of work. Engaged in hard and lowly labor, knowing nothing, a society grew up here like wild grass. At the same time, some people enjoyed themselves well. Under the cover of that enjoyment, the local rulers acquired the ability to do anything and destroy anything. The king needed the ability of these local rulers. He gave them positions and titles.

[GV3:P1437] They created a system that the untouchable castes need not know the Vedas, scriptures, and Upanishads. If they go out at dawn and work till dusk, they will get a measure (idangazhi) of paddy. They have to work for a measure of paddy. Not even for a measure of rice. From this measure of paddy, they would give some to the shop and buy salt or chili. The rest they would roast, pound, cook, drink it, and pass their time. The familiarity of wearing a good cloth came after a very long time. None of the workers had the ability to wear a dhoti worth more than ten chakras. If you look on a taluk basis, you wouldn't find even ten such families.

[GV3:P1438] During a time when we lead such a life, what kind of knowledge can we acquire? The inability to lead such a life was due to the flag-hoisted works of the kings. And some rich people who got used to sitting with their legs stretched out, indulging in all comforts to help the kings. It was they who destroyed these poor people by making them do such hard work. Accommodating these revelers, there were many people who led an exploitative life through that.

[GV3:P1439] It went from the rule of the Ettuveetil Pillamar to the rule of Marthanda Varma. During Marthanda Varma's reign, the story itself says that the heads of those who used intellect, logic, and knowledge were cut off. If they lived, the king's head would not be seen. We have administrative histories before us where the heads of the men from the Ettuveetil Pillamar families were cut off, and the women were caught and married off to fishermen.

[GV3:P1440] The government is the 'Ponnu Thampuran' (Golden Lord). It is said that the Ponnu Thampuran ruled and made everything flow here. But who did he make flow? He beat up and killed all the capable poor people here. The rest were ruled using force, just like the British government did. After ruling like this, the British came here and struck the caretakers with the butt of their guns. Then everything became alright here. The Vedic practitioners and godfathers here started to chant mantras and throw flowers. They chanted and threw flowers for many days. Thus, when they saw that the butt of the gun would climb on the backs of those who said the British could be plucked away, they wisely became their slaves. Not only did they become slaves, they also betrayed the country to them. Then they started paying tribute. There are records that the king here paid up to eight lakhs as tribute in those days. Yet, the royal rule went on like a kind of skin.

[GV3:P1441] The people who lived under this rule had no realization or wisdom about good and evil. It was in this context that the desire for freedom arose, and as a challenge to the British, a struggle was waged for about two hundred years, leading to the attainment of independence.

[GV3:P1442] When independence was achieved, a considerable amount of the culture of the British, Muslims, and Christians was imparted here. At the same time, the people who wielded the power of the Hindu administration, due to their loyalty and subservience to the West, cut up the very history of India and rewrote it, making it appear like a flashing torch. If you see a torch being spun in a circular motion from a distance, wouldn't it seem like a large fireball? In reality, it is not that big. Similarly, these historians could not understand the ancient history of the Hindus or the glories of that history. Therefore, the nation and politics ceased to exist here. Everything is like a mixed-up mess of coir pith. In this state, just as if the administration were handed over to an ignorant donkey, anyone can rule; they hand over the administration saying, "You rule too." That is why whoever gets their hands on power rules here.

[GV3:P1443] Even though you have done all this, God has deliberately brought about one thing. The author of the Gita says, "Sambhavami Yuge Yuge" (I incarnate from age to age). It means that what must happen in each age will happen in the world from time to time. The meaning of this is that if you do not accept it, you will be killed. Accepting it is the duty that people must perform with wisdom. That what must happen will happen is an unquestionable fact.

[GV3:P1444] Religions like Christianity, which is only two thousand years old, and Islam, which is fifteen hundred years old, present things by updating them according to the times. If you look at their history, you can understand that in the past, things were going on without any discipline or order.

[GV3:P1445] Because of the fault of the administration here not imparting knowledge about Gurus and Mahatmas, and the ignorance of the people who lived without education, wealth, and enlightenment, we do not know the Guru tradition. We don't even know our own mythological tradition. Complete slavery is what happened to us from this. At the same time, the age of mixing (Sankara Kala) prophesied by the author of the Gita has already happened here. Today, everything is mixed. There is hybridization in humans, birds, animals, and even plants. There is adulteration in fertilizer, milk, food items, and everything else. What is going to come next is a mixture of religions. When they see that they cannot win by fighting among themselves, a hybridization of religions will occur.

[GV3:P1446] If we are to know the Gurus, if we are to have love for them, the caste system that has destroyed us must leave our hands. When one has a love for caste, one becomes unable to absorb the glory of the Guru. In one part of the Manusmriti, it is stated that the Varnas of Mahatmas should not be asked. When Varnas are mentioned, it refers to Chaturvarnya. The Guru might be a Shudra. Still, the Varna should not be asked. It is said that the source of a river and the birthplace of a Mahatma should not be inquired into. But we begin to accept a Guru only after inquiring where he was born, whose son he is, and so on.

[GV3:P1447] When accepting a Guru, we should only take into account the Guru's glory. Otherwise, mistakes will happen. We have inherited the misfortune of not being able to know the Gurus completely or respectfully understand their state. To be freed from this misfortune, we must become somewhat aware. There is a part called the 'Guru's lie'. It is impossible for us to understand it. But we can see subtle explanations of it in the history of great men. If we look at the history of the Prophet, Jesus, Kabir Das, or Ramananda, we can see specific distinctive qualities in each of them. As for the Prophet, he lived like an ordinary man until the age of forty. The son of an ordinary carpenter who carved and sold idols. There is nothing special to say about the birth history of Jesus either. But how high they stand in history. Why? For example, the Prophet constantly received formless voices as truth. The words jotted down then and there became the scripture. In that lineage, a few Mahatmas and following them, some disciples came. They compiled the words jotted down by someone earlier and made a code. But later on, many fables crept into it. They have been creating histories in such a way that Muslims are depicted as the cause for all the transformations in the world. But surpassing this, the history of the Baha'is is also emerging. These two histories do not agree with each other, and competition is growing between them; Muslim countries are already fighting entirely among themselves. As the bond of religion tightens, fights increase. When the Pakistanis got the government in their hands, how many Mahatmas did they kill! They brutally tore apart and killed those who worked for the people and the country. The reason for this is the curse of the Prophet himself. The contemporaries of the Prophet could not see him in his true light. It is seen that the heads of seventy-three people, including his children and followers, were chopped off. That curse remains in them today. Whenever their administration comes into power, they chop down quite a few. The history of Iran and Iraq, as well as Pakistan and Bangladesh, are examples. They kill countlessly in all places. Only a little is known outside.

[GV3:P1448] Under Hindu rule in India, there has not been this much killing. However, it's not that there is none. We have the history of Mahatma Gandhi being shot dead and the history of great men being imprisoned while ruling. This destruction has affected us as a retaliation for the despicable acts we committed against Gurus and Mahatmas.

[GV3:P1449] The British ruled with a bit more diplomacy and deceptive methods. They brought their knowledge and wealth here, suited to their ways, and ruled for a long time. Thus, they established an administrative discipline among the people. But they carried away all the good things from here to turn their snow-filled country into a heavenly kingdom. Living subjected to all this, we could not see the glory of the Guru to its true merit.

[GV3:P1450] Kabir Das is the reincarnation of Suka Brahmarshi. He hadn't even learned a single letter. He accepted Ramananda, a wise man on the path of devotion with absolutely no supernatural powers, as his Guru. But even after giving Ramananda the merit and greatness to be the Guru of Kabir Das, he didn't know who he was or why he was here. Why did God bring him here without letting him know this! This is a divine decree. Only Kabir Das, who had no education or bookish knowledge to speak of, was able to reveal the Guru. Kabir Das was born a wise man due to the knowledge from his previous birth. We see Kabir Das as a wise man who knew what would happen and who everyone was. Calling him a 'Tulukka boy' (a derogatory term) by caste, all the Brahmins came out reciting and singing the Vedas in all possible ways. But with the arrival of the Siddha known as the protector of cows, all these Brahmins fled in fear. Even when he was a small child, they beat him up severely all over his body and threw Kabir Das out three or four times. Then, although he left the Gurukula, after serving the Guru and passing through all other states, Kabir Das returned. It was Kabir Das who showed the Guru who the Guru was and what the Guru's glory was.

[GV3:P1451] Ramananda didn't know who he was. This was a task set by God himself to prevent arrogance. Kabir Das, who came as the reincarnation of Suka Brahmarshi, said that even if all the water in the world's oceans were turned into ink and all the trees into pens to write, the glory of the Guru would still remain unwritten.

[GV3:P1452] What is the Guru's lie? How will we understand this Guru who is a 'thief'? Even though he had the recognition of a disciple, Ramananda beat and threw Kabir Das out many times. He was removed from the ashram as if he were killed. Yet, Kabir Das could not say a single word disrespecting his Guru. The reason is the soul's devotion to the Guru. It is difficult to get a disciple without deceit like this. It is also difficult to get a Guru without deceit.

[GV3:P1453] Let us assume we have obtained such Gurus. But how will we accept these Gurus? Among the Gurus themselves, from the Gurus who come every two thousand or five thousand years to the Gurus of an era, only five come in a Yuga.

[GV3:P1454] How will we understand people who come like Sri Krishna, the Prophet, or Sankaracharya? What they have all said in their respective languages is essentially the same in philosophical principle. We try to turn such Gurus into deities and hide behind the deity. This is the reason why Sankaracharya was confined to Tripura Sundari. It is a logic of the scholars. Even if they don't know, there is a pleasure in adding to it. When the scholars wrote it, the common people also believed it to be true.

[GV3:P1455] Only a Guru who imparts knowledge to a child can speak about the special nature of knowledge. How will disciples know about such a Guru, a Guru who came after two thousand or three thousand years? It is said that Agastya lived as a model for all Gurus. What attributes can be ascribed to such an Agastya? What attributes can be ascribed to Sankaracharya?

[GV3:P1456] What can be understood if it is said that a sage kicked Mahavishnu? Once a real human being becomes a sage, he will kick and spit on deities. Even if it is merely a story, it indicates an example. A sage is beyond the Trimurti (the trinity). A Sannyasi is beyond even that. The Guru is the one who goes back and returns.

[GV3:P1457] Any intellect born and raised in this world cannot understand a Guru. The reason is that this is a deception placed by God. Therefore, we cannot know that deception. That is why it is said that the Guru's deception cannot be known. It means there will be danger if the people standing near the Guru are not aware. It is of no use to say, "Our Guru is great, we are obedient," and so on. The Guru will only be of use to those who stay close, work, and try to humble themselves. So, if those standing near the Gurus do not have the intelligence, capability, and glory equal to the Guru, it is impossible to understand the Guru. Only a disciple with these qualifications can respect and know the Guru. The history of Kabir Das is proof of that. How many disciples stayed with Ramananda! How many people accepted discipleship and left! But no one was of any use, except Kabir Das.

[GV3:P1458] Do not think that you can know and believe in the Guru by yourself. You can believe to some extent by bringing it into thought through familiarity. Do not assume you can know the Gurus because it is written on paper. If the term Guru refers to a supernatural power assigned by nature, it cannot be known through written scriptures or poetry. Nothing to know the Guru is written anywhere. The Guru can only be known through experience. If one understands and acts upon what the Guru or great man coming for two thousand, three thousand, or five thousand years, or the Guru of an era tells you—that things will be like this, and you should only do such and such practices—you will experience that those words are the truth, that truth is the Guru, and the Guru is God.

[GV3:P1459] 2001 Birthday Edition (August)

[GV3:P1460] Liberation and Good Deeds

[GV3:P1461] People have written poems and stories praising the results of karma, going as deep as the depths of the universe, in such a way that it cannot be recognized. By combining these, they have created ritualistic hymns of praise, held onto them, performed penances, and even created theories to increase the qualities of that penance.

[GV3:P1462] Sita is the symbol of such a great truth. Is there any need to create a story of building a casket and hiding it? Similarly, the story fabricated about the birth of Lord Ayyappa of Sabarimala. You have no hesitation to tell your own children that your god and goddess were born through such a vile and despicable act. The poets and sycophants in the royal courts, who were the minions of the tyrannical rulers, are the ones who did this. If you cannot understand that such baseness has been spread throughout the world, in which part of the world were you born!

[GV3:P1463] I am expressing my sorrow. So many children have come to me. They have experiences too. Seeing the mother, father, children, husband, and wife, they see that all this is true. You must understand why they are shown like this. This is the mistake made by the lineages of Gurus that have come so far.

[GV3:P1464] What was caught in this mistake has been practiced as the dharma of the age. As it was practiced like that, it became three faces—Shakteyam, Shaivam, and Vaishnavam. Or Brahma, Vishnu, and Shiva. The fourth face is the face of knowledge (Jnana). That disappeared. Isn't this the karma that melted and perished entirely in the imaginary creations of these poets? What kind of work is this? You must fully understand this. This is an unhygienic and base moral sense that is unfit to be imparted to our homes, our lands, or our country. It has been passed down by all religious doctrines. India was the first to impart it. That took the form of caste, religion, and class. Due to this flaw of India, foreign countries are growing and hoisting their flags. You continue to follow right behind them.

[GV3:P1465] India, which had the wealth to rule the entire world, today suffers the misfortune of begging with a begging bowl before many. How long has it been suffering this!

[GV3:P1466] We know about the gem, adorned on Prasenajit's crown, set with gold and precious stones required for the livelihood of the people of so many countries. If this gem is used in this way, the place where it is used will end up in beggary. There has not been a personality to tell us about this ignorance.

[GV3:P1467] A Sannyasi is someone who should impart this knowledge appropriately at the right time. Instead, if one studies the Vedas and Shastras so much and fills pages with their branches, commentaries, and then Upanishads, calling it the truth, whom is it good for? It is completely plunging us into a pit of destruction. All this is written down and then it is said, 'None of this should be done; reject this, not this, not this, not this.' If you reject it like that, will your karma go away?

[GV3:P1468] We have with us the history of great souls who spoke the greatest Vedantas, learned habits accordingly, and repeatedly demonstrated those habits. If this karma is rejected, it will not go away. Or we might even assume that we have seen that this is not the truth. What is there to impart to the world, to take back and give as a gift to the children? We are praising and passing on the messy descriptions of ignorant logic, jumping around like fools without knowing any of this. Let us think like this: this story and play is an atrocity committed against the world.

[GV3:P1469] If a child rises in terms of vision, Sri Krishna is seen as having grown to the best level. It is said that two thousand four hundred and forty-four Gurus have come since Sri Krishna. If so many Gurus were to go astray, there must be a reason for it. What is it? No great soul came and told us this. What was the purpose of worshipping this devil and demon in such a way as to prove that the Kali Yuga is destructive? How do we remove this karmic flaw that has passed this massive destruction onto our children? Each of you must know this.

[GV3:P1470] As mentioned earlier, that mistake has happened even among the children who came to me. At the time of giving Sannyasa, I told some people, "You are not ready for it yet." But many insisted and told me that they had to do it. It was done. With that, it points towards the side of error. All these are the new clamors (Nava Kolahalams) of their homes. 'Nava Kolahalam' means the weight of the ruined lineage that is disrupted and praised under the influence of the nine planets (Navagrahas).

[GV3:P1471] How corrupt are you, who belong to this lineage! How painfully I made you experience the things that I have experienced. Every one of you is an embodiment of ignorance, not giving even an iota of consideration to that pain. Was it because of their flaws that Sri Krishna and Jesus were destroyed by this devil?

[GV3:P1472] From this position of ignorance, I am telling you about the aspect of truth gifted by God to reform each of you. This is something to be taken with so much difficulty. The ignorance that dragged us to the fort of destruction in the name of Puranas continues today just as it was in the beginning. This cannot be resolved even if you and the entire world think about it.

[GV3:P1473] There are quite a few people who have arrived here, knowingly or unknowingly. Many householders also live here. Because they acted without understanding this, they do not realize that it brought ruin to many. Similarly, some who live here as Sannyasis. They were born somewhere and grew up somewhere. Even before coming here, they were shown this truth. Having seen it like that, they have come here from the Aurobindo Ashram.

[GV3:P1474] Aurobindo Maharshi was the first to see about my birth. There is a part where Aurobindo speaks about Krishna. Those of you who have paid attention know that. You also know what happened when such a great Krishna came. It happened because of the ignorance of the Shudra class. When this Shudra was pushed towards doing whatever he wanted, he wore out and ceased to exist. God had even prepared royal duties (Rajakarma) for the Shudra. That is what was meant by saying 'Kali is with the Shudra'. This is not just singing a song. Along with fabricating Puranas and epics, there were some who spoke the truth. There were transformative changes in them in such a way that you couldn't approach them. Turning into a Sannyasi, Yogi, Karmi, and Jnani, and returning as Hindu, it was presented before us as Tantra, Mantra, and Yantra. How can this dressed-up karma be changed? The complete form of the tradition of this karmic flaw has now spread throughout the world.

[GV3:P1475] Instead of speaking about the Trimurtis (Trinity) in India, other religions have spoken of the Father, Son, and Holy Spirit. This has come under the cover of the Trimurti system. Blindly believing this, you have built a fort of destruction and passed it on to your children. You need to be a little more careful about this.

[GV3:P1476] After Sri Krishna came Moses. Buddha came. Thirty-seven prophets came in the lineage of Jesus. When it came to the lineage of Islam, it became twenty-five. It can also be said as thirty-one. Despite so many prophets coming, they haven't been able to correct us. This is my sorrow. I have that same sorrow about you too. I have tried with my life to remove the legacy of this karmic flaw present in you. You are unable to respect it.

[GV3:P1477] That is why we perform the ritual of Guru Pooja by God's command. Such a Guru Pooja is not taking place, nor has it ever taken place, anywhere in the world, let alone in India. Through this Guru Pooja, in the manner of 'Sankalpa Matrena Parameswarah' (By mere thought, He is the Supreme Lord), we are grasping all concepts that reach up to the state of the Trimurtis. We are taking hold of the ancestors and the worshipped figures of people of any religion. Then, we are liberating them, placing them in dharma, and setting them in karma in a way that is beneficial to you. They will take birth in your generations. What kind of gratitude can we show to God, who has given us the opportunity to undertake and perform such a great task!

[GV3:P1478] I am telling you the weight of my pain. There was someone here who was the secretary for nine years. It wasn't because he didn't have knowledge, nor because he didn't know the logic, that he couldn't grasp this. Similarly, some children who rose up and grew like some Karma Siddhantis who reached the status of the Trimurtis stood by me. Both men and women. They all left as if they flew and drifted away.

[GV3:P1479] If I don't tell you this openly, you will ask why I didn't tell you such a big thing openly. I am telling you openly. The problem is you don't understand the language I speak. There is a reason to say this openly again. For at least the next two generations to come, our girls need to come to the very top. Only after that will the boys—whether small or big—come forward. What God has gifted for them is different. What I have to say to the householders is this: You must take up the ownership of the five dharmas given to you, while maintaining dharma through yourselves, to give birth to those who went astray as Sannyasis, Brahmacharis, and Yogis. By undertaking these five dharmas—Grihadharma, Pitrudharma, Danadharma, and Devadharma—you will transform into the worshipped figures of the coming Satya Yuga.

[GV3:P1480] You haven't thought until today what the knowledge 'Atmanatmaniveditam' mentioned by the author of the Gita is. It is discerning the soul through the soul. What is my life, what are my ancestors, what are my Gurus, what are my prophets, who are the bearers of the Vedas that I have recognized as the bearers of the Vedas, what is that dharma, what is karmic flaw, what rituals are there in the merit called dharma, what blunders have been mixed up in it? You yourselves must know all this. This is the truth that God has gifted you.

[GV3:P1481] No one who has been with me for this long has been able to think of this or achieve it. I don't know if it's my fault or theirs. I am teaching them. If you make a mistake by even a single word or a single letter, you will fall into a deep pit far away. What is this deep pit? Until the Kali Yuga of the next Chaturyuga. For that long, you will suffer, heat up with the heat of that pain, and all this heat will remain as the pollution of the atmosphere and then begin to smoke out and kill everyone. This is the science you are dealing with. How much you toil for this! How much destruction you are trying to serve! To whom are you committing this karmic flaw!

[GV3:P1482] I am teaching the children and even the little ones who come here as much as I can. But they are not doing it. The children of those who come and stay here themselves are becoming an axe for me. This is my experience. What will I do with the children who come making this axe? This is part of my sorrow. I had to call four or five people who are trying to strengthen the organization by doing something here and make arrangements to give one standing here what he needs and send him away. You please forgive him. Let him also forgive. That is the extent of my sorrow. You all pray together for this. Let's try praying. You must pray for this mistake to go away for your sake and for the children to come. This devil has caught them and is spinning them. They are looking to make mistakes. They will enter through that mistake. If there is a movement through a word or a look, he will enter through that movement.

[GV3:P1483] Let me give an example of that. A devil named Gandharvan had come here. What have you heard about the Gandharvan? A great karmic sinner who, like Hanuman, made the Trimurtis helpless. By whatever means you paralyze him—whether through mantra, tantra, or sheer will—he will just lie there. There are many here who have seen this. His tongue won't lift, his eyes can't see. Yet, he will gesture to bring water. When water is brought, he will ask to be supported and lifted up. He is lifted up not to drink water. He will drain the energy of anyone who touches his body, just like an electric shock. This happened here. What can I say to you who do not understand even after seeing this?

[GV3:P1484] Your ignorance is that great. Due to an excess of knowledge, there are families who make a cat's head out of silver and worship it. The devil plagued by karmic flaws stood to ruin us in this state, and this devil spread into each of the children and ruined them. That is why I know that those who come here and those who stay here become like this. It is when my word is broken that this devil gets a place to enter. Otherwise, it will not enter those who stay inside this. What will I do with you, who have decided that it's enough to lay your children down like this without giving respect to the word? What should I tell you, who show the complete form of karma of the special rule? If you make mistakes in order to destroy this aspect that God has given for me, for you, and for the world, what will I do?

[GV3:P1485] It was in this state that Sri Rama stepped into the Sarayu river. What is said about Rama Tirtha is also the same. No greater yogis than Rama Tirtha are to come. When speaking of Rama Tirtha, if Vivekananda was someone who saw the flower, Rama Tirtha was someone who experienced it. Even such a great man had to step into the water. If you have even a little love for your children, you should not act in this manner. Dharmic flaws and similarly karmic flaws will affect you. I have explained everything to you to make you understand. I have not understood you by showing supernatural powers (Siddhi). It is to bring about meritorious karma through you, capable of sustaining dharma, through experience and realization, that I am suffering this much. You are doing a great harm not only to me but to all these past great men who are waiting for us. Every word and movement of yours, which gains intelligence and power, stands as atmospheric pollution and sows destruction. I have not harmed any of you through any karma. Then why, father, did this happen to me!

[GV3:P1486] God often gave us opportunities to remove this flaw—by making others speak and by speaking directly. It hasn't been achieved. You were called to pray from 5 o'clock for this. There is no use praying without saying this. Understanding this, you must pray with a painful heart that we own nothing in this world, and if we do, it belongs to the Guru.

[GV3:P1487] I reminded you in many ways about the causes of errors. Everything in the past became a muddled mess—karma, yoga, jnana, and bhakti. They lack self-knowledge (Atmajnana). They pinched off the fourth face. Do you think we will get any good from this sense of dharma or capability of karma, which was practiced so perversely and corrupted us all?

[GV3:P1488] Guruvani

[GV3:P1489] Santhigiri Janmadina Poojitha Samarpana Pathippu - 2001

[GV3:P1490] Historical Truth Through Experience

[GV3:P1491] What is the reason for so many Buddhists being in Kerala? The Buddhists here are the local people themselves. Cherthala Pooram, Kodungallur Pooram, Thrissur Pooram—all these are festivals celebrated by dividing classes and invoking caste. When caste was invoked like this, a king without caste came here. That is Buddha. Even when Buddha came, we did not take up that karmic capacity. We rejected it by saying it has no karma.

[GV3:P1492] Why was it said that Buddhism has no karma? The karma that has been moving around starting from a small creature is lying here. People cannot cross this and move forward. Or it will take countless ages to cross it. That is why it was taught that there is no karma. When Buddhism arrived, they accepted Buddhism. After accepting Buddhism, they brought in Mahayana and Hinayana, and strengthened Hinayana.

[GV3:P1493] Why was Hinayana brought in? One who does not drink Sura (liquor) is an Asura. Or one who does not accept the leftovers of a Yaga. One who accepts liquor and Yaga leftovers is a Sura. The South Indian was depicted as a monkey merely because he worshipped Rama. This is something that happened seventeen lakh years ago. Because you didn't understand why he did this, you assumed all sorts of things.

[GV3:P1494] What is the reason for such a notion to arise? Most of you did not accept mantra, tantra, and yantra. Furthermore, they turned the Illathu Nairs and others into their servants. This was brought about a very long time ago. However, God's intention is that this must change.

[GV3:P1495] God's intention means Brahma's intention (Brahmasankalpam). Even in that Brahmasankalpam, fourteen Manvantaras have occurred. Of those, the seventh Manvantara is currently taking place. In it, six Manvantaras and three Chaturyugas proceeded correctly. After three Chaturyugas, it went wrong. It was after that the Trimurti system came. In the Trimurti system, the Brahma Sutra was attributed to Brahma. That is, the position of Brahman was given to Brahma. When Brahma became Brahman, they became Brahma, Vishnu, and Maheshwara. Thus, history was fabricated. The Shudra who followed this history could not move forward in any way. Because of that, he became unable to act according to dharma. This became the cause for the emergence of countless castes in India. Because caste stood strong, even though Buddha came, it didn't work out. After that, the Mughals and the British entered here. With that, India was torn apart, spinning around in different garbs, languages, and cultures, and stood still.

[GV3:P1496] God brought the English to bring a change to this situation. So what did this Shudra do? He started acting in the ways of the English. Because of that, their homes were ruined. Father, mother, wife, husband, and children were all in foreign lands. But one part of the house could be seen hanging loosely.

[GV3:P1497] It was mentioned earlier that those who did not drink Somarasa became Asuras. Those who belonged to this Asura class were not considered as the people of this land. When that happened, all the prosperous men and women belonging to the Asura class ended up in places like Coorg and Singapore. These are the people with wealth and capacity in the land. When they found their own jobs there and came back, some stayed here. This is the history. Instead of this, history has been written in reverse.

[GV3:P1498] Buddhism turned into Hinayana and Mahayana. The Hinayana people all became lower castes (Heenajathikar). The Mahayana people became Brahmins. The Konkani, who was the original Brahmin here, ended up having no place here, having no language. They went to Goa. When foreign rule came there, they returned to India again. That is why they scattered here and there.

[GV3:P1499] When the dharma of Kali arrived, the Brahmasankalpam that the Shudra should get ruling power grew strong. That determination was the reason for Sri Krishna's birth. Even before Krishna was born, an incorporeal voice (Ashariri) began to torment Kamsa. When Kamsa ignored it, it said that someone would be born in his own house to kill him. That is how he beat seven of them to death. He didn't get the eighth one. Although Sri Krishna lived, while he lived, he was constantly chased around India. Krishna continued this running until there were only three days left for the Dwapara Yuga to end. Who saw that sorrow?

[GV3:P1500] Who in the world has been born as anything other than a Shudra? Who is a Brahmin?

[GV3:P1501] Brahmin, Kshatriya, Vaishya, Shudra—this is the Varna caste. The special attribute of Kali, the fourth quarter, is with the Shudra. Because he didn't understand this, the Shudra doesn't fit in anywhere. How to raise this Shudra? Is it enough to give money, capacity, and education? No. If the Shudra is to grow, he must get the administration. Several Chaturyugas have passed without getting that administration. At the end of this Dwapara, that divine decree decided to give birth to Sri Krishna directly. You could not accept Sri Krishna, who was born a wise man from birth. Should we accept Sri Krishna by offering poojas? You should introspect and accept how you got this karma, being the author of the Gita. It came through the Shudra lineage. That is why the Upanishads, which summarized the history of India, even taking the history of Sri Krishna, were created. This caused a downfall for a very long time.

[GV3:P1502] Ananthankadu is to correct this downfall. An endless forest (Ananthamaya kadu)! It would be good to understand something like this. It was from Ananthankadu that the rule of the Shudra turned. When this Shudra supported others, he ended up as a servant to the Illam. When writing the deed of a property, don't they write 'Pandarappattam'—meaning it belonged to someone in the past. 'Bhandaram' also means an offering (Kanikka). That means today's offering belonged to someone in the past. We must see through historical creation whether this is true. It cannot be just made up and said.

[GV3:P1503] We do not know the real history. The reason for that is that they scribbled some things somewhere and then turned it into illusion (Maya) and made it disappear. How many great souls pointed this out! It was understanding this that Chattampi Swami wrote 'Vedadhikara Nirupanam'. But who wanted this 'Aadu Kunjan Pillai' back then! The name the locals used to call Chattampi Swami was Aadu Kunjan Pillai. While walking in the forest, he would pick some medicinal herbs and chew them. These Gramappacha and Lakshnappacha are good for hunger and thirst. They have a slight sweetness too. Because he plucked and ate them, he became Aadu Kunjan Pillai. Because you didn't understand all this and judged him like this, you missed the truth and didn't even realize that the world's history was rewritten.

[GV3:P1504] I am shedding light on a history for you by showing the history that has been written in that manner so far. A historian can view this in that way.

[GV3:P1505] If you look at the Chekava history, you can understand that at that time the Ezhavas and Nairs were not two different entities. The great men who wrote awakening texts knew that. They wrote books like Vedadhikara Nirupanam for that purpose. It didn't work. After that, they placed Sri Shankara and Sree Narayana Guru on the left and right. Still, they didn't understand. That's why they fought each other and perished.

[GV3:P1506] Buddhists are independent people. When they lined up behind Buddha's ideas, they were beaten and chased away. Thus, they fled to places like Ceylon, China, and Japan. Those who lived virtuously here and those who gained literacy were the ones mostly beaten and chased away. The reason is that they couldn't be subjugated as obedient servants. Unable to stay, the virtuous, those with money, capacity, and education, ran away. What remained were the poor. They were not even considered human beings. Then only a group called Vaishyas remained here.

[GV3:P1507] If you look at the ancestors from a thousand years ago, you can understand that Ezhavas and Nairs were not two different communities. That is how I understood it. All their ancestors from a thousand years ago are alike. As time passed, they lost the Pitruyana (path of the ancestors). Therefore, they went along with the logic of the Mahayana followers.

[GV3:P1508] Veda is what was known by hearing it as Shruti. That Veda is what they are now fabricating and parading around. How can you get anything good out of this fabrication? There is no religion called Hindu. There is only the Sanatana religion. The Sanatana religion has no caste or religion.

[GV3:P1509] To empower the Shudra according to the dharma of Kali, two thousand four hundred and forty-four Acharyas were born in the Shudra class. But the Shudra did not know that. We must see who the Shudra became when the wheel of time turned.

[GV3:P1510] In Chempazhanthy, the temples of the Nairs and Ezhavas were situated in one compound. The elders of both communities used to sit together and decide on the festival. The history of Chempazhanthy Pillai is tied to this. As history was continuously written, Chempazhanthy Pillai eventually disappeared from history. After that came the history of Pothencode. A large forest. A forest where buffaloes (Pothu) could be grazed.

[GV3:P1511] How might Pothencode have been quite a while ago? Back then, a man would climb up here with a broken sword. And he would tell the story of a Pillai Ammavan (uncle). He is talking about someone who went from here as a Sannyasi. But they didn't go; they were all beaten and chased away from here. Then women were caught and given to fishermen. They are the Araya community seen in the Alappuzha area. You can tell by looking at their descendants; they are all fair-skinned. The Chempil Arayan belongs to this group. The reason for all this happening is that God's plan did not take place.

[GV3:P1512] This is no one's fault; history was written wrong. A king should only rule by looking at history. Similarly, we also failed to know our history. The reason is that we were not given education. Circumstances existed here where education was taught only inside the temple. When such conditions arose, Buddhism took root here.

[GV3:P1513] Guruvani

[GV3:P1514] Santhigiri Janmadina Poojitha Samarpana Pathippu - 2001

[GV3:P1515] How to Lead Life

[GV3:P1516] One should strive to bring about a change in the mind day by day. One must bring as much of that change into action as possible. This is how patience is developed. Once you come to the ashram and see the Guru and the atmosphere here, you should learn how to act in a conditional system (Upadhi Sampradayam).

[GV3:P1517] If you start believing in the Guru, you must believe unconditionally. You should believe whether you know what the Guru is or not. The love one has for the Guru should shine through humility. One's love will depend on one's logic, fortune, and capability. What is capability? Capability is the depth of love that makes someone a good person and brings them closer to us.

[GV3:P1518] If a small change occurs in our mind, we should not take back the old shortcomings. For that, it is enough not to desire and pray for anything. The benefit I receive must be useful to everyone through me. "O Guru, please reform me. Along with that, reform my family and my people." If that love gets confined to oneself and becomes devotion, one will not feel like talking to anyone. In this state, our most wicked tendencies might be stirred. Then we will have to beg and cry in pain.

[GV3:P1519] We must have the humility to feel, "I have done very little." Only then can devotion be sustained through love. To experience the coolness of devotion, one must see the Guru as the Supreme Brahman and love him in that state. Everything must unravel for that. Thus, one should acquire intellect for intellect, love for love, and humility for humility.

[GV3:P1520] If one has the merits of past lives (Janmanthara Sukrutam), one will have an experience as soon as they see the Guru. One will naturally understand that this happens because of the Guru's complete love. Then, the love for what is seen and subsequently the humility will keep coming to us without our knowledge. The sacrifice for attaining God will arise. That sacrifice will manifest outwardly as sweet speech.

[GV3:P1521] Know the Guru first. Then know the organization. Do not destroy the merit earned through word and deed. After that, look for ways to increase merit. The Christian says that one-tenth must be given to the church. It is difficult to get an ashram like ours. A place of realization is very rare. It is the greatest fortune in life to be able to know such a Guru. If one gets the Guru, one can be fearless of evil.

[GV3:P1522] In my childhood, I had deep devotion. But I never met a good person. Even though I walked very carefully without committing any wrong, I couldn't get an experience like you have. Could there be a greater fortune if you recognize this carefully and act upon it? When I was in Sivagiri and its branches, I saw and talked to many saints, but I got nothing from it. It was at the age of twenty-five that I received the Guru's advice. That advice was proven true within three months. And after another two and a half years, I had a vision. In that case, how lucky you are. Because of you, not only you or your family but the world is benefiting.

[GV3:P1523] The mind should have slavery only to God. One must be able to think deeply about what is seen today and know it clearly within six months. Within three years, a character should develop that can clearly tell about the past, present, and future of any person and country. If you study about yourself and what you get, you will be able to know who you are within six months. If you give yourself dignity, many things will happen through you. Within a year, people will begin to say that many things happening in the world are happening through you. One should think about how to instill the love of God in these blinded people. It is enough if a person develops the habit of thinking, everyone will become straight. Nobody will do wrong. The evil ones will not be able to set foot here.

[GV3:P1524] Unbroken love, wherever it is, is the love of God. That love is eternal. Where can you find love that is ready to bear and resolve the wrongs of another by risking its own life! In this place filled with such love, not a single mistake should come from you. If it does, you will perish along with your family. Then I won't be able to do anything. The first word given to you is not to make a mistake, isn't it? When pain comes, the firmness of love and trust should not go down the drain. Pain belongs to the world. Even if it is a thorn, hold on tight. If you understand this love and consider it trivial, it cannot be said where you will end up. If you become good, this place will become good on its own. If you don't stand strong, the harm caused by this cannot be resolved by anyone. Here you can do what you want. But you must remember one thing. You come here to surrender. You should not tell stories here. Your home will be ruined. Do not try to justify holding this experience in your hand. I have come for one thing. I will bear any sacrifice for it. Even if the body is cut into pieces, I will do this. You must stand strong by handling it with humility and truth. If this humility reaches the truth, the truth will reach the Guru. The Guru will reach God.

[GV3:P1525] The Guru is equal to God. Guru and God are not different. They are the same. The Guru is the only medium to reach God. What is the true nature of the Guru? The Guru is an indefinable lightness. Because the Guru's light stands in the five elements. The five senses are formed by its transformation. What shines through the five elements becomes knowledge through the five senses. That knowledge operates in the Annamaya Kosha, Pranamaya Kosha, Jnanamaya Kosha (Manomaya Kosha), Vijnanamaya Kosha, and Anandamaya Kosha. Everything in the world is created with the Annamaya Kosha. Without the proximity of Prana, the Annamaya Kosha will not grow. Then the light goes out in both the Annamaya and Pranamaya Koshas. In the Manomaya Kosha, everything became recognition in the part known as the knowledge of the mind. The Vijnanamaya Kosha specifically understands that recognition. Its fulfillment is in the Anandamaya Kosha. Then nothing remains but bliss. That bliss is the bliss of life.
