[GV3:P1700] A long time ago, we fell into a dharma where we didn't know how to live and act according to Yuga Dharma. That dharma made us bad. This is the reason.

[GV3:P1701] In Kali, the dharma of Kali must be practiced. Saying Kali is bad, saying we will come after Kali ends, can we go anywhere? Then what will we do? If there is bad in Kali, how will we change that bad? Do we need some tool for that? We don't have a lack of guidance. Many great souls came among us. During their lifetime, did we ever peek to see if their guidance was good or not? Or if we did peek, we contained it in a call 'Oh God!'. Calling like that, we ended our life and health. What is left? Only bones, skin, and phlegm. Does this tool for change come just by acquiring qualification through talking big?

[GV3:P1702] This mistake happened many crores of years ago. We don't know this. If we want to withdraw from this karma, we must know what happened in the Yuga Dharmas. This cannot be known through a scholar or a theory. It was said earlier that this mistake lies within ourselves. Apart from the fact that there is a greater or lesser degree of this mistake, does anyone have more possibility towards the right? Is it in our own experience? Can we get it by studying Puranas and Epics? Can we get it by chanting all the Vedas differently?

[GV3:P1703] We have no clue about this. All we have heard is that Kali is bad. Aren't we born in Kali? If we say Kali is bad, where will we go? This has become a word that confuses us. Kali is not meant to make man or dharma bad. The dharma of Kali has come to show opposition to all this beggarly behavior, change everything by tying torches to them, stir and clear everything, and finally pull away this slavery. Some will say according to their ability that this is a karma suitable for our thinking habits. Who gave this bad? If so, shouldn't that thought have been acquired by following the path it took since childhood?

[GV3:P1704] A karmic error that failed to justify the dharma of the Yuga has lost this merit accumulation. There is no use in any of us standing aside and talking. Understanding like this, we should seek. If we seek without understanding this, we can seek until we suffocate, but there is no use.

[GV3:P1705] The author of the Gita says that change comes through quality and duty. If so, how did we acquire that quality? There is a reason for it, when I looked for where it is, I am telling the knowledge shown to me.

[GV3:P1706] How many Gurus came in each Yuga Dharma? In what all ways did they practice? What is the reason for it to come so? What happened to the special qualities that came and went in a Manvantara? We cannot move forward without looking at all this. With what standard will this be checked?

[GV3:P1707] We have a knowledge while we are alive. How will we acquire and go beyond this knowledge? Without seeing its end and boundary, running helter-skelter, won't we fall before the run ends? This is where we have lost. The reason for that is our failure in karma. We don't even have the opportunity to think where this failure happened.

[GV3:P1708] In our discoveries, what we see as the biggest is science and other related things. Does it say in which period discovery started according to modern science? For that, whose side was used from whom? Only if such a thing is trained, a lightness can be given to it. But we have a predicament where we cannot give such a lightness. That is a nature that is not understood even if understood. Someone has to tell us this for us to understand. Otherwise, it will not be understood.

[GV3:P1709] As far as our aeronautical science is concerned, doing, seeing, and acting on each, it has reached a nature of saying how that knowledge can be linked to another and done. There was a time when the metal used for this was discovered. What does modern science say about that time? How has it been studied and evaluated in this twentieth century or in the times before that? From where, when, and how was it discovered?

[GV3:P1710] You say that there was a glass here that would not break if shot at. From the time it was discovered, it has reached the third level of its evolution today. Even if three, four, or ten levels pass, it will not finish evolving. It will just go a little faster, but modern science cannot state the original karmic path of this.

[GV3:P1711] This is the proper study regarding a subject. A karma that stands at our scientific and progressive side. There was a root cause for this. That root cause originated in India. Why did it slip away from India? The Westerners who acquired this will not say this. If so, their greatness cannot be tied to our shoulders. But we don't have the original text of that science in our hands. Why did this happen?

[GV3:P1712] These Gurus who came and went could not tell us that Yuga Dharma has a dignity and that it should be respected in such a way. That is their mistake indeed. Aeronautical science is right for this time. If this science was brought and placed at the beginning of Yuga Dharma, could anyone take it? That was a time when people lived truthfully. They don't need any of this. That is the thing we are not told. From mother, father, Guru, or which place will we seek this knowledge? This is what was said, this mistake came from the Gurus. What is that mistake like? They didn't teach us that in the nature of Manvantara.

[GV3:P1713] What is Manu? What is Manvantara? How many Chaturyugas in a Manvantara? What is the mistake that came in each Chaturyuga? This is what should come from the Guru. We have not thought, nor have we been made to think, about the quality of the life that should stand attaining dharma in the theory of karma. Those who have seen this special quality have also said that it exists.

In Karunagappally and Karthikappally, there is a group called Thirumeni. South Travancore folks call this Thirumeni 'Potti' too. They don't have any such greatness.

[GV3:P1723] In my native place, Cherthala, they place one hundred and eight leaves for God. One leaf for each demon. So the more demons there are, the bigger it gets. Do we have any other story to tell today other than this story of caste? How can we understand the mistake beyond Chaturyugas from this story?

[GV3:P1725] Santhigiri Birthday Poojitha Dedication Edition - 2001

[GV3:P1726] The Truth the Disciple Should Know

[GV3:P1727] Even a small child should not be shown the state of the Guru as the Guru. That should go only by Brahman's will. It should not go according to the Guru's wish. The Guru should act only as an instrument. It is Brahman's will that shows who the Guru sitting in that instrument is. Otherwise, one shouldn't say the Guru showed it. This matter is not in any of our theories either. It is with this precaution that it is said one shouldn't show miraculous powers (Siddhi). Siddhi is an item kept by the person who shows it.

[GV3:P1728] Disciple: There are some distances we cannot reach. It may take lifetimes and states to reach. I don't think we have huge flaws. If we had such flaws, we wouldn't have been fortunate to see you. When there is a Guru like you, suffering the agony of further lifetimes, perhaps even greater agony...! When you are here, should you subjugate us to this time duration and this agony? Even if we have unsuitability as a vessel (Patradosha), can't that flaw be remedied and we be brought to that state?

[GV3:P1729] Guru: I have called and shown some people before this. They are not ones with vessel purity (Patrashuddhi). You can see a possibility there. When looking at parents who do not even know the lifestyle for vessel purity and their lineage, it is never a possibility they can even peek into. I have also mentioned its special nature.

[GV3:P1730] The karmic path shown by Narayana Guru Swami, Sri Ramakrishna, Vivekananda, and Sri Shankara is the model of Puranas and Epics. As for me, I am not someone who came after studying what Epics are or what resides in the greatness of Puranas. Therefore, there is no need to see me in such a nature. Then why was it seen like this? The biggest flaw I see is that there was no individual near me with the capacity to seek in this state. It is not I who should make myself heard. The responsibility to tell and make me known lies with the disciple nature. Or the followers. Then, regarding gotra-based understanding, we are also unfamiliar with a gotra like Parashurama gotra, Vishwamitra gotra, etc. We have never peeked into the karma called Gotra purity (Gotrashuddhi).

[GV3:P1731] The cause of Gotra purity is the life of a man and a woman together. Those who have come to me don't even know what a gotra is. They don't know what the traditional Guru path is. They don't know what a visionary is. Along with knowing this, it is better to know that there are different Guru paths.

[GV3:P1732] Now we have an understanding reached in Shaiva, Shakta, Vaishnava - or the Trimurti tradition. If it can be said that there is a class in gotra nature in this, it is only the Shaiva. In a small nature, the Vaishnava too. However, the capability that fulfills all three in gotra nature is what is called disciple wealth (Sishya Sampath).

[GV3:P1733] Disciple: Isn't the wealth of the disciple the Guru?

[GV3:P1734] Guru: For that, the disciple's vessel is not right. That is the mistake that happened to Jesus and likewise all Gurus who have come till today. That is the same mistake that happened regarding me.

[GV3:P1735] Disciple: If the vessel becomes pure, can mistakes be rectified?

[GV3:P1736] Guru: Divine will should also come with it.

[GV3:P1737] Disciple: Divine will! That is true indeed. But as far as the disciple is concerned, the Guru is God.

[GV3:P1738] Guru: We say Guru is God. When the word is guarded, when that guarding comes in his long-term experience, he unknowingly accepts the Guru as God. When the Guru crosses passing states, he becomes God.

[GV3:P1739] Disciple: The passing state where the devotee calls the Guru God?

[GV3:P1740] Guru: Yes.

[GV3:P1741] Disciple: Can the Guru become God only to the devotee?

[GV3:P1742] Guru: When saying devotee, what did you understand? A devotee is one who has no hesitation towards the Guru. We saw, prayed, showed humility - this can be called the sign of devotion. That doesn't become devotion. Devotion is a science of silence. Devotion is a dharma of sitting like that, thinking deeply, and acting with humility. A devotee will never see the Guru as a stranger. Seeing the Guru like himself and a little more, uniting in the humility arising from that karma and the truth arising from humility is a devotee. Not everyone can become a devotee. But the special thing here is that the truth called Guru has been shown to those who came from families without even such a thought. This is what is called divine will. But the predicament of not being able to take it back even after seeing this is what I have seen till today among those who stood with me.

[GV3:P1743] Disciple: Guru, what is the instrument to spread this idea in the world?

[GV3:P1744] Guru: Nothing creating miracles should be allowed. When you get familiar and used to it in your experienced story or knowledge, you carry the purity for it yourself. That is how this should be propagated. They should do it themselves through their intellect. The Guru should not dictate it. The visionary aspect should not be told in experiences. It can be said in another way. It should only be told in the model of the disciple's intellect. Do not tell it by astonishing. If told by astonishing, the Guru and disciple will separate.

[GV3:P1745] Till today, the karma that occurred as a Guru, prophet, Siddha, or sage has been said that he showed this miracle. They have seen that as divinity. The person who comes as God's representative should not show miracles. Why a miracle for him? In your view, there will be room for miracle in it. That miracle happens by itself. If you don't go on understanding like this, if you don't value the sight you see, if you don't value the knowledge you gain, you will slowly drift away and become distant. Moreover, you will reach the path of violence like the disciple of Jesus. No matter how long one has been familiar, that will happen. There is plenty of experience for that here. How many people have gone astray from here because they didn't know this path. They have experience, an experience they had never thought of. Magic they never knew would happen like this in the world. It is those who knew all this who went astray.

[GV3:P1746] Disciple: Can those who knew this much leave?

[GV3:P1747] Guru: They can. Why did you, sir, come to me without wearing a shirt or pants?

[GV3:P1748] Disciple: I didn't feel like it.

[GV3:P1749] Guru: You didn't feel like it. This sat in the depth of the mind. This must be understood. This is beginningless knowledge. What is that karmic path which thought that even if the body had a predicament, it could be handled sitting on the floor? It is the purity of purpose that sat deep. The depth of Guru-disciple relationship should be seen like this. Apart from all this, there is the story of asking, saying, and earning respect. This is the karma that comes through services. Another is, when someone else says according to our knowledge, so-and-so is saying like this, asking the Guru to know why that is. This is special intellect. Those without this familiarity get many logics and liberation thoughts and waste time asking many things to show perfection in a subject they don't know.

[GV3:P1750] Disciple: When I heard that those with such vision have left, I feel afraid about myself.

[GV3:P1751] Guru: Fear is definitely needed.

[GV3:P1752] Disciple: If those with vision felt like leaving... I have a fear like self-torture.

[GV3:P1753] Guru: That should be there. Most people are not capable of having it. They have acquired a knowledge that has become qualified through familiarity in education, wealth, lordship, and then knowledge. It is difficult for them to crumble down from that. For example, some writers, then those who have taken six or seven degrees in various subjects - they all live only according to their ideas. I am not talking about such greatness. If one says they believe in the truth residing in a small grain of sand, a large piece of iron, and the soil where the five elements spring, but lives giving priority to this learned karma, what to do? There is nothing to say right or wrong about them. They have gone according to their knowledge.

[GV3:P1754] Disciple: When talking about knowledge, there are many knowledges. It is difficult to identify which one is the knowledge that gives guidance among these.

[GV3:P1755] Guru: Yes. Each of us has the knowledge called 'I' or 'we'. When saying 'I', it is said keeping it aside as an isolated individual. When saying 'we', it can be two, if needed, more than that. But it is none of this. When saying 'we', we should have a special understanding. It is not alien. There is change only for the life (Jiva) residing in this bodily structure made of the five elements. It is the state of the life that reaches the place of knowledge. The rest may learn and accumulate many things. Regarding a scholar, he might have swallowed a whole library. But that is not this said knowledge. We cannot say it is not knowledge either. We need not say anything more than this, right?

[GV3:P1756] Disciple: You gave a great knowledge. The vision you gave about Kali Yuga has not been heard from anyone till now. I am hearing for the first time that Kali Yuga can remove the collapse that came in other Yugas and create a vision like self-realization, and perhaps that vision will be helpful to go to Satya Yuga. I understood that a Guru is needed to take from Kali Yuga to Satya Yuga. You explained from the time of Sri Krishna. You also explained about the Gurus who came after Sri Krishna. You explained Madhva's Dvaita, Sri Shankara's Advaita, and Sri Narayana Guru's acceptance of Advaita. But the assurance that what Kali Yuga needs is our complete background liberation, and that it is possible, is a relief.

[GV3:P1757] Guru: That is beyond our intellect. Only through oldness can one determine the Guru. Without determining like that, they also say 'my Guru'. We shouldn't talk about miraculous events or those belonging to scientific methods. The scientific method is a karmic theory that is being discovered today and tomorrow. It is just spoken in many languages. It also speaks about the path it travels in many ways. That is science. But the science of this is not like that. It is the science about the evolution of life. The science that becomes clear in the disciple's path of experience.

[GV3:P1758] Disciple: So that which is called scientific and the physical science called science are two different things?

[GV3:P1759] Guru: Certainly. This science will start explaining from the very beginning up to where it has discovered. Science is not like that. It just keeps describing only what it saw. This is not something I can explain to you, sir, who knows the German language. Just because you acquired that language and scholarship in it doesn't mean you will get this scientific knowledge.

[GV3:P1760] Disciple: After his biggest discovery, a scientist said, although what I saw is very big, I know it is very very small. When I struggled this much for this effort, how much should one struggle for this big thing! The doubt about the astonishingly big was removed for me when I heard your words. More astonishing than that in the big is the humility of its beautiful discipline. So Einstein was actually accepting spiritual scientificity. If there had been a good disciple like that scientist, there would have been no gap in the analyses of science and spiritual science.

[GV3:P1761] Guru: There can be two reasons for not joining like that. One, it will not join due to the Guru's lack of qualification. Two, it will not join due to the disciple's lack of qualification. It is the disciple who determines the Guru's qualification. The absence of a conclusion and a vessel for that qualification was the reason for Jesus' death at that time. Many people stood with me. Those who were considered the wealth of disciples for ten, twenty, and thirty years. They moved out and destroyed it mentally and naturally according to the size of their intellect. This is the lack of knowledge of his soul.

[GV3:P1762] A person we once loved may hurt us for a long time. That is a matter related to knowledge and culture. This is its essence: this is God's wish. What is the reason for God to have such a wish?

[GV3:P1763] Everyone will say there is no caste, no religion, and so on. Then why don't you become people without them? You do not have the course of dharma or the karma for that. This flaw in dharma turns into a flaw in karma, losing humility and respect, and you stand merged in seeing the political peaks that maintain a special dharma suitable for a cruel regime. You only need a single mind to know and respect that qualification which makes you realize the qualification and disqualification of this underlying soul that has turned into a sage, a sannyasi, a wise man, a devotee, and a worker. That is, rely only on the love of the Guru who can see to the depth of the wealth of discipleship.

[GV3:P1764] Guruvani

[GV3:P1765] Santhigiri Janmadina Poojitha Samarppana Pathippu - 2001

[GV3:P1766] That truth is known through the Guru in the world as the course of karma comes

[GV3:P1767] How did the five senses reach the five elements? It is known by touching, smelling, and observing. When we understand this knowledge, we learn what the nature of the soul is in the sum of this knowledge. Then we learn what the function of the soul is. This is what we study in engineering.

[GV3:P1768] How do the flows of steel come from the air? Small particles fly in the air and solidify into steel. That is how it turns into the five senses. Then the soul is attached to it. Then what we know is the variations of the soul. Then what are the dynamic movements of the soul that are known by touching, smelling, and observing through the five elements and the five senses? His merit depends on those movements. According to this merit, they turn into male and female.

[GV3:P1769] The mind is one for both male and female. There are male and female in all seeds, even in poisonous seeds. Similarly, there are four castes. Then what is meant by caste? Varna. This varna is seen in the states called Jagrat (waking), Swapna (dreaming), Sushupti (deep sleep), and Turiya (the fourth state). Even a sannyasi cannot stay away from varna. As time passes, it only wears out and changes. In a way, science teaches you this. These colors stand in the rotation of each of the nine planets. That is Nava Jyothi. The earthly plane is the turning of Nava Jyothi.

[GV3:P1770] Color strikes on all sides. There are four sides, eight sides, and ten sides, right? What is the secret of these planets standing in space? You study its primary aspects in science. A certain distance from the earth comes as the sky. The part of the sky that is dense after the earth's atmosphere is called the void. Crossing this void, it goes to another sky. It goes as a layer of another gravitational field. Thus it stands as nine layers. Of these, seven planes have forms. Those are the seven skies. Science cannot describe the form and measure of the eighth and ninth skies. Only a sannyasi can say that. That is why science has multiplied by seven and stopped. In the eyes of science, there are only seven colors. It is not possible to cross into the other two skies. That is Jyothi (light). Anthar Jyothi, Bahir Jyothi, Pratyag Jyothi. We see all this visibly. Jyotir Jyothi, Swayam Jyothi - this is it. Then Atma Jyothi, Shivosmyaham.

[GV3:P1771] They only went that far. They went and stood near Shiva. The Vaishnava said something else, Swaha. The Shaktheya said another, Om Shakthi. Their knowledge only reached up to here. They could not cross this. Sree Krishna crossed this. They could not know Sree Krishna who came crossing this. Therefore, the clan in which Sree Krishna was born could not know him. Even though he was brought down and born so low, caught in the tangle of doctrines, they could not reach the knowledge. Since then, no one who came till today has been able to.

[GV3:P1772] To speak about the four kalas and bring them closer to Yugadharma, corrections on all four sides are needed. What exists in the polar regions of Uttarayana, Dakshinayana, East, and West directions? Demonic nature exists there. Those who live there will do anything. The people there are fat brutes like elephants. Sunlight does not reach there. Nothing grows in that snow.

[GV3:P1773] The earth is always rotating, isn't it? If the earth, perhaps leaving its axis, gets a rotation, it will go here and there and flip this over. It is said that the Himalayas are young in age. There is a difference between the soil in the plains and the soil in the Himalayas. It was formed when the earth was compressed at some point in time. There are huge mountains like the Himalayas in the sea as well. They were formed by flipping when the earth was compressed. Science has stopped at this point.

[GV3:P1774] Why am I telling you this? You, who are born in India, are not bad people. The interior of the earth is boiling. Water and other liquid substances are stored in this boiling place. It is stored like this so that the structure of the earth does not change. It was formed by rotating like this for so long. It is not only this solar system that rotates like this. There are millions of solar systems like this. That is how you should view the structure of this universe.

[GV3:P1775] We talk about the count of twenty-seven stars. But if you look at the sky, how many crores of stars are there. Each of these stars is a soul.

[GV3:P1776] It is said, O Agni, lead us along the path of righteousness for a good state. This has rotated and come to you. This is what is meant by "That truth is known through the Guru in the world as the course of karma comes." Can this science or anyone else remove these equations?

[GV3:P1777] How long will the axis of the earth last? Fourteen Manvantaras. When fourteen Manvantaras are over, one Kalpa ends. Then it will remain as nothing for that much time. Then it will go half way and come rotating like this. Then again there will be a Kalpa.

[GV3:P1778] The axis of the earth is our imagination. It was created to determine the equator of the earth. The earth should not deviate from this imaginary axis. If it deviates, the earth will turn. Even if any one of these nine planets flips, the earth will flip. As the earth rotates and moves like this, it will also come to the other side of the sun. Therefore, the rotation affecting the earth can come from any planet. It will happen only from any one planet. It will not come from anywhere else. Even when the earth rotates and goes above the sun, the earth can flip.

[GV3:P1779] We, human beings, are the remainder of the lineage that mediated. There are four directions, right? So the meditated male and female stand in four tiers. These are the four castes. If the caste is Brahmana, Kshatriya, Vaishya, and Shudra, the people of the western direction are Shudras. Shudras are ignorant. They are the ones who made inventions up to nuclear explosions and sent them here. If they increase this science in a way that connects these skies, a time will come when we cannot sit inside a room. We won't be able to sit outside either. If the wind hits, it will catch fire. This is dangerous. The dust particles on the moon catch fire. It lies crushed on the moon by the sun's rays. Seeds are formed when those dust particles reach the earth. These seeds rotate in various zodiacs. These are the castes in the seeds.

[GV3:P1780] I said there are four directions. Everything born where it turns and falls belongs to a caste of nether-world patients. They are the ones who venture into various atrocities. The earth will turn, won't it? Then these poison points will hit some corner. The place where poison points hit will become poisonous. The reproductive organ of these seeds will be in a different place.

[GV3:P1781] Children born every twelve years will have ten times your intelligence. After every twelve years, the amount of this intelligence keeps increasing. From nineteen seventy-three, it has been happening exactly like this. Its complete evidence to the world is obtained in seventy-three. We will talk about that later. Those who have noticed the course of science changing from seventy-three will understand. During the time of these children, all projects of science will change. The future will not be today's treatment systems or methods of applying medicine. This does not come under the limit of human determination. This is God's determination.

[GV3:P1782] Being able to be born in this world is the greatest thing. Man is rising and reaching up to the stellar sphere. This is the stellar sphere coming in the calculation of the earthly sphere. There are many spheres like the earth and there are stellar spheres for all of them. There are numerous planets like this. We see the stars seen in those spheres. When we go there, that star will not be seen anymore. Why? It would have taken birth and gone.

[GV3:P1783] Being able to be born in India is the greatest merit. You, who are born and raised in this Bharata, are carrying what has come from somewhere else. If you do that, India will be ruined. That is how we were ruined through the Trinity system. You should not do the work to be ruined again. It is to understand this that the birth and caste of the westerner were mentioned.

[GV3:P1784] This truth was indeed seen by the ancient Acharyas. But you could not respect or accept them, even Sree Sankara, Sree Narayana Guru, and Chattampi Swami who were born in this very Kerala. If you are not ready to accept this light anymore, this light will go to the other side. How will the karma of this soul go there? This is spiritual.

[GV3:P1785] If it goes like this, can we touch this science after a thousand years? But after about three hundred and fifty years, this science will be brought down. If it is not brought down, man cannot live here. This science will grab the soul, gnaw and eat it. If it is eaten like that, this body of ours will become useless even for oil cake. You should see that it has this much harm. We will remove this science from Bharata. The children who can do that will come here. You just need to understand that the preparation for it has already started here.

[GV3:P1786] How do we prepare this? We all know Jagrat. We also know Swapna. We also know Sushupti. But have you seen a sannyasi sitting? Because this standard of measurement is given, even if one sleeps, sleep will not come. It is said in Tamil, 'Thoongamal thoongi'. This sannyasi will sit like that with eyes closed. That is why I say the sannyasi is a thief. He will sit like a cat. His intention is different. It is in this sense that he is called a cat-sannyasi.

[GV3:P1787] There were two scholars. One of them was Mannath Padmanabhan. That was the time when only kerosene lamps were there. When the other scholar went to Mannath Padmanabhan's house, a cat came and was holding the lamp. Seeing this, he said, this is good. Mannath Padmanabhan said, it means if trained, it will obey. The other asked, then what about before that? Reply: it will obey. He left. After two or four days, this man went there again. He had caught a small mouse, put it in a sack, and kept it on his lap. They sat to eat. The cat came and started holding the lamp. This man took the sack from his lap and released this mouse. Leaving the lamp, the cat ran after it. When asked how what is trained will be, and how what is innate will be, he said he now understood that a cat can be trained like that, but nothing can be done to its own nature.

[GV3:P1788] In this nature, its own love, respect, devotion, humility, and knowledge are given to everything. It is the difference in this knowledge that each of us shows. Sannyasis are those who acquire knowledge and the knowledge beyond the known meaning. They have not gone for what is not there. They have gone for what is there. We do not know that greatness. How many births a sannyasi must have taken to reach this path?

[GV3:P1789] From the day the origin of this earth took place, or this solar system originated, this calculation also started. When that calculation is taken, it is then inquired from where and how this origin took place. That is the size of the moon. It is the power of the sun's rays. That is what falls on this earth. When it comes from the moon to the earth, it will become a seed. It is these seeds that show all these troubles. The remaining poisonous seeds are spread out as four castes on each side.

[GV3:P1790] When the sun comes to Uttarayana, one of our characteristics changes. Then it will be waiting to come to Dakshinayana. The details beyond this can be told later.

[GV3:P1791] An effect cannot exist without a cause. This karma is done here to understand what this subject is, to acquire it through your life, and to earn merit through karma. That dharma will come with you. If you help someone, that karma will stand there. Even if you harm someone, it will stand. How does this stand in these sky spheres, and reaching the void, where does it stand?

[GV3:P1793] Santhigiri Janmadina Poojitha Samarppana Pathippu - 2001

[GV3:P1794] How to carry forward Devotion

[GV3:P1795] When handling devotion, some flaws tend to come. What are those flaws? Which among them can be changed? What can be discarded? What will be the experience for those with and without mental knowledge in the first step of devotion? What will be the first flawed emotion arising from devotion?

[GV3:P1796] The five senses are the special qualities of the body. This quickly runs and reaches the mind. The five senses of knowledge arising through the five senses must be kept without putting them into practice. Due to lack of innate nature, whatever is thought causes emotion. That emotion arises due to love. If this love is not mixed with devotion, there is nothing special.

[GV3:P1797] If you want something, you must do it constantly and daily. There are various justifications for it in different religions. In Islam, women are not allowed in the mosque. But burial can take place in the mosque. Wise women are specially buried in mosques. For example, Thuckalay Mosque, Beemapally.

[GV3:P1798] Let's come back to the subject. How to control the five senses? If a certain desire catches hold without changing, your mind should be offered with purity of heart to the form most liked.

[GV3:P1799] If you examine the nest of a very beautiful bird, it will be full of stench. Man is much more stinky than this. How can one approach a man with so much stench? By love. With the beauty that is love. The eye only sees this beauty. It is the mind that accepts it. Therefore, this must be changed using the mind itself.

[GV3:P1800] The body is the seat of eight passions. These are the tendencies that destroy us. Whenever these tendencies rush into the mind, they must be changed through action and dedication. When anything is offered to the Guru, it will be destroyed. When one's own course of karma is seen in the light and the Guru is seen in that place, the flaw will burn and be destroyed, and goodness will come in that place. The light will destroy the flaw. This is a vision.

[GV3:P1801] There are many such visions. There are visions in every religion. The vision that highlights the course of karma. When praying to the Guru, the course of karma will burn and be destroyed. When there is devotion, there is also an experience of feeling a special pleasure. There is another experience where that pleasure increases. If a person without devotion comes near, not only can that pleasure not be increased, but it will also change and go away. Then that person's tendency is also imparted to us.

[GV3:P1802] When true devotion comes, there will be no laziness to do a task. There will be no fatigue either. Fatigue will only occur if one does not work. When devotion comes, there is a special pleasure in doing work. It is good to work and sweat at that time. After a little while, any one emotion in you will come out. Or else anger will come. Immediately great humility should be shown. Anger should not come at all. If work is done with devotion, any work can be done, and through that the course of karma will be severed. In that place, love will be felt towards everyone and everything.

[GV3:P1803] What arises first from devotion is attentiveness. When true devotion comes, one will get up only slowly. One will behave only gently. But a devotee will have anger towards another devotee. If a devotee gets agitated over a mistake done knowingly by another devotee, that agitation will take effect. When devotion comes, there will be many difficulties. It will not be ordinary intelligence. It will be the intelligence to do whatever needs to be done.

[GV3:P1804] He who does not complete the karma generated in devotion cannot untie that karma no matter how many births pass. This will become like a divine curse. When devotion comes, what we should think is: You gave me this much devotion. The desire came to forget everything and get closer to you. But in the next moment, you yourself bring in me the base natures to destroy all that. What should I do? If one desires with pain to change like this, there will be much crying. If this happens ten times, a great change will come.

[GV3:P1805] A sannyasi got intelligence and logic through devotion. At the same time, a flaw also came to him - if a woman practicing prostitution came to his door, the flawed thought to drive her away. Due to this flawed thought, this sannyasi was indeed destroyed. Because, even if she is one who practices prostitution, she has a good quality. She will not speak ill of anyone. She will only say that she is worse than everyone. She will always have the awareness that she is committing a mistake. What is the reason for this sannyasi to be destroyed? The hatred he felt towards her. This hatred is called Abhichara dosha. By this Abhichara dosha, that sannyasi's power of penance will be lost.

[GV3:P1806] Then who is better? This prostitute or the sannyasi? It is her. She became like that in some course of karma. Except for that, she does not have such a thought at all. She will always guard her mind. God will only have mercy on such people.

[GV3:P1807] When devotion comes, the voice will decrease and a change will come to the voice. When devotion comes, many imaginations will occur without our knowledge. Finally, a state may come where one does not know what devotion is. In the early days, one would feel like crying and speaking. Then after passing through various stages, a condition will come where nothing can be known. If this is not guarded, devotion will be destroyed. No matter how much devotion a woman gets, she should only proceed by satisfying her family members.

[GV3:P1808] Everything should be moderate. Moderation in expenditure. When devotion comes, there will be enthusiasm to work. Still, it should be done moderately. Doing things with a lot of noise is not devotion. If done moderately, anything can be done. Understanding this, always do the goodness that can be done.

[GV3:P1809] When devotion comes, there are other things to pay attention to. Knowing that the family members are without innate nature and that many flaws can come from them, this opportunity should be used to pray meltedly and act meltedly that all the flaws that have come to me be removed.

[GV3:P1810] People will only obey if you tell them after doing the karma. You can make them do it only if you do it. When someone makes a mistake, it is not good to prepare to speak without tact. They should be called gently and told only with humility. Then it will be alright. When they don't obey what is said, one should think and act saying, I will do my work, I will also do theirs, if there is any love in them, I will earn it. Then their love will leave and vengeance will come. Because that vengeance came, I got an opportunity to cry. That is my victory. Do everything always smiling at everyone. One should only smile at those who are younger in age. One can smile at boys and girls. One should not smile at equals or elders. Treating everyone equally might not be liked by the elders. That will do harm. You don't need to show off your work in front of one who has experience in working. He would have seen everything.

[GV3:P1811] When devotion comes, a state where nothing can be thought of will come. That is when bliss occurs. That devotion will never leave. It will never leave those who have experienced the ecstasy of bliss. Even if such devotion comes for only two moments, it has great value. There are people here whose devotion has remained like that for a whole day. When there is a change in the mind, it will change. Even if any thought comes unexpectedly, it will change.

[GV3:P1812] Devotion is a special merit. Not everyone will get it. Devotion arising out of sorrow for the wife or husband is not devotion. If the devotion immersed in the ecstasy of bliss is obtained for three and three-quarters of a nazhika, it means a time of samadhi is obtained. If a person with discrimination gets this, he will start making it experienced. Devotion is a subject that goes on imparting experience. For this, three states - Jagrat, Swapna, Sushupti - are seen recorded. Everyone has these three states. In this, past, present, and future are seen reflected. That is the vision.

[GV3:P1813] If one sits with eyes closed, the past, future, and present are shown intermingled. That is, the impression of what was seen earlier, the impression of what is currently standing and functioning, the impression of getting mixed in something - all these three are shown intermingled. Brought to manifestation, words will be obtained from it. Through whichever light this is seen, that light will also show the dynamic movements of prana.

[GV3:P1814] Where is the seat of prana? In the Annamaya Kosha. The body is made of Annamaya Kosha. Prana sits in the body. For this to exist, there is a space in the heart. This is near the brain. Attention should also be given in seeing and experiencing where this exists and what the course of karma is. Whatever is being thought about, one should think by giving full form to that subject.

[GV3:P1815] A great soul gets devotion immersed in the ecstasy of bliss for only three and three-quarters of a nazhika. But here, the children with vision get it for days together. They get this without suffering any pain. A rich man's children have a nature of rejecting whatever is given to them, right? Your way is like that. If given to a poor man's child, he will receive it with both hands extended. Even if there is a desire to use this, you do not know how much benefit it has. My sorrow is that you, who have experienced this much, go after the tendency shown by anyone. You do not give priority to your tendency. You do not think about the flaw of your course of karma. What all you had experienced and lived, worked in previous births - you get the knowledge to remove these courses of karma through this experience.

[GV3:P1816] If you know about light, its frequency, and how light dissolves, you will be able to know about your course of karma.

[GV3:P1817] It was said that when devotion comes, various emotions will rise up. If he is one who has renounced everything, whatever happens, he will stick to this itself. For that, the Guru is needed as a medium. He who says that once reaching such a level, the medium is no longer needed, is not speaking true knowledge. If you look at the sky, it is void. But what all exists there! Let the mind be broad like the sky. The mind is also broad like the sea. Whatever comes into the mind, the flow of thought towards one thing cannot be removed. Wherever one sits, the mischief of past karma tends to come into the mind. And the mischief yet to come too. But what you like permanently will remain. The Guru, who is the medium you have targeted, can change this, if the special merit of devotion occurs, if dedication occurs...

[GV3:P1819] Santhigiri Janmadina Poojitha Samarppana Pathippu - 2001

[GV3:P1820] Anomalies in Devotion

[GV3:P1821] To bring small things to perfection, one has to pass through many sorrows. Our ancient history is an acquaintance with such a thing. When we say 'our', it is not just mine or yours, nor just of our ancestors. We are trapped in a karma capacity where we completely failed to get a knowledge that has been changing from millions of years ago. To change this, many Gurus and prophets have come and gone during this time. But the knowledge and familiarity to know or impart this was not enough. It is this situation that has affected us.

[GV3:P1822] There are many people who say we have made some mistake. But where did this mistake happen, how did it happen, this is what is not known. The truth is that our ancestors who were karma theorists or the Acharyas and prophets who came to clear ideas could not figure out where it happened. As far as these Acharyas are concerned, and therefore as far as we are concerned, this stands as a completely unfamiliar karma. We flowed and fell into this ignorance. That is why we could not take up dharma. Dharma must be taken up with humility. We have not been humble there, except for nice words.

[GV3:P1823] This lack of humility is experienced in different ways. One is caste. The other is religion and race. How did these castes, religions, and races originate, and when? How did the opportunities arise for them to keep changing like this?

[GV3:P1824] The theorists belonging to the Trinity system brought and conceptualized ten avatars. Besides that, many other avatars have occurred. Now avatars are coming limitlessly. If avatar purushas come limitlessly like this, which one will we accept? That is a problem. Then there are some who are designated as belonging to the prophetic religion and Guru religion. But what is the greatness of time, what is the belief appropriate for the time? We also do not have the knowledge to understand this, learn in detail from those lineages, and move forward. This error didn't happen just today or yesterday. We should at least look from the time of Sree Krishna, know about this weakness, and act. We can understand through Kamsa that the divine determination had a prior preparation for the arrival of Sree Krishna. We have never been ready to respectfully know about that history or take up any aspect of that suffering and act with sacrifice till today after the arrival of Krishna. After Sree Krishna, many Gurus came all over the world.

[GV3:P1825] The knowledge that there are numerous globes in this atmosphere, just like our concept of the globe, has been obtained today according to science, hasn't it? It is a limitation to just stop at the place of that knowledge once a knowledge is discovered. Staying in that obtained knowledge, they don't think about how it happened. Now, each of you who studied English says that Ptolemy is the greatest person in thought. His disciples have also come in that same path. But a nature of stopping at the place of that discovering knowledge can be seen here too.

[GV3:P1826] We cannot succeed by including an account of two thousand, four thousand, or five thousand years in the life of a great soul. Even if we say four or five lakh years, it won't suffice. The calculation of at least one Chaturyuga should be stated as a minimum. One Chaturyuga has a calculation of over forty-three lakh years. Even that is divided into various dharmas. But we don't know anything about that. If we follow the calculation of these English people or what is formulated by the Trinity system, we won't be able to tell anything to the children who are going to come.

[GV3:P1827] In order to understand the magnitude of this mistake, we need to see the emergence of thousands of Mahatmas. The information is that 2444 Gurus came since the beginning of this Kaliyuga. If this many people came within just five thousand years, how many lakhs of people would have come within this Chaturyuga? As far as we are concerned, this stands as a big question mark. We can only handle this if we knock this question somewhere and take any opportunity.

[GV3:P1828] What we can take as a basis are only Mahatmas. Even among these Mahatmas, we should know whose history and calculations will create a favorable situation for our inquiry. First, we need to get to know about the Gurus who have grown up till today in the Trinity system and about the course of karma that was born, grew, and perished in that lineage. But fundamentally we have an obstacle to accepting it. That obstacle is what is said about Swayambhuva Manu in the Trinity system. We should know that Swayambhuva Manu, as the name suggests, originated in karma that was self-created or born. But it is tied up as being the son of Brahma. This is where the previously mentioned limitation has come. There is no method anywhere that knows what to do to change this limitation.

[GV3:P1829] Our ancestors speak about Sree Rama. Or they speak about the history that came within this Chaturyuga. But let us peek into the final period of all these great souls. We will feel pain here. God has given us the opportunity to see the boundary of the lives of many great souls. This opportunity can come to you only from a center of faith. When we say center of faith, it must be a place that recognizes what the Trinity system and the Manu system are. Wherever the Guru lineage or Manu lineage, Deva lineage goes and stops, the path to finding and correcting this starts from there. Instead, the mistake that happened to all great souls was tying them to the Trinity.

[GV3:P1830] To say how this mistake in the beginning occurred, a lot will have to be said. That is not needed here. It is enough if we know from that matter which we failed to know about Sree Krishna. Sree Krishna was born in the Yadava clan. What is the karma to get that this great soul, who worked for the people, will be born on such an occasion before he is born? After being born, what did it turn out to be in the end? We have never thought about this. We haven't even thought why it happened so. Till today, only a karma has taken place where everything is repeatedly said and confined to Vedanta, reached the sweet sound of letters, and torn and interpreted. This is what is preserved as Vedanta. We cannot prove the truth from this Vedanta.

[GV3:P1831] Some people speak making Ramarajya an example. Bringing forward that great soul who suffered so much, in what way have we seen him? There is no one to clearly tell us Sree Rama's stance. If we take all the people who have come till today after Sree Rama as a whole, we will understand that they are not the ones to be taken up in this Kala-dharma (dharma of this age).

[GV3:P1832] What currently exists is the twenty-eighth Chaturyuga of Vaivasvata Manu. Where did it go wrong in between? Till today, efforts have been made to fill what went wrong. Where it went wrong is what story writers and thinkers have looked for and not found. What is not found is beyond thought. How can what is beyond thought be obtained?

[GV3:P1833] Standing in the karma called 'Dirghadarshi' (visionary) and conducting a far-sight of the solar system, the subsequent knowledge is whether the solar system is subjected to Manvantaras or to something else. It is this knowledge that should bring you straight. Such knowledge will not be transferred to us immediately. It will be transferred very gradually. For that, God should not be brought in and limited as a sleight of hand. We should come to reclaim and see the times when dharma could be practiced with life, and seeing it, reclaim and work on the mistakes in it. To undertake that, we should observe the course of karma that stands in four ways: gross (sthula), subtle (sukshma), causal (karana), and effect (karya). And then we should look at when dharma should emerge. Since it is Kaladharma, we must specially look at Kalidharma. We must look at what Kali is about, and how far the person in whom the dharma to be undertaken as much as the acquaintance of Kali is vested has reached, or where he stands. The person implementing Yugadharma by adhering to it must know all this.

[GV3:P1834] What guidance should that person give for us? Without looking whether it is a ghost, deva, sage, or sannyasi, what is the mistake in the course of dharma that has come till today, and what should be done for that mistake - this is what must be advised. There is no use in advising anything else. Whatever is said, whether Yoga theory, Karma theory, or Jnana theory, none of that is something a person can cross over. Then what is that karma? Seeing the course of karma that passed earlier, catching that karma and correcting it. That karma is worthy to accept even your children as a contribution to the world. Here, I should be excluded. There is no point in saying I am an advocate of liberation, a rationalist, a scholar, mute, or one with no knowledge, an ignoramus. All these fall under your verbosity. Don't let each one try to win by saying such things.

[GV3:P1835] Here we must remember what a miserable state we have fallen into. Speaking rationalism and liberationism, we are moving away from the course of dharma and from the thought of what all stands harmonized in dharma. When it happens so, there will be a shortage in the knowledge required to know about each period. Then the karma written and preserved by science scholars and Vedantins will climb into that place. This is an obstacle on the path to truth.

[GV3:P1836] The truth is none of these. It is in each one of us. The fire in the five elements is in us. There is fire in everything in the world. In the part of knowledge mixed with the five elements, the growth of the soul stands blocked. What has happened to it, in which tangle is the soul standing - this must be known. Knowing this is the mark of a visionary.

[GV3:P1837] How will this knot be untied? If the visionary is to untie that knot and give it to us, we must have an understanding that we will get everything through that visionary. What has happened in past times is vision; if we sit waiting thinking we will get everything from it, it will not come. Such people will end up with the miserable fate of a soul without vitality.

[GV3:P1838] This is not what I am telling you. In the course of karma where you grow as male and female, the karma of birth and death rests in everything, even in an atom. What is the karma worked out and confined in this atom? Who must you become to take back this dharma? What should the Guru lineage be, what should the Guru be, who has reached this abundance of merit? You must see and know this yourselves. It should not happen except through such a path of knowledge. This is not to happen in one or two children, not in one caste or one religion, not in any one caste of living beings. The subject is finding what the achieved attainment of the course of the soul traveling in all living beings is. By that achieved attainment, where do we stand. This should not be told by the person who has deserved it, or seen clearly and become qualified. You and your children should see this and say it. Why will you say so? I have been given a waking nature that must be operated by comparing it with your past times and the present and future times. Knowing what this waking, dreaming, and waking-deep sleep are, we must also see what all ripens in deep sleep. This has come in the course of karma where my birth and death are thus known, done, and made to be done. This truth is not told to those who say truth does not exist or to those who say it exists.

[GV3:P1839] In the course of dharma where eight planets also join like that on the earth where truth stands as the foundation, how has this truth worked and confined itself? It is the Guru's obligation to show this. Only a disciple who has seen the Guru should tell you. Others should not tell. You must understand that this is the experiential side of truth. This is not the dharma where intelligence is mixed in the foolishness of stringing together fragments of Vedanta. This is not speaking the ignorance of the scholar or the scholarship of the ignorant.

[GV3:P1840] What is your capacity, as householders who see the three times and take up the responsibility of future times, which shows how we can maintain dharma, and how will you take it? This is not meant for a sannyasi. Let the sannyasi take it or not take it. There is no big deal to say in that. If the sannyasi wants, he will take it. Or he will throw it and go. Let him do whatever. You need this. You, your children, and every one of you living need this.

[GV3:P1841] Gurumargam is the path of knowing the unfortunate fate of that soul, which repeatedly brings out the good and the bad clinging to the course of righteousness of the past times, and what is the fate. I am talking to you about such a Guru's path. Nobody except Sri Krishna has spoken about this path. Jesus speaks about this in the best way. Vivekananda also speaks well. How will it come to us in a good way? Vivekananda's conclusion is that the gods have met in the heaven against this. What if they meet? This will go away only if it is a Guru who stays in the heaven. If it is a Guru who stays in heaven, it will go away. This is what has happened so far. This is what I am telling you now, placing such an upadhi in front of you. You should get any child who has grown up through this upadhi and can be seen. Otherwise you have no salvation. Not only do I tell you this, but I also give you such a karma. Observe whether it is true or false, and learn to perform karma. Also look at how alms and virtues will reach us. Look at who we are in that humble karma where the virtue of that alms has reached luck. Each of you be a person for that. This is what I have to tell you.

[GV3:P1844] The reality to be known through the theory of experience

[GV3:P1845] Sri Sankara and Sree Narayana Guru brought a new idea. Then Radhaswami to say there is an idea. But he has no disciples. And there is no advice side. Although Madhva brought Dwaita, the followers tied it to the old Hindu way. We have a familiarization that we cannot distinguish the idea brought by these Acharyas and the old system.

[GV3:P1847] The lamp that clears the tripudi

[GV3:P1848] Will not be obtained by a false ascetic

[GV3:P1849] How many people understand that Sri Narayana Guru has said this about the obstacle in the spiritual field?

[GV3:P1850] A personality born in a knowledge nature greater than Sri Krishna is not seen. But Sri Krishna has brought progress in such a way that he has hidden our ignorance and sung praises about something else and travelled the wrong way. Even the one who wrote Gita Govinda could not say what Sri Krishna is. Similarly, we have been given knowledge in a way that our devotion gets some trouble.

[GV3:P1851] Our Acharyas have not come and gone in a small category. They came in a high nature. But because they could not see the truth, they tried to go independently and otherwise. But if the independence itself cannot be handled in the high way of doing it by the power of yoga as Sri Krishna said, that is also dangerous.

[GV3:P1852] Logic does not mean logic according to our intelligence. Similarly, yoga means the sides of the soul's good deeds. That yoga can be obtained only by God's decision. It cannot be obtained by a little struggle. A purity of purpose should have worked behind this effort so that we get a knowledge to go as far as God's destiny. Instead of that, the trimurti system has been tied up and we have been buried in it and drawing water. This is the lack of purity of purpose.

[GV3:P1853] One may ask whether all these Acharyas who came were not according to God's decision. They are those who came like that. But what to do if the old scent is present when taking birth? How can one know these old scents by oneself? Only those who are born wise can know like that. What all Sri Krishna did because he was born wise. But these scholars praised all this and brought it close to great knowledge and put it all in maya, and then put it in God's maya. If God shows maya, we can also be a little! Only a person who makes up for the lack of maya or aranyas can say what is maya and what is truth. The forest dweller cannot do that.

[GV3:P1854] Ashrams, temples, tombs of great men are our places of worship. But all these are places where the aranyas are completed once again. This is what is said to be Upanishad. If the scholar interprets the Upanishad, everything will turn into a form of light. This is the stream of light that the Muslims say. This is what is said in Ishavasya too.

[GV3:P1855] There is no one among us who knows to say what aranyakas are. Is anyone to blame for that? If some great man comes and works with us, they will change it into an avatar and write it as unwanted. Even if they don't know anything, they will say that he is an avatar. This is a Hindu cover up. What can we gain by saying this? We also say what our predecessors said. The successors will say the same thing. From all this we cannot know where the position of dharma stands.

[GV3:P1856] How will we, who have accepted Sri Sankara as the world Guru, enter into his advaita side? Nobody has said it wrongly. But nobody told us how to enter into it. The Acharyas living today on the side of Sri Sankara know very well about this shortcoming. But they will not say it out. I am telling my experience.

[GV3:P1857] When a person who stayed with me started to travel all over Kerala after having an experience, I scolded him and told him to go out. So he went and met Kancheepuram Sankaracharya. He asked him to tell him something for knowledge. Then he asked what is your experience. When the experience was told like this, he asked, Hey, where is this from. He meant me and said, there is a person leading a small life, it is from him. Immediately he said, this is the way of Adi Sankara, you just go there. He understood this, but the one who had this experience did not understand.

[GV3:P1858] If he understood that it was Adi Sankara's way, why didn't he take it? Kancheepuram is the matham that occurred closest to Sri Sankara mathams. If this is the reaction there, is there anything to say about other places? This is not something the Acharyas do not know. They do not know where to correct. It is not their fault. If complaining, it is the ignorance of the complainers. All traditions are standing behind such a mistake. How can we change this mistake?

[GV3:P1859] If someone comes independently to clear this mistake, these poor people have knowledge like Gita Govinda. They cannot go beyond that. At the same time, if anyone gets Vedanta, they will go after it. The one who tells the end of the Veda will not tell what is in the beginning of the Veda. Every scholar tells it through a story and completes it as a poet's stage and goes away. Apart from this, these scholars do not know the meaning of the independent word of the Acharya.

[GV3:P1860] Question: We know its meaning. Can't we say that?

[GV3:P1861] Guru: Even if we say it suddenly, will they understand? We should say it slowly. As far as we are concerned, this is a small matter. But I am unable to say it. To whom should I tell? Therefore, I am in pain now. I have no time left. Of my companions, didn't many leave after coming to know this? To think that those who stayed with me and knew this were even ready to file a case against me ٹ.! This is all these poor people know. They do not have the karmic capacity to know the other side of it. What will I do? I am suffering in intense pain. They do not have the aptitude to know this. Taking an unknown subject, making a big deal out of it, and turning a goat into a dog is a habit everyone has.

[GV3:P1862] Question: Does the Guru have a relation with these ignorant people?

[GV3:P1863] Guru: There is a relation. Now whether it is father and son or mother and daughter or Guru and disciple, what to do without mutually sharing idea, love, respect and humility?

[GV3:P1864] What is the world situation that has come today? Our contemporaries are people who call knowledge even a siddhi. There are advaitins and then some are mukti vadins. Some are rationalists, some are vagabonds who come behind it. How to correct such people?

[GV3:P1865] I will only tell the knowledge from my experience. I have not gone to get any siddhi. I have not gained literacy. Since our ancestors themselves did not get the sense of dharma that should be gained through life, they are sitting without even the knowledge to think why they came to me. How many great souls came to bring this to us. Kumaran Asan said a word about Sree Narayana Guru: Life, penance and body were sacrificed. But even scholars who say this is wrong have come to this field. Their argument is that nobody can sacrifice penance. This is said by a great scholar.

[GV3:P1866] Question: It is enough to come and see in Santhigiri, nobody needs to say how penance has been sacrificed, to understand.

[GV3:P1867] Guru: This person who is known as Acharya stands on the side of the scholar's knowledge and stands in the karma of saying that he is also a science person. He is not coming forward from there. Why is he standing like this? If someone asks him something, he should answer, shouldn't he. Saying and making the vision side of his Guru said, and showing it with respect, and taking another side to teach through theory that such Guru and disciples have some ignorance, and saying like that there, and saying like this here, and then another one of his and his ancestors. What to teach these people with this? Where is this tie standing. That is what I said, these poor people have not done this mistake. Pretending to do the necessary effort to grow up to the idea and at the same time becoming a mere science person, and adding the knowledge on that side and the knowledge of other thinkers, and tearing apart the theory of experience, aren't they standing in that karma?

[GV3:P1868] In the place where the karma of sacrifice should be highlighted, I am highlighting the familiarity of the unfortunate state where each of us has reached. Not to blame anyone. We are not hearing anyone say the greatness of the sacrifice of the great souls who have come till today. If we say good, it is good, it is very big, will our obligation to it be over?

[GV3:P1869] It is a good thing to be able to grow in the science system. But it will take another three hundred years for this science to reach the spiritual level. Science today sees many things through light and gives us proofs. That is a truth. But as long as the truth is not completely discovered, what is the use of it?

[GV3:P1870] Question: Science cannot go beyond what can be discovered.

[GV3:P1871] Guru: Science will continue to grow for another three hundred years. But when going to the spiritual level, it cannot be touched there. Great souls themselves are proof of that. Although it has changed in many ways, how much has human effort over many periods been of use to us? There are some sayings that great souls strive for themselves. That is true. We will get it only if they become great, won't we. What is the use of going to a person without wealth and celebrating and saying the visheshas of wealth? Wealth needs wealth itself. Education needs education. What is the Ishavasya language highlighted by Sree Narayana Guru? Other than by avidya, other than by vidya, those who have known both vidya and avidya will become immortal by vidya. We have not understood this matter. There is no use scolding someone else with cause and effect for that.

[GV3:P1872] It is not the matter whether individuals are good or bad. We have to know about a working capacity. We can only do that. What are we going to do by rejecting what our predecessors did as bad? Nobody can say that it is not. Vedas, tantras and puranas were created and pushed away in a way that we could not understand the predecessors, without even giving us an acquaintance about them. I am telling you my experience. If I don't tell you this, it will be the biggest trap I can do. I have told you this in many ways. But today it stands as a great sorrow for me. I have no sorrow in that. I opened up and told you what is there. I also got a Godly vishesha to do that. You should also know this.

[GV3:P1873] This Godly vishesha is not something that can be completed by anyone's ability or scholarship or any siddhi visheshas. We did not see what the great souls said and showed. Praising Sri Krishna, he covered it with maya so that his special love could not be seen. I said a matter that I understood very well. If anyone else has understood in any other way, I am not guilty either.

[GV3:P1874] Guruvani

[GV3:P1875] Santhigiri Janmadinapoojitha Samarpanappathippu - 2001

[GV3:P1876] The storage that humanity must acquire

[GV3:P1877] What do you need? Purification of your soul. For the purification of the soul, you must know what the worship nature of your past acquaintance is. That is, this Guru must bring before you everything that you have worshipped in the past. All the gods and goddesses, all the gurus and all the prophets that your ancestors and you have been worshipping should be included in this Guru. Who are you who got such a Guru? You have the freedom to hold on to what you like. Whatever you hold on to, you should know like this.

[GV3:P1878] Do not say anything to those who say that the things that have come till today do not exist. Let them say it. If all these things that have come have right and wrong, you must speak and look about a knowledge to see where is right and where is wrong. Where does each of us stand in the mistake that has come over time? You should not think that all people who have set out to call God can gain this knowledge. You should not take it as their fault either. A completely ignorant person cannot step out of the house for this matter. Even a person who has no desire to see God with blind faith will not step out of the house. So if ascetics or something do something wrong, he is an ascetic, you should not be a householder who only learns to say this.

[GV3:P1879] What is an ascetic, what is a jnani, what is a karmi, what is a yogi? Seeing like this, he must see how much in it. A householder should be able to do this. As a proof to this, a story of a householder is told. A sadhu went asking for alms in a house. At that time, this woman was giving food to her husband. Thinking what does this woman have to show so much enthusiasm in giving food to her husband, this sadhu stared at the woman. Then she says, this is not a crane, Konkanare. Why is it said like this? This sadhu was doing penance sitting under a tree. He has often looked with force like this. One day a crane excreted on his shoulder. He got angry and looked at this crane. Its skin got burnt. It fell down unable to fly. Thinking that he had achieved everything, he got up and went to that house. Then this woman says, this is not a crane, Konkanare. What did you understand? Is this yogi great, or this woman? The woman is great. How did she become great? This woman is a person who has a Guru. That Guru has told her everything about her dharma. If this woman could be like this, who can't you be?

[GV3:P1880] This is the karma and dharma of this period. Only this should be conducted. None of these siddhi arguments are needed. If we get involved in a Guru-disciple relationship, we can become the greatest in everything. The Guru is a visionary in the karma taken together by husband, wife, children, father and mother. If you do not understand this, not only will you not be able to become anything, but you will also become spoiled. You should not be spoiled at all. Where to take good children from without a good householder?

[GV3:P1881] Do not blame the ascetic. The ascetic is one who does not have a dhoti for the mind. He will put a dhoti on all other parts. This knowledge should be for the householder. The karma that should exist in his progeny should be taken by him as a share with the Guru and the Guru lineage. In that place, if the householders learn all the cooked up stories and learn the end of the Veda, they will get nothing. If you learn Vedanta and reject the end of knowledge, the situation will come that you will not get the Veda and you will not get the end. Then what will you tell your children?

[GV3:P1882] I am not saying that householders should become ascetics. It is enough to become a good householder. From that householder, women, children and everyone should have the sense of karma to do charities properly. Nobody other than them can save this world. I will not agree to any other argument. No argument will work either.

[GV3:P1883] There are many types of Guru-disciple relationships. Nobody can correct you except a Guru who can tell what is there in the headquarters of rishis and gods standing in various levels acquiring divinity, and divine souls who have gone according to the yugadharma. There is no point in saying any other argument about this. Their knowledge should be able to lead you and your children to knowledge, to the end of knowledge. This is my idea.

[GV3:P1884] There are experienced children here. You should acquire it. Other than that, I cannot do anything. The right and wrong done by our ancestors are made to be taken by them. And then completing that karma. Only after taking and seeing that right and wrong, a right for tomorrow can be placed in that place. Seeing that is not Vedanta knowledge. If you go by the Vedanta argument, you can't see anything. That will only be removed by saying swaha. If there is a dharma that it will come in the world according to the course of karma, you are the one to bring that karma into dharma. The most virtue-earned dharma that you and your children work and settle for that course of dharma is in your hands. As long as you do not do this, you are nobody in this dharma.

[GV3:P1885] Once an ascetic told me that a householder means one who has done and completed all worthless things. This is ignorance. We do not need this ignorance. This knowledge of making one an heir of virtue by completing humility from children who completely share love and respect is enough for us.

[GV3:P1886] I am telling my experience. Not dragging you into stories by telling stories. I will not do that either. There was an ascetic named Dr Sivanandan. He has given initiation names to many. But these disciples have nothing to say about the swami. That is, the disciples have not got a knowledge about the swami. Whole of Kerala knows that Abhedananda Swami is a disciple of this Dr Sivanandan. I know him before he became an ascetic. He also has not told about his swami. But he is a complete ascetic in the logic of Kerala.

[GV3:P1887] What did we understand from this? The karma that is in hand is such that even ascetics cannot understand what a Guru is. Everything is tied under gods and goddesses and water is poured. Dr Sivanandan has only reached a little, but he has taken it that pure. He did not get time to do the rest. He gave discipleship to a woman. That woman came as the head of the mutt there at the end. Nobody knows what state was given to that woman. The other disciples there also don't know. What is that? A karma of woman is needed there to do service to his feet. This is what he showed. If all these learned ascetics sit around and say no to this love, will it become no?

[GV3:P1888] I am not saying any of this. I will not tell you such a karma. How should you and your children bring the karma that you have to do yourself and think as house, land and country? How should the children you contribute to the world from that rule be? The vision in those children will cross the danger mentioned above. You are the authority to say this strongly anywhere. But it won't work unless you take it up. Not saying that your children should become ascetics. Your third generation will be pure here. I expect only this much from you. That pure generation will see and know the greatness of the karma done by rishi, ascetic and jnani. Then they will push away the bad with the good. I demand such a generation from you. You should do it if you can. I do not wish to do anything by calling what cannot be done. I will not say such a thing either.

[GV3:P1889] A dharmaputra and a dharmaputri should be born from you. It is enough to donate only this here. Whatever else you donate, it will become bad.

[GV3:P1890] You, those who come here, did not come knowing anything. You understood after coming here, didn't you? If you still don't understand this, some difficulties will come here. Those who have come here and done guru pooja should be especially careful. Those who have not done guru pooja should also know about the danger situation that can come if they are not careful. How many marriages you have done without doing what I said to do and doing what I said not to do. Is any of this useful to you? Is it useful to me? Is it useful to the world?

[GV3:P1891] The guru pooja done here is not done anywhere in the world till today. Nobody can do like that. If this is done for you and you cannot keep it decently, then why are you making me do it? This is for your children. The mistake that came to us traditionally will be understood only after three or four generations. After that, the bad in what we have worshipped will be destroyed and the good will be maintained. Purifying and standing like this is waiting to be born. How will it be born? This is done to make it born like that. I have an obligation to do all this like this. That is another matter.

[GV3:P1892] I am not doing spiritual. Because you have not come for spiritual. If there is spiritual in you, you will get it. Whatever things you have worshipped will come before you. And then the Guru will reach it in jagrat, swapna, sushupti and then to turiya. After reaching, if you obey the word, you will have its prosperity. That is the experience of all of you. Otherwise there will be failure.

[GV3:P1893] Keep the word. Otherwise I cannot do anything. You might think that I have done it with my ability. I have done what that Brahma Shakti told me to do like this. You have to struggle a lot to keep it to your children. Otherwise your children will go wrong.

[GV3:P1894] Why is it said that this karma should be kept in its decency. For your children to grow up, and in that lineage the gods and goddesses and rishis and jnanis and everything that your ancestors worshipped to come and take birth. This is a subject you have not heard. That karma is showing you yourself. Not saying that everyone can see it. It is seen like the goodness of the lucky soul. Whatever is born--even if it becomes god and goddess and rishi--its change will happen only if it reaches the mukti lineage.

[GV3:P1895] Such gurus do not come always. Only gurus who come in the end of yugas can do this. If they don't do it, how many yugas will go on again. Even those who do not believe here see my form. There is a lineage called Kozhikode Sivananda lineage. It is called Vadakara Sivanandan. Four generations have come in that lineage. They still have wealth and nourishment and everything. The locals say that there is no one as good as them. But they are all patients. What did you understand? They are people who say that everything will end with yogavidya. You will feel sad if you see those children. In a house that believes in the Sivananda lineage, their father died and the tomb was built well and kept properly and buried. But this house got sick and wandered. I had to go there. I went and saw and told that this is it. They also understood. Having done it for three or four generations, they could not achieve this. When asked, they are saying that this comes like a magician and nothing is seen. How is it here? If this is done, it is seen that all the things you have worshipped reach the guru. Not taking it and throwing it away. That karma will stand in the atmosphere of this ashram. You seeing it, getting wisdom seeing it, children are born to you from that wisdom. Or those children are born in your lineage. If these things are not born in your lineage itself, your lineage will have destruction. So if father and mother do not live straight and take children straight, this will become a very harsh thing done to them.

[GV3:P1896] Then what will I tell those who say that what we worshipped till today is not there? It is there. They all must get liberation from where they should reach. If it has a karma to be born, it should be born. The existing worship should be changed and another one should be given instead.

[GV3:P1897] This is our subject. Not showing siddhi, showing miracle, saying some great things and bringing money, showing all that to you, making you fall in all that and perish. All this will come according to your luck. If you have luck, you will look after the movement. The movement is coming for you. Other than this, if you say I have gone, I have seen, how much will you get? How much will the movement get?
