[GV3:P2040] Devotion is the perfection of love. No matter what other thoughts arise, it will not be able to overcome true devotion. The nature of true devotion is to see one's chosen deity or Guru as God. A person who desires the love of God may have to endure extreme sorrows. In that situation, one should take comfort that it is to remove the impurities from my sinful power and stand firm in love again and again. Since he can see all sorrow as full of love and nectar, his sacrifice will not seem like a sacrifice when seen. Sorrow must necessarily come. Even if he is convinced that everything he does is good, if his fellow beings accept it, he will not be able to know what sorrow is. Without experiencing some sorrow, one cannot know what sacrifice is. Or suppose he didn't experience sorrow, can he understand another person's sorrow? Then he cannot experience it himself, nor can he know another's sorrow.

[GV3:P2041] The love he has for all living beings will be in accordance with his dedication to God. His asset must be the purity of character not to hate anything and to see everything as love. Experiencing any sorrow and behaving lovingly, transforming into that by realizing and experiencing it. Then the seat of sorrow and the seat of happiness becomes himself. He will be able to see the earth as a bed of flowers. He will be able to see forests full of stones and thorns as flower gardens. Through the feeling he gets when going to such places, he will be able to enjoy all the beauties of the world. Through that, he might have seen God. Thus he will have a full mind to see God in everything. This is what is called sensible intellect. Devotion other than this is for the fulfillment of desires. That devotion will eventually lead to disappointment.

[GV3:P2042] If the pile of desires collapses, there will be sorrow. Even if it is compromising devotion, there will be sorrow. Whether a true devotee or not, there will be many outbursts of crying. In reality, he sees the form of his chosen deity in all moving and non-moving things with all joy. Then unknowingly, due to the love capable of seeing the glory of God, he ends up crying. Thus a devotee who has seen God in various ways will cry out of love. Even if all this happens, when those who lack the knowledge to see love tell unknown stories and cause sorrow, there may also be a cry saying, "These people are causing me sorrow without knowing the truth, isn't God seeing this?"

[GV3:P2043] Guruvani

[GV3:P2044] Santhigiri Janmadinapoojitha Samarppanappathippu - 2001

[GV3:P2045] Experiential Knowledge

[GV3:P2046] I am telling you my experience. There is a difficulty in telling it to deceive. That is why it is told in this manner. If told in any other way, you will not be able to take it. Among those who come to me, there are a few who have known me since childhood.

[GV3:P2047] I was a believer in God. That is all I knew. I know nothing else. I lived at home till the age of fourteen. I had to tell a lie to leave home. There is a custom among us to go to Palani. For that, taking a garland and vow, I wore it. Thus, under the pretext of going to Palani, I left home.

[GV3:P2048] I went straight to ashrams. I involved myself in ashrams in many places. But I didn't get satisfaction from any of this. My entire desire was to have an experience. The Ramakrishna Mission and the Sree Narayana Mission are the two institutions standing strong in Kerala. I was very familiar with both. Then the lineage of Madhva and the lineage of Sree Sankara. They will not be useful for the Shudra class. Because caste is buried there. So there is no salvation there. There is an ashram belonging to the Ramakrishna Mission near my birthplace. I have been associated with it. Eventually, I spent seventeen years associated with the Sree Narayana Mission and its other temples. Not only associated, I also handled some things in its administration.

[GV3:P2049] I observed puja rituals and all other rules in the Sree Narayana Mission. Whatever it is, there is nothing significant to call an experience from this. Except for an experience of two days, no other nature of experience has occurred from there. Therefore, with the desire that I need a Guru, if I hear that a person is sitting somewhere, I would tell some reason and go from there. Finally, I went regularly for ten years to a person who lived like an Avadhuta. I go to this Avadhuta saying that I am going somewhere to the branches of Sivagiri. For name's sake, I would go there once. And at night, I would sit entirely near this person. He lies in the sand on the beach. Even after going for ten years, I got nothing from him. It's not that he doesn't know how to speak, he just didn't speak.

[GV3:P2050] While so, through someone, I found a Sannyasi. As soon as I went, he understood me. But that day he said nothing. Let it be tomorrow, he said. When I went the next day, he asked, "You are the one who came yesterday, right?" He lies in the sand and bushes. He asked me why I came. I gave the answer in one word. Saying "You are deserving," he hugged me and cried. He wrote me a mantra and said I should keep using it, then experience will come, and when the experience comes, I should come here. Experience came, I went. Everything is there but it will take a long time. I didn't understand anything. Even after two and a half years, there was no result. In the meantime, difficulties, troubles, and hardships come pouring in. I don't do anything fitting for that. At the end of the hardships, experience happened. But not clear. Except that it was proven true, I didn't know what this is, or what it is like.

[GV3:P2051] Only if told as it is, you will understand. If told in other ways, it will not be clear to you. Therefore, I am only telling the experience. At least you should pay attention to that. He has a desire to somehow give me an experience. He says it whenever we meet.

[GV3:P2052] One day, a situation arose where I had to leave Sivagiri itself. I hadn't done anything wrong for that. While wandering as an Avadhuta, he came looking for me at Sivagiri. And he told me one word. That was before I left Sivagiri. One day, after the puja, I was coming out with the plate. Someone who knows me came and said that a Swami has come looking for me. I quickly gave the plate and went. As soon as he saw me, he said: I am on a journey from here. You have a change from here. Don't leave without seeing me. He left saying this just like that. I couldn't believe it. Because I have been serving for so long. Without taking a single penny too. How old is the association with Sivagiri. How could I go from there? Or even if told to go, would I have the courage to go?

[GV3:P2053] When there was no choice, I moved from Sivagiri. I built a hut and moved to the hill beyond Sivagiri for seven years and four months. On the fourth month, on the first day of Vrischikam, seven people came. They have come with all kinds of food items. They said, "Today we have a vow here." As they went back and told, ten people came the next day. Thus, within that month itself, nearly a hundred people were coming daily. They say that their diseases and other troubles will be cured. And those who come do see peace. This is the knowledge they told. After going like this for about two and a half years, nothing was effective.

[GV3:P2054] It was at the Thuckalay Munsiff Court that the experience occurred. That was completed in about three years. That is, the entire Trimurti system was completed. All demons, whatever names are mentioned, all are there. What we have heard in this world is very little. What comes in front is many times that, in crores.

[GV3:P2055] All this exists. But what spiritual strength will this give us? When making some offerings, one will get a result for making that offering, but when becoming people with experience, nothing is gained from it. It became clear to me that it comes for their spiritual peace.

[GV3:P2056] Pratyakshavaham Dharma. Appearing visibly and explaining. Because it was taught like that, I was convinced that this is true. After being convinced, it became a question of how to handle this. When handled, it all worked. Not a single letter failed. This result is seen in hundreds of people. I was not the only one handling it. By disease, even those given up by the Medical College came and left cured. Even urinary bladder cancer was cured. Many people came like that. Then this also became ineffective. People started scolding suddenly. I had no idea what it was. I searched through all possible ways. It wasn't clear. Then, the first two lines of the Akhandanamam here were written and shown for two people and spoken as a disembodied voice. You must chant this and strengthen yourselves, otherwise you will face harm. But it was not understood. Since my name comes in this Akhandanamam, I told the children who got it not to chant it. Since it was told to them, they would chant it without my knowledge and without making a sound.

[GV3:P2057] After three years, I moved from this place to the place I am sitting now. I went there only after being told three times not to sit here. There it was said not to sit in a way that two people could see. There is nothing in that place today. It just became barren land. It lies on the slope of the hill like withered grass.

[GV3:P2058] After coming here and a little time passed, many people started coming. Usually, such crowds do not gather at Hindu worship places. But this was a huge crowd indeed. People just started coming like that. Again suddenly nothing worked. I don't know the reason for this. I haven't disobeyed what was told. It was said that it was because of disobeying. If disobeyed, why wasn't it told? The people who came were very many. If necessary, it would be enough to harm the ashram itself. There are big businessmen, there are those coming from great life facilities. They live on this stony floor in mist, rain, sun, and wind. It is for their disease to be cured. There is a small pandal-like structure. There are no other buildings. How many people can lie in this pandal? Everything went into disarray. But these people have faith in me and my actions. Taking a decision, I told them, I will come out only after knowing about this. You must look after these patients. There are mad people, people with stomach aches, and diseases of various kinds. Saying this, I went inside and sat. All these patients got agitated. People with stomach aches screamed terribly. The mad people, since there are no trees here, went to hang from trees in the nearby plot. About sixty of them got agitated like this. Those who ran away were chased, caught, and brought back.

[GV3:P2059] By five o'clock that evening, there was a notification not to eat food. It was told not to even bite water. Two people who were merchants, saying that a rule has come that water shouldn't be drunk now, went to the well, drew water, and drank. After five minutes, what they vomited was poison like green mixture poured out. Swelling also rose in their bodies. When two others went outside, they got two beatings from somewhere unknown. With that, everyone got scared and stayed inside.

[GV3:P2060] When it was five minutes to six that evening, a notification came that I would come and everyone should be ready for prayer. And they were told to eat food. They did so. That day itself, three patients were told to do things a certain way. That disease was cured. Thus in fifteen days, all diseases were cured. After that, I was told to make a journey. In that journey, I went up to Chidambaram, the biggest temple in South India, and then didn't continue the journey. Walking through each area starting from Kanyakumari, finally reaching Chidambaram. Even after going there, there is nothing to give. I decided I would go nowhere anymore.

[GV3:P2061] Whatever has a name in this world exists. If we name and imagine knowingly or unknowingly, that also exists. But we haven't learned where and how to use it. This is what I was able to understand from my knowledge.

[GV3:P2062] Worshipping with the knowledge up to the Trimurti system, and without any difference, I have seen its reflection with physical eyes. Even its sub-deities, ghosts, spirits, and demons are true. But what can it do, what should we do for it? I have thought and realized this. That is, I have thought and realized about the dharma of Kali.

[GV3:P2063] If Kali has a dharma of its own, it belongs to the Shudra. Many Brahma Jnanis came among the Shudras. The first evidence for that is Sree Krishna. It is the Yadava clan, isn't it? The Yadava history is not only spreading among us, but we also keep it without letting go today. Then came the Parayi Petta Panthirukulam. Eighteen Siddhas joined in between. We couldn't see any of this as one and grow in the single system of being Indian in one language or one attire. Standing behind a system that spread in such a way that small gods for small castes and big gods for big castes, unable to stand together and impart, is what Hindus have done till today.

[GV3:P2064] I am telling the qualification that belongs to my karma path, to dharma shastra, to truthfulness. Many others may have many qualifications. I am not denying it. I am not saying it is not so either. Because I saw what the spiritual form from the host of spirits up to the host of stars is, I understood that it is true. I can scientifically prove it by learning whether our form gets severed there. I am telling exactly what I have seen and obtained.

[GV3:P2065] Our spiritual awareness is the ability to see how nine planets, twelve zodiac signs, and twenty-seven stars stand and operate and cause to operate. Otherwise, there is no point in saying it was like that or like this.

[GV3:P2066] One must first study what the special power of the Sun that operates the moon dust falling to Earth is, and what the karmic path of Earth, which is its component, is. This is not what I say by studying. It is seen. If a person comes like that, I can explain it. There is this evidence in this part, and only if asked what to do for that evidence, can I say. Mine is not the knowledge that speaks by codifying scriptures.

[GV3:P2067] We have now got the knowledge to do things like how life paths have operated in various ways up to the host of stars, how to liberate those souls, or if they are to be destroyed, how to destroy them, and if liberation is to be given to a worshipped deity, how to do it. If this knowledge was not there, I would have had to stand along with those who say there is no karmic path and there is no such thing as realization.

[GV3:P2068] Everything is as we imagine. Everything is an imagination, isn't it? That is, Sankalpa Karma Manasa. That is one. Another is, if one was born as a snake in a certain life, what was the nature of that snake, what has come from that snake's life now, how did it come as an animal, what was its functional capacity then, what is the reason for coming up like this in those karmas, what is the reason for saying that humans themselves will return as animals, all aspects of this must be recognized. When knowing and knowing like that, it becomes clear what that soul was. That is how it should be understood. What each one was, what form it had taken, how that atom operates, with what color is it distinguished. This changed into nine. The execution of this nine is all that we see. Just as taking out a little bit of the same color, our thoughts and feelings can also be distinguished.

[GV3:P2069] When distinguishing like this, the light stream of our mind changes and grows. That is not according to the calculations mentioned by yogis. Having nurtured it, I have been able to see what that karmic path is, which grows the Niyabhara bandha (Kundalini power) from the triangular shape to the Sahasrara Padma. It is an executive victory of life. It is only about one person. It is what is said as Kundalini power according to yogic rules. When it comes to Manipuraka, its shape and nature change. By then, all colors would have joined and operated. In this world, all kinds of things and their atomic entries are the subject of our vision. We can see the special operation of these atoms where blood, flesh, marrow, etc. join and operate. It is explained using color itself. Divinity comes only if a certain color is joined. Not in a certain one. There will be an element of darkness mixed in it. In the other, an element of light will be mixed. To say a certain light, a vision must be completed. When a vision is completed, for a state to be completed, when it is known and completed as its gross, subtle, cause, and effect, 7444 visions must come. Thus completing seven is the presence of God. How can this be explained. This cannot be discovered through science. Science has not grown that much. It is not said to deny that science does not exist. Science has not grown. When it comes here, it will collapse. A scientist can only reach the full form of research imagined by a branch of science, right? There was a science in India that thought beyond that, operated it with nine planets, operated in twelve zodiac signs, discovered its calculation and gave it to us, reached the stars and repeatedly taught us the birth qualities that reach us up to its fractional calculations. This is that very calculation being told. It shouldn't be told wrongly.

[GV3:P2070] That karmic path that grew up to the Trimurti system in this way can only be explained by a Guru who is an authority who has risen to the position of Devendra or the position of the lord of Ganas. It is said that Ganapathi has the root authority for this, right? What is the authority of Moola Ganapathi, or how far will the karma of the form called Parashakti go in that position, all these have been subtilized and scientifically realized. Only if this is again subtilized, codified, and known can we be convinced about its evolution. Otherwise, we won't be convinced.

[GV3:P2071] An action I do here to change this: Someone said disease. Is the disease real or not? If so, what is the cause? There is no effect without a cause. When finding out what the cause is, if it came through his karmic path, it is that. If not, if it came through his ancestors, it is that. If not that, if it came through his family worship, it is that. Turning it like this, the action that he needs to do will be made to be done by him. The karma standing completed will be caught here. When catching it, there will be a pain of karma in his soul. It will be in the body too. It will be in his actions too. After that, if he desires for self-realization, he will be told that way. Telling will be useful only if they have the right to it. Even if not, this can be told to a few people. Because they are their desires. When the desire increases, it will affect the karmic path operating inside.

[GV3:P2072] The knowledge here is saying what karmic path is operating in any human being seen. That karma will help in liberation. No doubt. Each of us has a karmic path right from birth. How is that karmic path formed? Shaktheyam, Shaivam, Vaishnavam were worshipped like God, right? Until reaching Advaita, one has to act according to this. Advaita is something else. As long as one sits in Dwaita, what should we do. Can we just say we don't want Dwaita? Dwaita cannot be denied. It can be discarded after that knowledge comes.

[GV3:P2073] Where will the part that people understand as Maya reach? Shouldn't it reach the position called self-illumination? Can it be done just by saying? One has to strive for it. Only the karma of striving can reach that position. Karma must be taken for that. One must decide for oneself what one's own karmic path and dharmic path should be. It won't work if anyone else decides. For that, we need a knowledge center or a research center.

[GV3:P2074] Guruvani

[GV3:P2075] Santhigiri Janmadinapoojitha Samarppanappathippu - 2001

[GV3:P2076] Brahma Resolve through Guru

[GV3:P2077] All the karmas we perform with vows and other rituals have results. It has been informed many times that this is not a lineage with knowledge for that. It is experienced so in the acquaintance till today. The reason for that is that we do not have such spiritual knowledge among us. We have not accepted Siddhi rules or other karma rules. But we believe in karma. Jnanis can untie karma with faith. That is why it is said that self-realized souls do not accept it. There will be only one or two such self-realized souls in the world during our lifetime. Others may be seen in plenty. Though Yogi, Bhaktan, and Karmi may be there, self-realized souls will be few.

[GV3:P2078] Those with some experience tell us about the merits of yoga and karma and give us knowledge. But self-realization begins only after crossing the position of Devendra. Devendra stands in the sixth state. So self-realization begins from the part where the Indra of Gods and the lord of Ganas stand. The decision of Jnanis is that what is up to there cannot be called self-realization. But here we often call someone who has been walking around as a devotee for ten or twenty years a great self-realized soul and a knowledgeable person.

[GV3:P2079] Self-realization is something that lingers only in the knowledge of lifetime after lifetime. Such knowledge means a gathering of all demons, all gods, sages, sannyasis, and jnanis. That is why I said not many with such knowledge come among us. And not everyone can reach that knowledge. If such a great soul comes, how can we recognize him? He will always show love to us in a very grand manner. That love will be with glory in a way we can never forget him. However, the disciplinary nature will not go away either. He might discipline us in every way. But we don't have the knowledge to stand obediently and listen or understand all that. The reason for that is the lack of acquaintance mentioned earlier.

[GV3:P2080] Even if the ignorance of not being able to implement the idea that came to us in our time comes upon us, we should have at least the knowledge to listen to it and accept it. There are many who think that if they mock and despise everything, others will think they are very great. I am not talking about that kind of knowledge. This is very deep.

[GV3:P2081] Although I was dealing with the knowledge gained through twenty-seven and a half years of experience, it was from 1973 onwards that I could retrieve it and obtain it through its wisdom aspect. Before that, the aspects of the Trimurti system were prevalent. From that, I had to experience many issues and hardships. That is because a god or goddess, or even a sub-deity, is highly glorified in the position of Pratyakshavaham Dharmam—even in the Vedas, Puranas, and Upanishads. Vedantins, on the other hand, speak of matters where the experiential aspect of Pratyakshavaham Dharmam has faded. When I had an experience like this, it seemed like a great wonder to those who were with me at that time. Then they became very worried about whether this was a lie or not.

[GV3:P2082] The words told by those under the Trimurtis and the Trimurtis themselves reached a state where we could not use them. It was not known why that happened. We were in a situation where we didn't know whether it was our fault or a flaw in some other aspect. For eleven years since I started dealing with this, I didn't know what was happening. From the eleventh year onwards, I began to understand things one by one. After five years, I could firmly establish the understanding that we wouldn't make a mistake anymore. Yet, very few people have understood this even today. Even among the believers, those who have fully understood this would not even be two percent. This is not a field that can be understood so quickly.

[GV3:P2083] We have been told to take up this subject through our karma. It has been informed that an understanding should be firmly established in all of you, and acting according to that understanding, great souls will be born through your children according to the magnitude of each one's actions. This begins to happen from the third generation onwards. Even the children born now have only a small knowledge about this. Although children born today have more intelligence compared to the past, they have very little knowledge about this.

[GV3:P2084] Birth for the third generation is stated for our Pitrus. When we say Pitrus, great souls standing in various levels like Devas, Rishis, Yogis, and Sanyasis are leaving their Pitruhood and standing ready to claim birth according to Brahma's will. Now, the Pitrus who are standing aside claiming birth are waiting to be born in those of the second generation among the believers who reach full faith. Therefore, you householders must be very careful. The magnitude of the actions you do today cannot be described in words.

[GV3:P2085] We have been told for what purpose these Pitrus are being born. Their activities will start here after twenty years. When this much time passes, there will be a change even in our current administrative system. The children born after 1973 are coming to the position of this knowledge.

[GV3:P2086] Among what was told in the vision, crores of Pitrus (Rishis)—those in the Trimurti system and the Manu lineage—came and said that this should be achieved through karma. Karma means, if we make even a single movement, it falls under karma. That is why it was said that these Pitrus will be born only in those belonging to the second generation who can reach full faith.

[GV3:P2087] Due to the karmic aspects done by our ancestors and the circumstances in which we ourselves are born and brought up, some shortcomings have gripped us. It has risen in the life force (Jiva). When this life force starts functioning, the vasana (tendency) of the life force and the karma of the ancestors combine, making it impossible to complete a karma. It will take a long time to understand this. You lack the tendency to take up karma that stretches this far. Here, there are those who believe the most and those who say they believe but change midway. All of them equally have the belief that "what I do is right." There are even those with us who think, "if what doesn't happen through me doesn't happen, it's nothing." This notion is never right. The attitude you should have is a pleading tone: "I am not up to this, I don't even know how much an entity called 'I' can do in this karma, let me at least try making an effort as small as an ant." Instead, you have the attitude of "I". Along with that attitude, acquiring many life cultures, you stand as a life-tendency ready to bite and die like an ant. How can you, being like that, absorb and achieve anything in this? Therefore, it is impossible for us to take this up together. Then, if we believe and follow, we might be able to achieve a lot. "Oh God, I don't have the ability to do this, at least my lineage should be able to do it"—you must have this dedication.

[GV3:P2088] We have been informed about the growth of this lineage. I mentioned earlier about seeing crores of Rishis. Among them, I saw many Brahmin Pitrus, Brahmarshis, Sanyasis, Yogis, etc. The reason for pointing them out specifically is that this authority, knowledge, or wisdom has not been passed on to the Pitrus in the Varnasrama Dharma. On the day the completion karma started here, they came and pleaded, "You must save us." The reply they got from the Brahmam was, "Let it be, let it be later." When they came again, twenty-five percent of them were merged into the Brahmam. The rest have been kept in the subtle realm. They want to merge through the eligibility we are going to achieve. There are about three lakh such Pitrus standing like this. These three lakhs have come amidst how many crores of other souls.

[GV3:P2089] Looking from the position of knowledge, how did such a great karma come to us, who do not even have the knowledge of a demon standing in the most despicable demonic state? We have no other word for it than Brahma's will. What is the reason for Brahma's will bringing it about this way? The mistake that occurred in the Manu lineage. After the mistake occurred in the Manu lineage, the Trimurti system was brought in. But the Trimurtis could not move forward. In the Trimurti system, it stops at Devendra. Since the aspect of self-knowledge is not there, it cannot be claimed for liberation. Devendra, however, is lying under the curse of man. So, Devendra has only attained a state capable of receiving a man's curse. This proves what state a man can reach if he lives well. Especially in the era of the Shudra.

[GV3:P2090] Shudra means one without knowledge. Kali is the era of this ignorant one. An era where one jumps into anything without any determination, thinking it is supreme knowledge. It is in such an era that we have been born. That Brahma's will had arranged things well in advance for this right to come to the Shudra. In South India, especially in Kerala, except for picking up a few from the Shudra class and making them Shudras according to Varnasrama Dharma, knowledge was not given to others. That is the part where they say the one with physical strength became the manager. Starting from the Ettuveettil Pillamar, it ended with Chithira Thirunal. But except for the lineage of the one who becomes the king, others were not even allowed a place in the feast. That is the Vedic Dharma here. What knowledge will we get from this Dharma? This Shudra who is picked and mentioned has no other knowledge except to eat, dress, carry things around, sing, and perform Thalappoli. When it reached this level, our social knowledge went very low. In addition, if some Puranas are told and Advaita is added to it and interpreted, we are satisfied with it. We are the children of this level of ignorance. It is to such of you that, by grace, birth has been given in the Kali Yuga itself.

[GV3:P2091] Kali is a mixed era. The country itself is mixed. All utility items are mixed. There is a mixture in humans, birds, animals, and plants. A mixture in father and mother. What is being said about those we worship and respect? There is a justification that if Panchali could do it, why couldn't we? But we only know this counting part. We don't know any other things about Panchali. We are mixed offspring living impurely by doing chaotic karma in this manner. No matter who says what, a pure life will not come to this mixed race. If told, it won't enter the head. A habit of achieving goodness by mocking, laughing at, and insulting when told stands with us. From this state, ours is a path given according to that Brahma's will to become pure. A path of karma where one can reach by oneself the level where Pitrus, Devas, Rishis, Sanyasis, Yogis, and Jnanis stand. Make one eligible for birth, give liberation to the one eligible for liberation and keep him merged in Brahmam, give us the authority to perform karma for the pure birth of children to be born hereafter—this is the path. You are the ones standing in the initial form of this path. Now, the householders in this lineage have the authority for this.

[GV3:P2092] Those who believe in this should have a lineage of our own. Your Pitruhood is being lost by attaining liberation. The karma to give liberation to everything from the God and Goddess we worshiped up to the Brahmarakshas has come into our hands. This is our karma.

[GV3:P2093] You have been made the authority of a purifying karma in this state. But you need the knowledge to firmly establish that understanding, think broadly, and grasp that karmic eligibility. Today, we can only pass on that knowledge to our successors. Do not think that anything else can be done. Or, if an opportunity for that is obtained, it is the will of the Brahmam.

[GV3:P2094] Who will be the child grasping this knowledge? It is said to be the birth of a Guru who led crores of people. Children blessed with the fortune to give birth to such a great soul are born to us. We need karmic purity for that today. For that, you are working closely together. Never have the ego that "I am great". You should only think, "I am nothing, I am merely a participant in a karma that involves all of us." Only then can you think that there is goodness in you. We are all nothing. We should only have a sense of dedication that "we will do as much as we can."

[GV3:P2095] There is another reason for showing the Brahmin Pitrus mentioned earlier like that. For them to get release from the bondage of the Deva, it can happen only through the Dharma of a Guru. This Guru does not belong to anything like a Deva, Rishi, or Sanyasi. A Sanyasi attains liberation and leaves. A Rishi becomes the authority of some matter and moves away. A Jnani says all this is good. They are all Gurus indeed. But this Guru is not that. The Guru who gives liberation to all these Rishis and Sanyasis. That is its meaning.

[GV3:P2096] This knowledge itself will cause you a headache. Our need is to acquire this knowledge. This is knowledge. Where do we stand in the aspect of that knowledge? We have got an opportunity to call out "Oh God" without knowing any of this. Therefore, everyone should call out together with sincerity.

[GV3:P2097] What we have seen in the world today is nothing. We have to reach a much higher level than all of that. It will certainly come. An intimation for that was received like this; we are a primeval lineage. The Manu's lineage and the Trimurti lineage are unifying according to Brahma's will, and the creation of another lineage is beginning. This is a small sprout of that Brahma's will. You have received the initial karma of the functioning of that sprout. When karmic purity is attained without discrimination, knowledge arises from it, eligibility is acquired from that knowledge, and when fortune favors, all this will arrive at that position. We must be careful until that fortune favors. Do not become arrogant just because you got some knowledge or become defiant because you acquired some wealth. Those are all qualities that we need to remove from ourselves.

[GV3:P2098] Guruvani

[GV3:P2099] Santhigiri Janmadinapoojitha Samarpana Pathippu - 2001

[GV3:P2100] Importance of Pournami Prayer

[GV3:P2101] We have an acquaintance of not knowing why the Pournami fast and prayer are observed. The Sun, Moon, and Earth—these three planets are associated with Pournami. The earth to complete karma enters the karmic field due to the movements of life that underwent evolution by the power of the Sun. You need to become acquainted with an understanding that tells you what to do and what not to do in this karmic field. Otherwise, do not disrespect these truthful words spoken for your knowledge towards a karma that destroys you by telling and enforcing fragments of Vedanta.

[GV3:P2102] The result of Pournami prayer is exceptionally noble in a way that cannot be diminished by anything else. It is on Pournami that one can know the purity of intention of many births and complete it by acting upon it. The Pournami fast is a fast where the benefits of all fasts come together. By practicing all the virtues of this fast, one can become a Guru. It is also on Pournami that this Guru attains liberation. This is its spiritual aspect. Similarly, for the worldly aspect too, it is on Pournami that one can attain completeness.

[GV3:P2103] The special feature of Pournami is that darkness does not spread even a little. On one side, joy and coolness, on the other side, the quality to capture anything. Seeds get more benefit in the coolness. That quality has the ability to generate and sustain it.

[GV3:P2104] If one attains Samadhi by acquiring merits through many births, that Samadhi has completeness. On Pournami, the atmosphere is calm. There is no gap between sunrise and sunset. When one rises, the other sets. When one sets, the other rises. Wherever the rays of the Sun and Moon fall, all the above-mentioned benefits also enter. Pournami helps the Guru reach completeness as Ishwara and Brahmam. Whether you think about the benefits of Pournami prayer or not, you should resolve that our faults should all be destroyed so that all living and non-living beings get benefits, and distinguishing between good and evil, our prayer should bring benefits in a way that contributes to world peace.

[GV3:P2105] Planets will attain equilibrium, and through that, a special power beneficial for world peace will emerge. Pournami is the most suitable day for creation and dissolution. Let that creation be one that benefits the whole world. Just as this state is seen in imagination in a mango blossom, it functions similarly in human beings too. Although the spiritual and the material are mutually complementary, whichever we give priority to will grow.

[GV3:P2106] The mother giving birth to a child on Pournami should be conscious. She should know that this is for the world and that it has been ordained like this from God.

[GV3:P2107] We have gone by respecting what currently exists. When we came through the path of the ancestors, some obstacles arose. After that, a deviation was made. You can ask how this was obtained despite respecting what exists. It is difficult to answer that. We don't think about how those who came before were. But we adopted such a path. How this happened is a subject to be pondered upon. Sivananda called Hatha Yoga as Raja Yoga. But Raja Yoga is none of this. True Raja Yoga is something one falls into unknowingly due to the good deeds of past births. For example, Sri Krishna. Krishna did not reject anything. He reinforced what existed until then and showed that he is none of this. Krishna acted desiring a karma suitable for the times. What has been done so far has value. But he is standing by stepping outside of it. Krishna is a perfect Jnani. It was as a result of reading the Gita that Karl Marx told to behead. But the essence of the soul was lost. An ordinary person's intellect should not function in things done by visionaries.

[GV3:P2108] We have been able to learn one thing from the great souls who came and went. Most of them have rejected the past path. But to be able to see what the old is, one must come through the old itself. As far as we are concerned, we do not need the old. Because God gave us a new path. We received this path through a disembodied voice. It will only say, do this at this time. We couldn't know everything in advance. As God, we are being redirected from time to time. We are redirected through intimations.

[GV3:P2109] Mistakes must be corrected in the Kali Yuga. And then let into the Satya Yuga. What sequence can we give to this correction? So far, what has been done is only on a small scale. More is to be done hereafter. Even so, I have been seen as a denier. A nature for this has arisen from that imagination itself.

[GV3:P2110] From the words "The thing I desired for ages has been completed today," it can be understood that this is an imagination that is direct and existed earlier.

[GV3:P2111] Now, how should this mode of worship be carried forward? Those who created each mode of worship did it after asking and knowing how it should be. We also do the same. We have another uniqueness too. We were given this method of worship without asking. Even then, I should not do it by myself. Those who ask, do, and make others do this have a responsibility. That is why I have said that householders cannot do this. But householders who live like Rishis can do this. That householder must be one with the determination that he will step back only after reaching a certain stage.

[GV3:P2112] How and who should implement this idea? Anyway, I am not the one to implement this. Even if one asks, many know, and some think, it can be implemented only according to the karmic capacity of the one who is going to implement it. This cannot be implemented just by mere belief. What all should be included in what is called belief? There is a need for an understanding about belief. A word is uttered after it rises in the mind. That word must be put into practice. Belief can end up in the Ashta Ahankaras (eight egos). That is, the attitude of "I" will always stand out. Only one who can transform these Ashta Ahankaras into Ashta Aiswaryas (eight wealths) can put this into practice. We should be able to impart the love and affection of one who is so pure. You can work closely with his karmic capacity. Only one who acquires the Ashta Aiswaryas, turns around, thinks what is needed, asks, and acts can do this. Anyway, the path of karma must be pure for this. Even if one acquires the eight Siddhis, one must be able to maintain honesty. Disregarding honor and dishonor, one must stand as a seeker of truth.

[GV3:P2113] It was mentioned earlier that doing such a prayer here is according to an intimation. At the time of doing this prayer, at the time of doing this prayer that resolves the mistakes of a long time, you must remember that if even a slight scratch of ignorance enters inside, it will spread. It will spread in many ways. We are doing the prayer to rectify the anomalies that have occurred in all the actions of householders, as well as in all the actions of Sanyasis and Brahmacharis. This is not something according to your mind. Therefore, some ignorance and mistakes might occur. We are being made to do a special thing at a distance that no one can say by anyone, there is nothing else to say about it except that it is God's will. You should always think, "May God grant that the shortcomings of myself and my ancestors over time, not only that, but the overall shortcomings of the people of the world be removed, and in its place, this Guru and the Guru lineage given to us by God be able to impart goodness of double weight." The children you raise and grow, and the youngsters standing here taking Sanyasa are equally participants in this noble karma. You must also think how it should be done if a path of redemption is to come through the karma you all do together. Whatever work you do, you should always think that not only through Sanyasis and their lineage but wherever in the world shortcomings have occurred, all those should be destroyed and in that place, goodness according to Brahma's will should come through this Guru lineage. When studying children do not get an understanding in their minds, you should pray for goodness to come in this way to all the children who have gone astray, not just the children of this lineage.

[GV3:P2114] This is the word God told us. Boys and girls must be given special attention and prayed for separately. The karma coming from me, a woman, should correct the mistake of the woman in this world. The man should also think like this. That should be your eligibility. This eligibility of men and women is advancing together here. At that time, our minds should not become bad, nor should we fall into situations that make the mind bad. Especially the men and women growing here are the ones who have to make judgments for the world. It is with this sense of Dharma that we should become great. Standing in this Dharma, our prayer always should be, "Clear all the mistakes that have come traditionally not only in this solar system but also through other solar systems, and give us the knowledge, acquaintance, and experience to do what is needed when needed." When we have that knowledge and God tells and makes us do it, we should be ready to do it. Apart from this, thoughts like rubbish lying on the road should not be allowed to enter the mind. If this prayer is done on a Pournami day, it will yield full results.
