[GV3:P2116] Santhigiri Janmadinapoojitha Samarpana Pathippu - 2001

[GV3:P2117] Historical Event through the Aspect of Vision

[GV3:P2118] Let us look at the lives of great people born after the beginning of Kali to see whether they experienced happiness or sorrow. Gandhiji was a great man born as a materialist. Gandhiji had no spiritualism. Congressmen have acted as great spiritualists. But we have not been able to see Gandhiji having such a history. We were neither able to take up that sacrifice, nor participate in that Dharma, nor get the aspect of that merit. This is the truth.

[GV3:P2119] A misfortune has befallen us that we cannot give contemporaneity to and develop people who have embraced a life sequence of so much sacrifice. What we say about Mahatmaji is that he was a person with a spiritual side. Let it remain so. We shouldn't try to change and say otherwise. Because Mahatmaji taught that Rama Rajya should come. When speaking about Rama, which Rama should we take? In Sanskrit grammar books, we first learn how many Ramas came. Where do we stand in the karma that says 'Ramo Rama Ramaha cha Kalki Janardana'?

[GV3:P2120] To impart goodness in our country, God took the Vamana avatar and came. He stepped on an Asura who ruled the country well and pushed him down to the netherworld. Why did he step on him and push him down? Because he was an Asura. There is no question as to why the Asura ruled well. It is also not known why demonic nature was imparted. Going into the intricacies of such fictional stories without any information, whom will we ask and understand what?

[GV3:P2121] We have not been able to understand the contemporary history of Mahabali and Vamana. For what, for whom did they do all this? Without knowing any such thing, how will we detach and remove the misfortunes and untruths that have crept into the karma we have been praising and glorifying till today? If our children ask us about this nature, what will we tell them? This is the biggest misfortune concerning us.

[GV3:P2122] Here is where we should see Sri Krishna as a historian or a Yogeswara. What does Yogeswara mean? We praise Sri Krishna, saying 'Yatra Yogeswara Krishna'. So, what is the point of praising the author of the Gita, the Yogeswara Krishna, like this today when he came and we were ignorant? If someone asks you about Sri Krishna's end, what will you say? You will say that Krishna didn't die like that, he was consumed in the yogic fire. Then it's enough, right? We all got salvation. We are enjoying ourselves by babbling stories and asserting a lack of Dharma in this manner.

[GV3:P2123] You should not study or teach about Sri Krishna like that. We cannot move forward without unknowingly thinking, knowing, acting, suffering, or in some way understanding that man to the extent of his karma and sacrifice. For us to be able to know about such a matter, we need the desire, persistence, detachment, and love to know about the state of some karma that aligns with truth.

[GV3:P2124] What is Vairagya (detachment)? It is the reverence of love. Vairagya should not be studied in the sense of enmity. We must become detached ones of love. There are certain things in us that show how we can become detached ones of love. We must be able to learn to take up the karma that should be taken up out of this love. That can only be done through a renunciate. What kind of person should that renunciate be? Here we must see Mahatmaji as a renunciate. Not because he taught anything else. That man is indeed a renunciate. Even after getting kicked by British soldiers, he didn't take it as a big deal. How were we able to learn from that wealth of sacrifice he showed before us?

[GV3:P2125] What could we learn from Mahatmaji's love? We couldn't adopt that discipline of detachment. It is shown before us that there are rulers, administrators, or people acting in its name who grew up in that lineage that didn't teach us to take that love and discipline of detachment in their true magnitude. How can we adopt the model of Mahatmaji's sacrifice that they show? We never can.

[GV3:P2126] If history has shown contemporaries of sacrifice, no lineage has been able to adopt it. Without that, if we study the history written by people who are exhausted talking about rationalism or liberation-ism supporting and opposing it, how far will we go? Therefore, no one should talk about liberation-ism, no one should say any nonsense, it won't work.

[GV3:P2127] What is the karma we need to achieve in our lifetime? Without knowing at least this much—what sacrifice is there in that karma, what hardship is there in that sacrifice, and how that hardship should be experienced—we cannot become worthy of worship. This is what is called history. The one walking before us as a great renunciate today hasn't been able to achieve anything in this state. Looking at it that way, none of our ancestors are models for us.

[GV3:P2128] What does the Prophet's prayer in the graveyard during his last days show? A true Muslim cannot say that prayer doesn't exist. Whatever karmas were used to study about the renunciates, all of them have become subjected to something else and corrupted. For example, there is a song indicating God's love, "Who knew God's love, oh mind." Yes, who knew God's love? Will God come if you show it by singing, saying, playing, making others play, stomping feet, beating, and jumping high?

[GV3:P2129] So, no more evidence is needed to show that we are bad in every way. If a change is to come to all this, I am telling you something known to me regarding what we should do. That is my knowledge. I don't know how you will understand it.

[GV3:P2130] In the karma that speaks of Sri Krishna's sense of Dharma, see that Dharma called Pratyakshavaham Dharmam perceptibly. Not the empiricism spoken by current poets and intellectuals. What do these empiricists say about the sunrise? It burst open. That is, it's a bursting sun here. If these miscreants could lean such empiricism on your ignorance, where are you standing! The sun cannot burst or open up like that. We all know that. If you are taught about empty space and non-empty space in a karmic course spinning within these nine planets, will you move away from this karmic course?

[GV3:P2131] What is Pratyakshavaham Dharmam? Krishna asks Arjuna, why aren't you killing them? I cannot fight and kill them. Do you trust me? Definitely. Then why aren't you obeying my word? It is only in the act of killing that I cannot obey. You sit and think. So he sits in the chariot itself and thinks. When he sat with closed eyes in that chariot, what did he see? There is no harm in killing them. So, is this how God comes? According to the logic of these empiricists and you, will God come like this? What are you saying—that one shouldn't cause pain even to an ant. How can you, thirsting for this compassion, kill this? How do Jain theoreticians walk? Tying a cloth over their mouths. How does one who says even germs shouldn't be killed live here? So will God shout that you and I are great sinners? How many living beings do we chop, boil, and eat. Saying "We won't eat these living beings," if you cut and eat the sprouts of these seeds, eating and eating its leaves and peels, isn't that taking a life?

[GV3:P2132] What respect are you going to establish with this Advaita philosophy? If you are convinced of that matter seen by Arjuna, it will be good for you. If you want to say that you are not killing anyone, nor making anyone kill, from where would you have seen it to say that? Whether it is true or false, or a writing from intellect, it is a direct perception. That is why he starts the war by praying to Krishna. To Bhishma, who is lying on a bed of arrows after fighting, Krishna asks, you could have gone before this, why are you lying like this? Then Bhishma says, let this go Krishna ٹ.., this will go. When we look at the historical truth of Bhishma and Drona saying this, we can see who they are. What is the reason for Bhishma and Drona falling like that? Or, in a karma path where there are disciple and guru, why did Sri Krishna stand like this and get them killed? Why did he kill?

[GV3:P2133] If you get an opportunity to think about this, after thinking, an experience of the history that came and went and is coming and going in this human Dharma should be available to you as Pratyakshavaham Dharmam. Again, what is Pratyakshavaham Dharmam? That fortune to see the realms where our ancestors' lives are stuck without progress. Other than that, it has no other justifications.

[GV3:P2134] If one holds firmly onto the things Sri Krishna saw and taught and thinks about it, Krishna himself has shown again that those things are dangerous. It is that karma that ultimately became the reason for Krishna to fall over and cry and even for Krishna himself to say, there is no escape now, at least take these women and children and save them. When all these karmas stand in this state, who can firmly hold on to it and do what?

[GV3:P2135] The words of the Gita are a perfect example of Pratyakshavaham Dharmam. But we have already seen what finally happened because of that perception. So what kind of perception do we need now? That perception must be obtained in Jagrat (waking), Swapna (dreaming), and Sushupti (deep sleep) states. This shouldn't become mere storytelling. Even if most of you couldn't see it, there are a few among you who come as authorities for it. You have Jagrat, Swapna, and Sushupti in your experience. But it is a few people who see the history of the respective times in these states. What is the meaning of what was said? This is the karmic result you get to be close to me.

[GV3:P2136] We are taught what Sri Krishna is and what he did. Similarly, we are also taught what the Trimurtis, which is its previous state, are. If it is said that the learned one who pinched off Brahma's head was here, it makes us think what that karma is. Who pinched off Brahma's head? Why is Subrahmanya, who pinched off the head of the Guru and God, being worshiped? It is the heads of the father, mother, Gurus, and ancestors that were pinched off through pinching Brahma's head. That is, they all have only three heads now.

[GV3:P2137] There are people here who have told and served stories like this. This is not what I am telling you. Pratyakshavaham Dharmam. Making it perceptible and seeing it before you. It is the children here who see it. What do they see? What is Krishna, what is Shiva, what is Vishnu, what is Brahma, what is Guru, what is God—lifting all this and showing them. This is Pratyakshavaham Dharmam.

[GV3:P2138] You must bring it perceptibly to a karmic path that shows what Subrahmanya does to the one whose head was pinched and the one whose head was not. This is vision. A Dharma that settles things with karma after ending up speaking mere rhetoric is not our subject. We shouldn't do that karma; we don't need it.

[GV3:P2139] God has given us an opportunity to completely achieve this. So that you have no doubts about it, I am showing the children here who have the vision before you. Through them, we can know. All the past ancestors and great souls must come before them who know and speak like this. They should come and tell them what the truth in their life history is. They must ask the Guru whether the history they tell is true. Those truths and untruths can be determined through the Guru. Only in this way can even the past historical truths be known.

[GV3:P2140] So there is a karma here that can show your children in this state. Are there any other children in this country who can show you in this state? Or is this telling a lie? You yourselves would give an example to know this—that gold should approach a touchstone. The purity can be known only if gold is rubbed on a touchstone. It won't work if ground on a grinding stone.

[GV3:P2141] The work done here is not for a caste, religion, land, region, or country. For example, Jesus will appear before a child belonging to the Hindu religion who has the vision here. Like that, people belonging to all classes and religions will come. There are so many people among the believers here who see and know this truthfully. However, they cannot look at and tell the history of great souls. Only the children standing here can do that. You should know this through those who can know it like this. Having known, desire to serve it through your effort and love. This is what I have to tell you. This grace of God should be available to men, women, rogues, mahouts, drummers, and everyone, if they are God's children. There is no rule that only so-and-so will get it. So, everyone must think what they should become if they are to be God's children. This is its aspect.

[GV3:P2142] First, you must see in Jagrat, Swapna, and Sushupti. You should not move away from history or truth. Just as you see in the waking state, you must see similarly in dream, deep sleep, and Turiya. Only after that should the journey towards Turiyatita be made. You have got the fortune to this karma. You should think about what to do to reclaim that fortune. Thinking about this, knowing and acting, you must strive to reach the consciousness of truth, no matter what we lose. That should be your persistence, your karma, your discipline of detachment. That should be the truth you foster.

[GV3:P2143] If Jesus could be beaten like this, people will come to beat and kill you too. Those who say 'I cannot die' need not come to this. That is, you must get the subtle bodies of past great souls and ancestors. You must find out where in the atmosphere, in which realms, that subtle body stands. What is the shape of the realm from which it controls you? What all happens there? How will you be taken to the story happening there? The one who tells and gives you this is your Guru.

[GV3:P2144] Guruvani

[GV3:P2145] Santhigiri Janmadinapoojitha Samarpana Pathippu - 2001

[GV3:P2146] The Mistake that Happened to India

[GV3:P2147] What is the state of a Shaiva, what is the state of a Vaishnava, what is the state of a Shaktheya? All these are the same state itself. One gives priority to Shakthi. One gives priority to Shiva, another gives it to Vishnu. This is the only difference. Other than this, there is no aspect of state transformation. If the one seeing is to have a change of state, if he has to ascend a state, he must get all three. Otherwise, let alone what he catches, the rest will lie there. All three must go together. When he catches his resolve and goes there, a story is set there in such a way that he cannot move forward.

[GV3:P2148] If one follows one path, Shaiva, Shaktheya, and Vaishnava should be obtained together. The lack of possibility to get it like that is India's biggest ruin. Although Parashurama Gotra, Vishwamitra Gotra, Shankara Gotra, and many others are mentioned, all of them are contained within the Trimurtis. When speaking of the purity of gotra, all this must be contained in it. That is, the four classes of Brahmin, Kshatriya, Vaishya, and Shudra should be equally contained. If not contained, when the aspect of gotra purity comes, all this will separate. This mistake of separating in this manner is what happened in India.

[GV3:P2149] At the time the vision comes, when the Vaishnava's vision comes, only that matter will be told. This Guru cannot tell the matters of the Shaiva and the Shaktheya. There is no point in a Vaishnava saying Vaishnava is great, a Shaiva saying Shaiva is great, and a Shaktheya saying Shaktheya is great. What is the point in saying 'Mine is great, the others are fine, they are good'? When the Trimurti system is completed, if the Guru is not one who has attained the position of Devendra, the position of Parashakthi, and the position of the Ganas, how will the disciple get it?

[GV3:P2150] Because it has not been taught in this way, we do not know the correct aspect of the Manvantara. Because the knowledge about the Manvantara has come in a wrong way, these three sects in India have turned around and are fighting among themselves. Therefore, we Indians were unable to stand together. As a result, the Asian continent itself broke into several pieces due to natural calamities. If we stand divided and act like this, each time an era changes, a new danger will arise.

[GV3:P2151] An example of this is the planet Jupiter. Jupiter is a large planet, isn't it? It is said that a comet crashed into it, and its face changed. In another time, it will become a place lying in ruins. In this way, even events that happened two crore years ago are obtained here. If matters from crores of years ago are to be obtained, the correct knowledge about the Manvantara must be obtained. But the mistake of India, as they say, is that Gurus couldn't know the Manvantara and go along that path.

[GV3:P2152] When the Asian continent lay together, some people climbed over here. They lived in the manner of this place. When this split into different countries, some people remained here. They learned mantras and tantras and created the Vedic religion. The Vedic person became the authority here. This is the reason why the people here do not unite. These people won't touch and get close, won't touch and take. Thus, distances increased, and people moved apart from each other.

[GV3:P2153] The Trimurti system was carried forward in a way that they wouldn't get close to each other. Nothing included in the four-faced karma existed anymore. It is said that the four-faced one created the four Vedas. It was said that Brahma created the four Vedas. There is no history about that Brahma. If there is to be a history, the Manvantara must come. When knowledge about the Manvantara disappeared, knowledge about Brahma disappeared. In the Trimurti system, Brahmam was conceptualized in the place of Brahma.

[GV3:P2154] Brahmam should be contained in everything, right? Brahma is not like that; it is only in the creative karma. However, they made people believe that Brahma is above everything. But Brahma is not above everything. To tell the story of Brahma, Manu is needed. There is no knowledge about Manu either. Then history said it was given to Ikshvaku, the son of Surya. In that history, there was no place for Manvantara. Thus, we went into error. Dividing into the four gotras of Brahmin, Kshatriya, Vaishya, and Shudra, Vedic religion came.

[GV3:P2155] When vision comes in this manner, it will be seen like a kind of smoke. In matters cleared like that, one must pay attention to what the Guru says. If you do not pay attention like that, you will go back to the path of error you went and err again. Then you will leave from here. If you leave, what comes next will be aimless.

[GV3:P2156] When coming here, the vision clears due to the grace of the Guru. Only such a Guru can distinguish and tell the parts of the Trimurtis and the position of the Ganas. When starting to clearly distinguish and see each of these, if one thinks they should go somewhere and say something there, they will lose their way. That part of losing the way will not be understood. You assume that the Paramatma is likely to take form and come. If the Paramatma takes form, how will you recognize it?

[GV3:P2157] Believer: It is the taking form of the six parts that is called Bhagavan. It is Sri Krishna who is seen and spoken of as omnipresent. Because Krishna is the highest among those who have come so far. Krishna is also the Paramatma.

[GV3:P2158] Guru: Yes. But it doesn't end there. It is true that Krishna is the foremost among those who have come till then. But that was a little before the beginning of Kali. Therefore, we were not given the Kali Dharma. We couldn't correct people in that Dharma either. One must ascend from the eighth position to correct the Kali Dharma. There, only Arjuna was corrected and led. Only Arjuna turned. Yet, there is fear about how to kill Gurus and elders. That is when it is asked, don't you believe me? I believe. Yet the fear is not relieved. Then Krishna says; they are neither being killed, nor made to kill. Being taught in this state, that is, the Dharma of Kali being taught like this, has come out wrong. Yuga Dharma was also not made to be done. People like Drona and Bhishma are the ones killed. They are scholars and born in royal families. They wrote wrongly and stopped at the Trimurtis.

[GV3:P2159] Only after passing the star group and reaching the eighth position should one even think about the karma to go to the Paramatma. One shouldn't say Paramatma. Not the soul of the Param, Krishna reached the part of the Paramatma. Did not come in karma. To bring him into that karma, standing as a Guru, he was making his disciple Arjuna do it.

[GV3:P2160] Sri Krishna was born in the eighth position. To ascend from that, it must be shown from the beginning to Arjuna. The karma to cross that position must be done through the disciple Arjuna. After that, the next position is completing the Navagrahas (nine planets). In this, eight were also completed. The form ended there. The color of that formless sky is given to Krishna. Going to the Navagrahas, then the disciple must be made to do it. That is, having seen the ninth planetary position, Krishna should teach Arjuna regarding that position. But when Krishna reached here, the demons and Devas all together drove him away without letting him stay here. He must stand on the other side, acquiring everything from the eighth position where he was born, and advise the disciple. Up to eight has been advised to Arjuna as well.

[GV3:P2161] Kurukshetra was shown the position of God. It was shown entirely through Kurukshetra war. Then form was lost. Later it was only colour of sky. In that sky Sree Krishna stands spread. Arjuna has gone seeing Krishna standing spread in eight and accepting him as Guru. But did not reach nine. Disciple should function ascending nine. Guru should not function there. Form went passing through nine planets, twenty seven stars and twelve zodiac signs completing this. Forms must be joined as eight, nine and ten in that sky. This is what is called tenth condition. In nine it is merging. There is no merging in tenth state. That itself becomes. There is no colour.

[GV3:P2162] Though some people coming here understand this condition here, they are unable to take this. That is the defect of their destiny. They will be looking at that old one itself. This is the trouble if the way of Guru is not correct. They will be moving in that old form itself. They will be turning this colour and this form like this. This is what was shown to Arjuna. Sree Krishna said, these people - Dronar, Bheeshmar and Salyar etc are the people who destroy the position which you and I were in the past. You are not killing any of them, nor are you making them to be killed. This is its justice.

[GV3:P2163] Anybody can tell the external nature of renunciation. Some people will also have vision. But how many people can see and tell clearly? Till today there has been nobody to tell clearly the shape of the stages, states and change of states of vision. Whether you are a monk or whoever, you must go to Guru to ask and understand the difference in state of vision. Otherwise the state which you have reached will not pass. It will only turn around there. You will desire to go there, to go here etc. Wherever you go, you will not get. Even if you lie without drinking water for days, you will not get.

[GV3:P2164] Vivekananda is saying what he has not got clearly because he has not got a Guru in that state. This is not clear to Jesus and Prophet. Not clear to Buddha. Going begging with hand is the proof that it is not clear. Whatever is eaten must be digested. Poets have written that Nirvana has been reached. How do these poets know what Nirvana is? It is the imagination of the poets to say that Buddha is greater than Sree Krishna and to give him the status of Nirvana. He did not come knowing. So Buddha told the children here that it was a mistake.

[GV3:P2165] Guru stands as Paramatma in the karma (action) got after leaving form. Paramatma is seen in this Guru. What is the soul of that Param should be known through Guru. There is no other knowledge. This is Paramabrahman. It is difficult to get a Guru who has become Paramabrahman. That Guru will sit with everybody. Guru is sitting with whatever is good and bad. It is devotion that such a Guru sends to karma.

[GV3:P2166] Vedanta, having been studied, and the karma mentioned in the horoscope being combined by a person will not result in understanding this. Guru is Brahman, says Vedanta. Once the position of Brahman is reached, what and who the Guru is, is what must be investigated next. Arjuna had vision, didn't he? That is what Sri Krishna said: look who I was before, who you were, and what you and I were. It was after showing this that Sri Krishna made Arjuna fight the war. After the war, Parashakti, the devas, and everyone else worked together to prevent moving past the eighth state. Then Dwaraka sank. That is when it was said, there is no escape anymore, Krishnaٹٹ

[GV3:P2167] If you understand the way here, obey it and then go in the old traditional way, you will perish in such a way that you will not even know where you will reach. I am talking about a subject which not even a single individual who has come so far has been able to know. Your intention should be to realize this. This is given to you by God. As long as you do not understand this, you will not be able to bring up your children by making them obey. If you are the people who have understood this, are you not the people who should search for this and work it out? Is this something to be tied in any caste or religion and watered inside their fort? No individual should work in this thinking like that. Never do it, evil will come.

[GV3:P2168] Understanding this, you have been given by God as the easiest way to complete the dharma of life and to individualize it, by passing it on to your tradition. Through it, the children of this Bharath should become a model for the house, country and the world. What has happened in India today instead of that? Since it is impossible to live here today, some people have moved out of India itself and become tigers and cats saying that they have done something and completed it to uplift the world. You must impart the knowledge capable of bringing up your children properly so that this tiger and cat do not jump on the back of your children. Through those children, it will be the cause for the liberation and existence of our country.

[GV3:P2169] This is the message we have got. If you do this as much as possible, doing and making others to do it in the way you decide, without telling anybody who should do it and without insisting that it should be like this, that action will surely be useful to the world. You must become fully capable to take up this action which is going to be useful to the world as God's will. Through that let what the people of the world have to get, be got as India's gift. It should be passed on through our little children. The need of today is not to give it through Upanishads which are said to be the source of Indian knowledge or through recitation of Vedas or through suktas prescribed by saints. You have to go and search for this where you have to search and suffer.

[GV3:P2170] Your ancestors are not virtuous. Or if a person has come as rich, children will be born capable of cutting the head of that wealth. This is what we see completely among us today. If there are seven children, and if some of them fail in any subject, seeing the parents weeping, it will seem as if these children who are to uplift the whole world have failed in a subject and they are feeling the pain! I have a sorrow that we do not have mother or father to think that let my children be able to use properly what has been earned by the virtuous results of my ancestors, which the poor people living without being able to learn anything and without fate for food for one time did not get, and let my children get at least this much. When they are ready for the buffoonery of being satisfied by showing unparalleled sorrow if a child fails in a subject, where will the children they bring up stand? I would only say that this is the complete form of ugliness and nonsense. You should not become parents who destroy the country by making the children who do not have this aptitude to suffer in this way, teaching them unnecessarily and making them useless for the country, incendiaries and robbers.

[GV3:P2171] The customary practices you show are a severe evil like this. This is the cause for your destruction. It is a severe crime you are doing to your house. It is an action which not only destroys the house, but also sows destruction for the country. Today the educated are doing robbery more than the uneducated. The education coming to your children is the one which invites destruction by imparting very bad aptitude. You should give your children only what they have aptitude for. If what they do not have aptitude for is given, bad influence is injected in him. Thus do not allow him to take up the ownership of the evil of karma.

[GV3:P2172] Like the life of a wandering monk who does not know how to dress, eat, drink and bathe, there is an Avadhootha life. Like wandering merged in God reminding that, your children drink, excrete, vomit and walk through many unhygienic conditions making them useless for anything, grow up as mischievous before this world, and wherever they reach, they go there and learn to show grimaces. Giving a lot of weight to this ignorance, you have reached the action of giving bribe even to admit the children in nursery. Going from there to other things to study a little and grow bad aptitude is with a mean action which has imbibed an idea capable of pushing down everything. Even if he comes to the Ashram, what will he be doing? He will not be working to take the liberation of danger, but to take up the action which invites danger.

[GV3:P2173] You who desire to come to the action called Ashram dharma should think that it is not only to clear my defects, but also to clear the defects in the action done by my ancestors. Let me have liberation if there is, let there not be if there is not. But let this country not be destroyed through my children acquiring this ignorance. At least that much love should be there in your action. I will not ask to renounce here without this. When the occasion and qualification to renounce comes, you can renounce. Never give up that desire. Do not prevent that desire. There is no use in preventing. I am not talking about the action standing around renunciation. You do not renounce. You become the children of our country. The dharma of the children who keep constant vigil about this country coming into your life, you should live for the action capable of attaining liberation for your children without your knowing. Doing and not doing that life, you can do it according to your knowledge, experience and habit. There is no use in my telling that too. Because I am convinced that you cannot be made to do it by loving and fondling you. But I have done what I have to do. I have worked so that the family defect sticking in the action of each one of you and the ignorance sticking in it do not pass on to your children. You yourself should manifest into the dharma to copy it reliably and believe that this is the action I have done so that the ownership right of that action comes to you. For that God has entrusted this world itself to you. I am issuing a good news entrusting this to you with great desire.

[GV3:P2174] Guruvani

[GV3:P2175] Santhigiri Janmadinapoojitha Samarppanappathippu - 2001

[GV3:P2176] Gurukula Brahmaniveditham

[GV3:P2177] There was a time when the world had no realization about Brahma nischayam or the glory of God. Even now it does not mean that knowledge is complete. Except that the world got the opportunity to do only the things told from Brahman from time to time, the world could not take that knowledge. Complete realization about Brahma nischayam was there only for six Manvantharas and three Chathuryugas. Sathyathranan was the great man who came at the end of the third Chathuryuga. Though he knew that he was the heir of Brahma nischayam, the secret of his own life was not fully told to him till his end.

[GV3:P2178] I am saying here what has been told to me in my experience. I do not know whether there is anything beyond this.

[GV3:P2179] One Manvantharam is the concept of Saptharshis. By that concept a solar system creation is formed. The solar system has nine planets having form and useful for the theory of evolution. Therefore the greatness of Rahu and Kethu has not been determined. These planets spread as twelve zodiac signs and give twelve results. In that it is not certain which all results Rahu and Kethu give. But destined things are told for everything. An incompleteness is seen for both these.

[GV3:P2180] Saptharshis gave concept for a solar system creation in the calculation of nine planets, twelve zodiac signs and twenty seven stars. Scientifically and by some other experiences, there are some people who say it as seven mandalas. These seven mandalas have form and expression. But Rahu and Kethu who revolve like this sometimes travel out of place at some time. Therefore we should not desire its complete result. Because nobody can determine how the time difference will be when it comes revolving in the action of one Vyazhavattakalam (twelve years). That is, their movement is not constant. Without being able to determine that, it travels in an order.

[GV3:P2181] The movement of the solar system has a measure of Brahma prakasam. Its influence may act changing here and there. But it will not leave this mandalam. If there is a difference of one second for this movement, there will be a mistake of about a yamam calculation for it. It cannot be determined accurately. But in our measurement there are seconds, minutes, nazhikas, vinazhikas and up to the very minute thrudi.

[GV3:P2182] Whatever calculations there may be, there should not be any mistake in the greatness which has come in the concept of Saptharshis. That is the way of truth. That is the life of man.

[GV3:P2183] There is sandhi (junction) for Manvantharam, Chathuryugas, Yugas and Yamas. Sandhyavandanam is being done as a sandhi which removes the yamasandhi. There may be people who are awake in three yamas and also people who are awake in five yamas if added. But we have been told the time of worship in eight yamasandhis.

[GV3:P2184] There is a calculation of five Gurus for one yuga. These Gurus bring about the calculation of Sandhyavandanam according to Brahma nischayam and the realization of times. But we cannot say how many great men come in yugasandhi. The Brahma nischayam is described to us in four yugas. If a mistake comes in yugadharma at any time, that mistake cannot be corrected later. For that a lot of effort is required. The life and words of great men prove it.

[GV3:P2185] In the knowledge of Chathurvakya told by Sathyathranan, scholars and ordinary people fell into a pit without being able to realize the times. This truth can be seen if we look at the last twenty five Chathuryugas. Our subject is what we got to know where the mistake occurred in this truth.

[GV3:P2186] As proof that this mistake occurred, we have thousands of lives crossing hurdles before us. The life of man, pithru, devan, rishi and sannyasi. The path shown by the rishi standing above the devan after seeing what is the life of a muni, what is the life of a rishi, coming down like that what is the life of a devan, what is the life of a rishi under a devan in these lives is the truth standing through rishi words. There was a time when nothing could be known about this truth. A karma skill which got told from where this truth exists above the Trimurthi system was completed at Varkala. That occasion was a time of physical and mental tortures. It is not possible to count or finish telling what all types of tortures. Taking it back is indebted to that Brahma nischayam who made that Brahma nischayam a person. The life of great men is what has come without being able to decide what is the right and wrong of Manu tradition, and how it will be fulfilled.

[GV3:P2187] Six Manvantharas are over. The calculation is 71 Chathuryugas in each Manvantharam. Four yugas for one Chathuryuga. The calculation is five Gurus for the fulfillment of each yuga. Kaliyuga is the smallest of yugas. This itself has 4,32,000 years. Double of this is Dwaparam. Dwaparam and Kali together form Threthayuga. Threthayuga and Kali together form Kruthayuga. From the birth of Sree Rama to the birth of Sree Krishna is the period which has come after many lakhs of years in the state of five Gurus for each yuga. It was Sree Krishna who was born as a great man who completely knew the Kali dharma before the beginning of Kali. Manu has assessed that the fourth padam, Kali, belongs to the Sudra class. That is, Kali is the time when the right to Brahma jnanam was completely destined to the Sudra after completely understanding all the subjects to be ruled and made to rule in the fourth padam. It is as an example for this that Krishna has been made to be born in Yadava kula. The prime mistake occurred twenty five Chathuryugas ago. It was indicated earlier that the word Aham Brahmasmi originated from a big mistake which occurred when the Rishi Sathyathranan, thinking to respect truth, took and saw the decisive truth which came as Chathuryugas and Manvantharas as what he was and what his predecessors were. Later when it was completed with Chathurvakyas, it was not perfection of idea, but perfection of letter that was brought about. That is the mistake which continues for twenty five Chathuryugas. Gurukula Brahmaniveditham is an action which corrects this mistake and brings about perfection of idea. This ceremony was previously conducted in a different way.

[GV3:P2188] Sudra class is ignorant. Therefore it was easy to deceive them by words. Each one in the Sudra class is the children who met with that mistake. There was a practice to start alphabet at the fifth year. This ceremony was conducted equally for male and female. This had an advisory nature. This will be done before completing five years. There is no such ceremony among the Brahmin community. There is no ceremony among them except starting alphabet. The ceremony which was kept for this among the Sudra class is Kettukalyanam. Like that there were ceremonies like Thirandukalyanam, Kalyanam, Pulikudi adiyanthiram. When a girl is born and these many ceremonies are conducted, that house will become poor. Then if four girls are born, it need not be told.

[GV3:P2189] A ceremony shown among the Brahmins is the Guru giving advice and starting alphabet. In the marriage ceremony they show an action destined by the birth of Vyasa. Vyasa has the name Krishna Dwaipayanan by concept action. Vyasa told us as if it came from the mouth of Krishna. It is not certain in which all yugas Vyasa has come. It is widely accepted that Vyasa is the son of a fisherwoman. The purana histories which divided and told the four Vedas and six Sastras are the truth destined and written for Hindus.

[GV3:P2190] The ceremony shown by Brahmins is nine and eleven days. This ceremony is making the male and female children sit and reading out briefly the things in Puranas and Ithihasas and explaining them physically and spiritually. Today we have known that whichever country it passes through, war takes place in all those countries. In this state we are watching play without knowing the story. That came before us in many ways as drama, chavittunatakam, musical drama and today stands reached in cinema. This is our history.

[GV3:P2238] Another example can be told for this. Muhammad Ghazni attacked and came to India and harmed Somnath temple many times. Because, who does not know that all inventions are building up fatal danger zones belonging to this category! This death junction is the asset prepared for the coming generation by everyone together. [GV3:P2241] Why have Harijans been suffering since the time of Sree Krishna? The eighty-four castes in the Sudra class are the result of the intellectual strategy adopted by the Hindu leaders to serve their own purposes. Because of the hold of caste, they neither mingle with each other nor let others mingle. It gradually brought them down to the level of 'Na-Ayyar' (Not Ayyar). The Nair community did not understand this strategy. The Sudra class, who fight among themselves without knowing anything, is what constitutes the Hindu religion, or the Aryan religion. The title 'Na-Ayyar' was given to them to make them fight like this.

[GV3:P2242] The dharma called Sanathana has not been practiced here. Sanathana has been confined to the framework of Manusmriti. But they have also outlined a danger in it. [GV3:P2243] The rule of Devas belongs to Treta Yuga and Dwapara Yuga. Only when Kali Yuga begins can this mistake be rectified. Only then will the dharma called Sanathana be completed. This one dharma is not practiced anywhere outside India. Understand at least one thing: after Rama, is there anyone else who can speak of a karma-gathi (path of action) in that manner? They have struggled hard to make this Rama a Vaishnava and achieve Loka-sangraham (welfare of the world). That is what is happening even today. It was out of ignorance, without knowing this karma-gathi, that they made Mahatmaji say it. That remains a dispute even today. Everyone knows who killed Mahatmaji. Even today, the karma of Godse is happening here. That karma is eager to create the Hindu religion all over India. As far as Hindus are concerned, they do not know this. Hindu people believe that this belongs to Sanathana. Look at another trick done to make them believe so: the day they started learning 'Aham Brahmasmi' (I am Brahman), from that day the misfortune of Sanathana began. Thus, through the four Vedas and Chaturvarnya, they acted without knowing the times. When they acted in that manner, that misfortune came, leading to a dharma where they did not know what the Yuga Dharmas were. When they reached that karma, Kali took over the karma-gathi of destruction and acted.

[GV3:P2244] Kali is not destruction. The four varnas always exist; they are there in this Kali too. They exist in other Yugas as well. Brahmin, Kshatriya, Vaishya, and Sudra must understand about character refinement. No one doubts Satya Yuga. But there is no one to act in it. The reason is that they acted in the other three periods without knowing the proper way. Therefore, Sanskrit is a language refined in such a way that no one can get out from under the Deva. Giving an interpretation to that language, they refine it and call it the language of the Devas. Even today, the eminent scholars who believe this to be the language of the Devas do not know that this is the danger of the mistake that came in the Manu lineage. No one knows the secret that the twenty-five Chatur Yugas have acted in this manner. It is in that situation that the four-faced nature went away, and the three-faced nature came - Brahma, Vishnu, Maheshwara. People say there is no fourth head. The story concocted for that goes like this: when asked the meaning of Omkara, he did not know. So, the head was pinched off. I do not know to which blind person this matter was asked. Therefore, to avert Kali, they added the exploits of Bhadrakali in the Karma Yoga called Bhadra and started praising it. Thus, they divided and established Shaktheyam and Shaivam.

[GV3:P2245] The people of South India were praised as monkeys. Hanuman, the devotee of Rama, is an example of that. It was the gimmick of meddling with Rama-rajya, caught in the ignorance of not being able to think whether Rama-rajya should happen in this Kali, that became the cause for Mahatmaji's assassination. And in that field, they brought about the karma-dosha of calling out Om Kali, Maha Kali, Karim Kali, and Bhadra Kali, added a story of Mahisha, brought it in the place of Rama-rajya, and even implemented it. That is how Om Kali, Maha Kali, Bhadra Kali Om came about. Thus, by adding Om in all possible ways and pronouncing it, they reduced the greatness of the syllable Om. Shaktheyan, Shaivan, and the four types of people in South India followed that path. If all the people in these four states celebrate in this way, one cannot even imagine what the situation of the people in North India would be. As a result of Vaishnava, Shaiva, and Shaktheya becoming bad in that way, we say here like this:

[GV3:P2246] For the sheep and the cow, two horns;

[GV3:P2247] For the Ayyangar Swami, three horns.

For that, they started saying everything by adding the word 'Rama'. That is the refined language. Sanyasis and scholars have lived and died without knowing this story. No one has ever understood that all this is the system of the Hindu religion. That is the karma-gathi brought from Sabdaroopavali up to Amarakosam. It is written starting as 'Amara Nirjara Deva'. When this was imposed, it became impossible to know the calculation of time. In what was considered time, Satya Yuga was changed to be true. Treta Yuga and Dwapara Yuga were changed to the time of Devas. It was also stated that Kali is destruction. They have reached a state where the Sudra class does not know that Kali is not destruction, and they are building a fence in the Hindu system. This is the flaw of India's Dharma theory and Karma theory. At the same time, it has been said in some places in the Puranas that 'Kali is the best among times'. A word has been given just to let it be known that there is such a truth. Not for the sake of knowing. Because such a dharma is not known, Kali is the time to know what India has been experiencing and accumulating for twenty-five Chatur Yugas. I wish to inform you this much. This is the habit, thickness, and destruction of the Hindu religion.

[GV3:P2249] The misfortune of the Sudra class that unknowingly celebrated the word 'Nair'! The destitute Sudra of today is the one who thinks he will perish if that is not added. Till today, even the Harijan has not understood that what has been coming for twenty-five Chatur Yugas is a danger. It is good for the government and the Hindu religion, which has lost the headquarters of the Manu lineage, to think about cultivating the karma-bodha (awareness of action) on what can be done about this. Otherwise, the danger that befell the Harijans is that they cannot think for which period the tantric rituals were written, creating ignorant deities that make one ask, 'Is this Bhadrakali?' about one's own daughter, making them known as scoundrels and beggars who act as great sinners forever and die. This ignorant Sudra is the inheritance of the caste determination tied to the head of the headless Sudra to show that he does not belong to it. Therefore, I am telling you one thing: Kali is the same for Brahmin, Kshatriya, Vaishya, and Sudra. That is the karma told to you in one word in the Bhagavatam: 'Among times, Kali is the best'.

[GV3:P2250] If we cannot break the cage of mistakes we fell into without knowing what Kali Dharma is, clear our karma dosha, gain the knowledge to think what this word 'Nair' is for, and reach the Sanathana system, Kali Dharma will end without our knowledge. The paths belonging to the purity of action shown to us today as God for this purpose are Griha Suddhi (purity of home), Pithru Suddhi (purity of ancestors), and Deva Suddhi (purity of gods). Completing these three paths, the fourth is entrusted to the Guru. This is the truth of Sanathana.

[GV3:P2251] Kali is the time when this is undertaken and performed in the place of Brahmavit, Brahmavaryan, Brahmavarishtan, and Brahmavareeshtan. To put it simply, Guru Dharma is correcting the mistakes of those three periods from Satya Yuga onwards. When this started to be kept as Hindu, the result of tossing it around changed Sree Krishna into the husband of sixteen thousand and eight women. What is the sastra dharma (scientific duty) for this? Or can the vidhi-vihita kala dharma (time-duty prescribed by destiny) be fitted here? The writers of today have magnified and shown it, and the cinema, which they say is one of the wonders that is a dangerous turning point, has come running. For this single reason, if people of any other country hear these things, they will reject the truth saying, 'I don't wish to walk backward, I wish to go forward'. Many such people come to India and again become worshippers of the wondrous idol. One of the mistakes in it is the karma dosha of not knowing what Kali is. The special rule of this Kali Dharma is not 'Harijans' or 'people of Hari', but the keeping called Kali-kala that shapes the theories of time. Let us rejoice that this is the time we got to be born. At least some of us might have thought, 'I belong to this downtrodden community'. Never think like that. Even Narayana Guru was pained and spoke about it. It is the achievement of the artistic trickery that has misguided it till today. The proof for this is that on the same matter, after Sree Krishna, two thousand four hundred and forty-four great souls came as gurus and prophets and suffered pain. All this is a trap of danger that we cannot fully understand. Can't we at least now change the ignorance of giving the word 'Harijan' to the great Sree Krishna and keeping the poor as Harijans? Beware of the ways in which the lack of dharma to tell the truth has misled. It is said that when Rama acted, he happened to see the karma standing above, and saying there is no point in living anymore, he went down into the river. It is written that Ramatirtha attained Jala-samadhi. Till today, it is not known where Sree Sankara's Samadhi is. About Vadalur Ramalinga, it is said like this:

[GV3:P2252] Did that supreme devotee go with his body without dying?

[GV3:P2253] Did the paramartha bhakta of that Parameshwara

[GV3:P2254] A wonder overflows here. We can see many such instances where ignorance happened even among Sanyasis. Then how will this knowledge reach ordinary people? The dharma system of Kali is not to show wonders or to speak of what is not there. The karma with the time calculation of Kali is to speak of what is there. Did this ignorance of Yuga Dharma come from one of us, or from ordinary people?

[GV3:P2255] Won't all this catch up with those who use the words Rama and Ravana? Were we not only able to deviate and travel on the path reached by Buddha, Jain, and Moses? Look at the great Jesus and Nabi themselves. The God of the Jews is waiting, saying He will come as the son of an emperor. Therefore, they could not accept Jesus or give peace to Jesus.
